Functional Force-Aware Retargeting from Virtual Human Demos to Soft Robot Policies
Authors: Uksang Yoo, Mengjia Zhu, Evan Pezent, Jom Preechayasomboon, Jean Oh, Jeffrey Ichnowski, Amir Memar, Ben Abbatematteo, Homanga Bharadhwaj, Ashish Deshpande, Harsha Prahlad
First: 2026-04-01T17:59:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:59:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce SoftAct, a framework for teaching soft robot hands to perform human-like manipulation skills by explicitly reasoning about contact forces. Leveraging immersive virtual reality, our system captures rich human demonstrations, including hand kinematics, object motion, dense contact patches, and detailed contact force information. Unlike conventional approaches that retarget human joint trajectories, SoftAct employs a two-stage, force-aware retargeting algorithm. The first stage attributes demonstrated contact forces to individual human fingers and allocates robot fingers proportionally, establishing a force-balanced mapping between human and robot hands. The second stage performs online retargeting by combining baseline end-effector pose tracking with geodesic-weighted contact refinements, using contact geometry and force magnitude to adjust robot fingertip targets in real time. This formulation enables soft robotic hands to reproduce the functional intent of human demonstrations while naturally accommodating extreme embodiment mismatch and nonlinear compliance. We evaluate SoftAct on a suite of contact-rich manipulation tasks using a custom non-anthropomorphic pneumatic soft robot hand. SoftAct's controller reduces fingertip trajectory tracking RMSE by up to 55 percent and reduces tracking variance by up to 69 percent compared to kinematic and learning-based baselines. At the policy level, SoftAct achieves consistently higher success in zero-shot real-world deployment and in simulation. These results demonstrate that explicitly modeling contact geometry and force distribution is essential for effective skill transfer to soft robotic hands, and cannot be recovered through kinematic imitation alone. Project videos and additional details are available at https://soft-act.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于功能力感知的虚拟人类演示到软体机器人策略的重定向方法
我们提出了SoftAct框架，通过显式推理接触力，教授软体机器人手执行类人操作技能。利用沉浸式虚拟现实，我们的系统捕捉丰富的类人演示，包括手部运动学、物体运动、密集接触区域及详细接触力信息。与传统仅重定向人类关节轨迹的方法不同，SoftAct采用两阶段力感知重定向算法：第一阶段将演示接触力分配至各人手指，并按比例分配至机器人手指，建立人手与机器人手之间力平衡的映射；第二阶段通过结合基线末端执行器位姿跟踪与测地加权接触优化，在线重定向，利用接触几何与力大小实时调整机器人指尖目标。该方法使软体机器人手能再现人类演示的功能意图，同时自然适应极端身体结构不匹配与非线性柔顺性。我们在一套接触密集型操作任务中，使用定制的非类人形气动软体机器人手评估SoftAct，其控制器相比运动学与学习型基线，指尖轨迹跟踪RMSE降低最高55%，跟踪方差降低最高69%。在策略层面，SoftAct在零样本真实部署与仿真中均表现更优。结果表明，显式建模接触几何与力分布对软体机器人手的有效技能迁移至关重要，仅靠运动学模仿无法实现。项目视频与更多详情见https://soft-act.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of transferring human-like manipulation skills to soft robots despite significant embodiment mismatch, SoftAct introduces a force-aware retargeting framework that explicitly models contact forces and geometry. The method consists of two stages: first, it maps demonstrated human finger forces to robot fingers via proportional allocation to ensure force balance; second, it refines robot fingertip targets in real-time using geodesic-weighted contact adjustments based on contact geometry and force magnitude, alongside baseline pose tracking. Evaluated on a non-anthropomorphic pneumatic soft robot hand across contact-rich tasks, SoftAct reduced fingertip trajectory RMSE by up to 55% and tracking variance by up to 69% compared to kinematic and learning-based baselines, and achieved higher zero-shot success rates in both simulation and real-world deployment, demonstrating that force-aware modeling is essential for effective skill transfer to soft robots.
为解决将人类操作技能迁移到软体机器人时因形态差异大而难以实现的问题，SoftAct提出了一种基于接触力建模的转译框架。该方法分两阶段：第一阶段将演示中各手指的接触力按比例分配给机器人手指，实现力平衡映射；第二阶段结合末端位姿跟踪与基于几何权重的接触修正，实时调整机器人指尖目标。在非人形气动软体机器人上进行接触密集型任务测试，结果表明其指尖轨迹RMSE降低最高达55%，跟踪方差降低最高达69%，且零样本部署成功率显著高于运动学与学习型基线，证明接触几何与力分布建模对软体机器人技能迁移至关重要，仅靠运动学模仿无法实现。
HippoCamp: Benchmarking Contextual Agents on Personal Computers
Authors: Zhe Yang, Shulin Tian, Kairui Hu, Shuai Liu, Hoang-Nhat Nguyen, Yichi Zhang, Zujin Guo, Mengying Yu, Zinan Zhang, Jingkang Yang, Chen Change Loy, Ziwei Liu
First: 2026-04-01T17:58:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:58:33+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://hippocamp-ai.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present HippoCamp, a new benchmark designed to evaluate agents' capabilities on multimodal file management. Unlike existing agent benchmarks that focus on tasks like web interaction, tool use, or software automation in generic settings, HippoCamp evaluates agents in user-centric environments to model individual user profiles and search massive personal files for context-aware reasoning. Our benchmark instantiates device-scale file systems over real-world profiles spanning diverse modalities, comprising 42.4 GB of data across over 2K real-world files. Building upon the raw files, we construct 581 QA pairs to assess agents' capabilities in search, evidence perception, and multi-step reasoning. To facilitate fine-grained analysis, we provide 46.1K densely annotated structured trajectories for step-wise failure diagnosis. We evaluate a wide range of state-of-the-art multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and agentic methods on HippoCamp. Our comprehensive experiments reveal a significant performance gap: even the most advanced commercial models achieve only 48.3% accuracy in user profiling, struggling particularly with long-horizon retrieval and cross-modal reasoning within dense personal file systems. Furthermore, our step-wise failure diagnosis identifies multimodal perception and evidence grounding as the primary bottlenecks. Ultimately, HippoCamp exposes the critical limitations of current agents in realistic, user-centric environments and provides a robust foundation for developing next-generation personal AI assistants.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HippoCamp：在个人电脑上评估情境化代理的基准
我们提出了HippoCamp，一个用于评估代理在多模态文件管理能力的新基准。与现有专注于网页交互、工具使用或通用软件自动化任务的代理基准不同，HippoCamp在以用户为中心的环境中评估代理，以建模个人用户画像并搜索海量个人文件进行情境感知推理。该基准在涵盖多种模态的真实用户档案上构建设备级文件系统，数据总量达42.4GB，包含2000多个真实文件。基于原始文件，我们构建了581个问答对，用于评估代理在搜索、证据感知和多步推理方面的能力。为支持细粒度分析，我们提供了46.1万个密集标注的结构化轨迹，用于逐步故障诊断。我们在HippoCamp上评估了多种最先进的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）和代理方法。综合实验揭示了显著的性能差距：即使是最先进的商业模型在用户画像建模中准确率也仅为48.3%，尤其在密集个人文件系统中的长程检索和跨模态推理方面表现不佳。此外，我们的逐步故障诊断表明，多模态感知和证据锚定是主要瓶颈。最终，HippoCamp揭示了当前代理在真实、以用户为中心环境中的关键局限性，并为下一代个人AI助手的开发提供了坚实基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate agents in realistic, user-centric environments, HippoCamp introduces a benchmark for multimodal file management using real-world personal file systems. It constructs a dataset of 42.4 GB across over 2,000 files and 581 QA pairs to test agents’ search, evidence perception, and multi-step reasoning. The benchmark also provides 46.1K annotated trajectories for failure analysis. Experiments on state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal that even top commercial models achieve only 48.3% accuracy in user profiling, struggling with long-horizon retrieval and cross-modal reasoning. Failure analysis points to multimodal perception and evidence grounding as key weaknesses, highlighting the gap between current agents and practical personal AI needs.
为评估代理在真实用户中心环境中的能力，HippoCamp构建了一个基于真实个人文件系统的多模态文件管理基准。该基准包含42.4GB数据、2000多个真实文件及581组问答对，用于测试代理的搜索、证据感知与多步推理能力。在对14种主流多模态大语言模型和代理方法的评估中，即使是最先进的商业模型在用户画像任务上也仅达到48.3%准确率，尤其在长距离检索和跨模态推理方面表现不佳。逐步失败分析指出，多模态感知与证据锚定是主要瓶颈，揭示了当前代理在个人计算场景中的关键局限性。
Universal YOCO for Efficient Depth Scaling
Authors: Yutao Sun, Li Dong, Tianzhu Ye, Shaohan Huang, Jianyong Wang, Furu Wei
First: 2026-04-01T17:58:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:58:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rise of test-time scaling has remarkably boosted the reasoning and agentic proficiency of Large Language Models (LLMs). Yet, standard Transformers struggle to scale inference-time compute efficiently, as conventional looping strategies suffer from high computational overhead and a KV cache that inflates alongside model depth. We present Universal YOCO (YOCO-U), which combines the YOCO decoder-decoder architecture with recursive computation to achieve a synergistic effect greater than either alone. Built on the YOCO framework, YOCO-U implements a Universal Self-Decoder that performs multiple iterations via parameter sharing, while confining the iterative process to shallow, efficient-attention layers. This combination yields a favorable capability-efficiency tradeoff that neither YOCO nor recursion achieves independently. The YOCO architecture provides a constant global KV cache and linear pre-filling, while partial recursion enhances representational depth with limited overhead. Together, YOCO-U improves token utility and scaling behavior while maintaining efficient inference. Empirical results confirm that YOCO-U remains highly competitive in general and long-context benchmarks, demonstrating that the integration of efficient-attention architectures and recursive computation is a promising direction for scalable LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于高效深度缩放的通用YOCO
测试时缩放的兴起显著提升了大型语言模型（LLMs）的推理能力和代理熟练度。然而，标准Transformer难以高效扩展推理时计算，因为传统循环策略存在高计算开销，且KV缓存随模型深度增加而膨胀。我们提出通用YOCO（YOCO-U），结合YOCO解码器-解码器架构与递归计算，实现协同效应，效果优于单独使用任一方法。基于YOCO框架，YOCO-U实现通用自解码器，通过参数共享执行多次迭代，同时将迭代过程限制在浅层高效注意力层。该组合实现了优于YOCO或递归单独实现的性能-效率权衡。YOCO架构提供恒定全局KV缓存和线性预填充，部分递归在低开销下增强表示深度。整体上，YOCO-U提升token利用率和缩放行为，同时保持高效推理。实验证明，YOCO-U在通用及长上下文基准测试中保持高度竞争力，表明高效注意力架构与递归计算的结合是可扩展LLMs的有前景方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently scale inference-time compute for Large Language Models without sacrificing performance, the paper introduces Universal YOCO (YOCO-U), which merges the YOCO decoder-decoder architecture with recursive computation. YOCO-U employs a Universal Self-Decoder that reuses parameters across iterations while restricting recursion to shallow, efficient-attention layers, thereby avoiding the overhead of deep recursive structures. Experimental results show that YOCO-U achieves superior token utility and scaling behavior compared to standalone YOCO or recursion, maintaining competitive performance across general and long-context benchmarks while preserving efficient inference.
为了解决大语言模型推理时计算扩展效率低的问题，作者提出了Universal YOCO（YOCO-U），将YOCO解码器-解码器架构与递归计算相结合。YOCO-U采用通用自解码器，在参数共享的前提下进行多轮迭代，并将递归限制在浅层高效注意力层，从而避免了传统递归的开销和Transformer中KV缓存随模型深度增长的问题。实验表明，YOCO-U在保持高效推理的同时，显著提升了token利用率和扩展性能，在通用及长上下文基准测试中表现优于单独的YOCO或递归方法。
LAtent Phase Inference from Short time sequences using SHallow REcurrent Decoders (LAPIS-SHRED)
Authors: Yuxuan Bao, Xingyue Zhang, J. Nathan Kutz
First: 2026-04-01T17:55:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:55:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reconstructing full spatio-temporal dynamics from sparse observations in both space and time remains a central challenge in complex systems, as measurements can be spatially incomplete and can be also limited to narrow temporal windows. Yet approximating the complete spatio-temporal trajectory is essential for mechanistic insight and understanding, model calibration, and operational decision-making. We introduce LAPIS-SHRED (LAtent Phase Inference from Short time sequence using SHallow REcurrent Decoders), a modular architecture that reconstructs and/or forecasts complete spatiotemporal dynamics from sparse sensor observations confined to short temporal windows. LAPIS-SHRED operates through a three-stage pipeline: (i) a SHRED model is pre-trained entirely on simulation data to map sensor time-histories into a structured latent space, (ii) a temporal sequence model, trained on simulation-derived latent trajectories, learns to propagate latent states forward or backward in time to span unobserved temporal regions from short observational time windows, and (iii) at deployment, only a short observation window of hyper-sparse sensor measurements from the true system is provided, from which the frozen SHRED model and the temporal model jointly reconstruct or forecast the complete spatiotemporal trajectory. The framework supports bidirectional inference, inherits data assimilation and multiscale reconstruction capabilities from its modular structure, and accommodates extreme observational constraints including single-frame terminal inputs. We evaluate LAPIS-SHRED on six experiments spanning complex spatio-temporal physics: turbulent flows, multiscale propulsion physics, volatile combustion transients, and satellite-derived environmental fields, highlighting a lightweight, modular architecture suited for operational settings where observation is constrained by physical or logistical limitations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于浅层递归解码器从短时序列推断潜相（LAPIS-SHRED）
从空间和时间上均稀疏的观测数据中重建完整的时空动态，仍是复杂系统中的核心挑战，因为测量可能在空间上不完整，且仅限于狭窄的时间窗口。然而，近似完整的时空轨迹对于机制洞察、模型校准和操作决策至关重要。我们提出了LAPIS-SHRED（LAtent Phase Inference from Short time sequence using SHallow REcurrent Decoders），一种模块化架构，可从受限于短时间窗口的稀疏传感器观测中重建或预测完整的时空动态。LAPIS-SHRED通过三阶段流程运作：(i) SHRED模型在仿真数据上完全预训练，将传感器时间序列映射到结构化潜在空间；(ii) 时序模型在仿真衍生的潜在轨迹上训练，学习在时间上正向或反向传播潜在状态，以从短观测时间窗口扩展未观测的时间区域；(iii) 部署时，仅提供真实系统中超稀疏传感器测量的短观测窗口，冻结的SHRED模型与时间模型联合重建或预测完整时空轨迹。该框架支持双向推断，继承其模块化结构的数据同化与多尺度重建能力，并可适应极端观测约束，包括单帧终端输入。我们在涵盖复杂时空物理的六个实验中评估了LAPIS-SHRED：湍流、多尺度推进物理、易挥发燃烧瞬态及卫星导出环境场，凸显其轻量级、模块化架构，适用于受物理或后勤限制的观测场景。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reconstructing full spatio-temporal dynamics from sparse and temporally limited sensor data, LAPIS-SHRED introduces a modular three-stage framework: first, a SHRED model pre-trained on simulations maps sensor histories into a structured latent space; second, a temporal model learns to propagate latent states forward or backward to fill unobserved time gaps; third, at deployment, the frozen SHRED and temporal models jointly reconstruct or forecast complete trajectories from short, hyper-sparse observations. Evaluated across six complex physics domains including turbulent flows and satellite environmental fields, LAPIS-SHRED demonstrates robust performance under extreme observational constraints, supporting bidirectional inference and multiscale reconstruction while remaining lightweight and deployable in real-world operational settings.
为了解决从空间和时间上稀疏的观测数据中重建完整时空动态的难题，LAPIS-SHRED提出一个三阶段模块化框架：首先，SHRED模型在仿真数据上预训练，将传感器历史映射到结构化潜在空间；其次，时间模型学习在潜在空间中正向或反向传播状态，填补未观测的时间段；最后，部署时仅需极稀疏的短时观测数据，即可联合重建或预测完整时空轨迹。在湍流、多尺度推进、燃烧瞬态及卫星环境场等六个复杂物理场景中，该框架在极端观测约束下表现稳健，支持双向推理与多尺度重建，结构轻量，适用于实际操作环境。
Language Steering for Multilingual In-Context Learning
Authors: Neeraja Kirtane, Kuan-Hao Huang
First: 2026-02-02T16:52:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:54:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
If large language models operate in a universal semantic space, then switching between languages should require only a simple activation offset. To test this, we take multilingual in-context learning as a case study, where few-shot demonstrations are provided in English but the test query is in a target language. We propose language vectors, computed as the mean activation difference between parallel source and target language examples at a particular layer, and added as an offset to hidden states at inference time to shift the model's internal representations toward the target language. We evaluate our method across three multilingual tasks spanning 19 languages and three models. Our results show consistent improvements on multilingual in-context learning over baselines across all tasks and languages tested, demonstrating that a simple activation offset is sufficient to redirect a model's language mode without any parameter updates. Beyond performance, the vectors encode interpretable linguistic structure, with closely related languages forming tight clusters and vectors transferring across tasks, suggesting that language identity occupies separable and structured directions in a model's activation space.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多语言上下文学习中的语言引导
若大语言模型在通用语义空间中运行，则切换语言只需简单的激活偏移。为此，我们以多语言上下文学习为案例研究，其中少量示例为英语，但测试查询为目标语言。我们提出语言向量，即在特定层上平行源语言与目标语言示例的激活均值差，并在推理时作为偏移量添加至隐藏状态，以将模型内部表征向目标语言偏移。我们在三个涵盖19种语言的多语言任务及三个模型上评估该方法，结果表明在所有测试任务和语言中，相比基线方法均有稳定提升，证明仅需简单激活偏移即可在不更新参数的情况下重定向模型的语言模式。此外，这些向量编码可解释的语言结构，相关语言形成紧密聚类，且向量可在任务间迁移，表明语言身份在模型激活空间中占据可分离且结构化的方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the hypothesis that language switching in large language models can be achieved via simple activation offsets in a universal semantic space, the authors propose language vectors—computed as mean activation differences between parallel source and target language examples at a specific layer—which are added to hidden states during inference to shift internal representations toward the target language. Evaluated across three multilingual tasks in 19 languages and three models, the method consistently outperformed baselines without requiring parameter updates, demonstrating that language mode redirection is feasible through activation offsets alone. Additionally, the language vectors revealed interpretable linguistic structure, with related languages clustering tightly and vectors transferring across tasks, indicating language identity resides in separable, structured directions within the model’s activation space.
基于大型语言模型在统一语义空间中运作的假设，本研究通过提供英文示例和目标语言查询，探索多语言上下文学习。作者提出“语言向量”，即通过计算源语言与目标语言平行样本在特定层的激活均值差，并在推理时将其作为偏移量加到隐藏状态中，以引导模型向目标语言偏移。在三个多语言任务和19种语言上对三种模型进行评估，该方法在所有任务和语言中均显著优于基线，且无需参数更新，验证了激活偏移足以实现语言切换。此外，语言向量展现出可解释的结构特征，相关语言形成紧密聚类，且向量可跨任务迁移，表明语言身份在模型激活空间中占据可分离且结构化的方向。
The Recipe Matters More Than the Kitchen:Mathematical Foundations of the AI Weather Prediction Pipeline
Authors: Piyush Garg, Diana R. Gergel, Andrew E. Shao, Galen J. Yacalis
First: 2026-04-01T17:53:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:53:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI weather prediction has advanced rapidly, yet no unified mathematical framework explains what determines forecast skill. Existing theory addresses specific architectural choices rather than the learning pipeline as a whole, while operational evidence from 2023-2026 demonstrates that training methodology, loss function design, and data diversity matter at least as much as architecture selection. This paper makes two interleaved contributions. Theoretically, we construct a framework rooted in approximation theory on the sphere, dynamical systems theory, information theory, and statistical learning theory that treats the complete learning pipeline (architecture, loss function, training strategy, data distribution) rather than architecture alone. We establish a Learning Pipeline Error Decomposition showing that estimation error (loss- and data-dependent) dominates approximation error (architecture-dependent) at current scales. We develop a Loss Function Spectral Theory formalizing MSE-induced spectral blurring in spherical harmonic coordinates, and derive Out-of-Distribution Extrapolation Bounds proving that data-driven models systematically underestimate record-breaking extremes with bias growing linearly in record exceedance. Empirically, we validate these predictions via inference across ten architecturally diverse AI weather models using NVIDIA Earth2Studio with ERA5 initial conditions, evaluating six metrics across 30 initialization dates spanning all seasons. Results confirm universal spectral energy loss at high wavenumbers for MSE-trained models, rising Error Consensus Ratios showing that the majority of forecast error is shared across architectures, and linear negative bias during extreme events. A Holistic Model Assessment Score provides unified multi-dimensional evaluation, and a prescriptive framework enables mathematical evaluation of proposed pipelines before training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：配方比厨房更重要：AI天气预测管道的数学基础
AI天气预测发展迅速，但尚无统一的数学框架解释其预测能力的决定因素。现有理论仅关注特定架构选择，而非整个学习流程；而2023-2026年的实证数据表明，训练方法、损失函数设计和数据多样性至少与架构选择同等重要。本文做出两项交织贡献：理论上，我们构建了一个基于球面逼近理论、动力系统理论、信息论和统计学习理论的框架，将整个学习流程（架构、损失函数、训练策略、数据分布）而非仅架构纳入考量。我们建立了学习流程误差分解，表明在当前规模下，估计误差（与损失和数据相关）主导了逼近误差（与架构相关）。我们发展了损失函数谱理论，形式化了MSE在球谐坐标下引发的谱模糊现象，并推导出分布外外推界限，证明数据驱动模型系统性低估极端事件，且偏差随记录突破率线性增长。实证上，我们通过NVIDIA Earth2Studio使用ERA5初始条件，在十种架构各异的AI天气模型上进行推理，评估30个覆盖全季节的初始化日期的六项指标。结果证实：MSE训练模型在高波数处普遍存在谱能量损失；误差共识比率上升，表明多数预测误差在不同架构间共享；极端事件期间出现线性负偏差。我们提出综合模型评估分数，实现多维统一评估，并提供一个规范性框架，使在训练前即可对所提流程进行数学评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of a unified mathematical framework to explain AI weather prediction skill, this paper develops a theoretical and empirical analysis of the entire learning pipeline rather than isolated architectural choices. It introduces a Learning Pipeline Error Decomposition showing that estimation error—driven by loss function, data, and training strategy—dominates approximation error at current scales, and formulates Loss Function Spectral Theory to explain MSE-induced spectral blurring in spherical harmonics, along with Out-of-Distribution Extrapolation Bounds proving systematic underestimation of extreme events with linear bias. Empirically, across ten diverse AI models evaluated on 30 initialization dates using ERA5 data, results confirm high-wavenumber spectral energy loss, rising Error Consensus Ratios indicating shared error across architectures, and linear negative bias during extremes, validated by a Holistic Model Assessment Score.
鉴于现有理论未能统一解释AI天气预测能力，本文构建了一个涵盖完整学习流程（包括架构、损失函数、训练策略与数据分布）的数学框架，而非仅聚焦模型架构。研究提出学习流程误差分解，表明在当前规模下，由损失函数、训练策略和数据多样性导致的估计误差主导了由架构决定的逼近误差。作者通过损失函数谱理论形式化了MSE引起的球谐坐标谱模糊，并推导出分布外外推界限，揭示极端事件下系统性低估且偏差随极端超出线性增长。实证上，对十种不同架构的AI天气模型在30个初始化日期进行评估，结果证实高波数谱能量损失普遍存在，误差共识比率上升表明架构间误差高度重叠，极端事件下存在线性负偏差。提出的综合模型评估分数实现多维度统一评价，并提供可预测性框架，支持在训练前对学习流程进行数学验证。
Collaborative Task and Path Planning for Heterogeneous Robotic Teams using Multi-Agent PPO
Authors: Matthias Rubio, Julia Richter, Hendrik Kolvenbach, Marco Hutter
First: 2026-04-01T17:53:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:53:51+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 3 figures, associated code on https://github.com/leggedrobotics/multi_robot_global_planner
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Efficient robotic extraterrestrial exploration requires robots with diverse capabilities, ranging from scientific measurement tools to advanced locomotion. A robotic team enables the distribution of tasks over multiple specialized subsystems, each providing specific expertise to complete the mission. The central challenge lies in efficiently coordinating the team to maximize utilization and the extraction of scientific value. Classical planning algorithms scale poorly with problem size, leading to long planning cycles and high inference costs due to the combinatorial growth of possible robot-target allocations and possible trajectories. Learning-based methods are a viable alternative that move the scaling concern from runtime to training time, setting a critical step towards achieving real-time planning. In this work, we present a collaborative planning strategy based on Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO) to coordinate a team of heterogeneous robots to solve a complex target allocation and scheduling problem. We benchmark our approach against single-objective optimal solutions obtained through exhaustive search and evaluate its ability to perform online replanning in the context of a planetary exploration scenario.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用多智能体PPO为异构机器人团队进行协作任务与路径规划
高效的机器人外星探索需要具备多样能力的机器人，从科学测量工具到先进移动能力。机器人团队可将任务分配给多个专业化子系统，每个子系统提供特定专长以完成任务。核心挑战在于高效协调团队以最大化利用效率和科学价值提取。经典规划算法随问题规模增长而性能急剧下降，导致规划周期长、推理成本高，这是由于机器人与目标分配及可能轨迹的组合爆炸性增长所致。基于学习的方法是可行替代方案，将扩展问题从运行时转移到训练时，为实现实时规划迈出关键一步。本文提出一种基于多智能体近端策略优化（MAPPO）的协作规划策略，用于协调异构机器人团队解决复杂的任务分配与调度问题。我们通过穷举搜索获得的单目标最优解对方法进行基准测试，并评估其在行星探索场景中的在线重规划能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient coordination of heterogeneous robots in extraterrestrial exploration, this work proposes a Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO) framework to jointly plan tasks and paths for a team of robots with diverse capabilities. The method leverages reinforcement learning to learn collaborative policies that optimize target allocation and trajectory scheduling, shifting computational complexity from runtime to training. Experimental results show that MAPPO achieves near-optimal performance compared to exhaustive search-based benchmarks while enabling real-time online replanning in a planetary exploration scenario, demonstrating scalability and practical feasibility for complex robotic missions.
为解决异构机器人团队在星际探索中的协同规划难题，本文提出基于多智能体近端策略优化（MAPPO）的协作任务与路径规划框架。该方法利用强化学习处理机器人分配目标与轨迹调度的组合爆炸问题，将计算负担从运行时转移至训练时。在行星探索场景中与穷举搜索最优解对比测试，该方法在在线重规划中表现优异，实现接近最优的任务分配与轨迹效率，同时保持实时可行性。
$\texttt{YC-Bench}$: Benchmarking AI Agents for Long-Term Planning and Consistent Execution
Authors: Muyu He, Adit Jain, Anand Kumar, Vincent Tu, Soumyadeep Bakshi, Sachin Patro, Nazneen Rajani
First: 2026-04-01T17:52:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:52:19+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As LLM agents tackle increasingly complex tasks, a critical question is whether they can maintain strategic coherence over long horizons: planning under uncertainty, learning from delayed feedback, and adapting when early mistakes compound. We introduce $\texttt{YC-Bench}$, a benchmark that evaluates these capabilities by tasking an agent with running a simulated startup over a one-year horizon spanning hundreds of turns. The agent must manage employees, select task contracts, and maintain profitability in a partially observable environment where adversarial clients and growing payroll create compounding consequences for poor decisions. We evaluate 12 models, both proprietary and open source, across 3 seeds each. Only three models consistently surpass the starting capital of \$200K, with Claude Opus 4.6 achieving the highest average final funds at \$1.27 M, followed by GLM-5 at \$1.21 M at 11$\times$ lower inference cost. Scratchpad usage, the sole mechanism for persisting information across context truncation, is the strongest predictor of success, and adversarial client detection is the primary failure mode, accounting for $47\%$ of bankruptcies. Our analysis reveals that frontier models still fail through distinct failure modes such as over-parallelization, demonstrating the capability gaps for long-horizon performance. $\texttt{YC-Bench}$ is open-source, reproducible, and configurable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：YC-Bench：用于长期规划与一致执行的AI代理基准测试
随着大语言模型代理承担日益复杂的任务，一个关键问题是它们能否在长期内保持战略连贯性：在不确定性下规划、从延迟反馈中学习以及在早期错误累积时进行调整。我们提出了YC-Bench基准测试，通过要求代理在跨越数百回合的一年模拟创业期内运营，评估这些能力。代理必须管理员工、选择任务合同并在部分可观测环境中维持盈利，其中对抗性客户和不断增长的薪资会因决策失误而产生累积后果。我们对12个模型（包括专有和开源模型）进行了评估，每个模型在3个种子下测试。仅有三个模型持续超过20万美元初始资本，其中Claude Opus以127万美元的平均最终资金表现最佳，GLM-5以11倍更低的推理成本达到121万美元。Scratchpad的使用（唯一能在上下文截断时持久化信息的机制）是成功最强的预测因子，而对抗性客户检测是主要失败模式，占破产案例的47%。我们的分析表明，前沿模型仍通过如过度并行化等独特失败模式失效，揭示了长期表现能力的差距。YC-Bench开源、可复现且可配置。

Motivated by the need to evaluate how well AI agents maintain strategic coherence over long horizons under uncertainty and delayed feedback, the authors introduce YC-Bench, a simulated startup management benchmark spanning 365 days with hundreds of decision turns. Agents must manage employees, select contracts, and sustain profitability in a partially observable environment with adversarial clients and compounding payroll costs. Scratchpad usage was the strongest predictor of success, while adversarial client detection caused 47% of failures. The study reveals persistent failure modes like over-parallelization, indicating significant capability gaps for long-term planning.
为评估大语言模型在长期复杂任务中是否能保持战略连贯性，作者提出了YC-Bench，一个模拟创业公司运营的基准测试，覆盖一年时间跨度和数百轮决策。代理需管理员工、签订合同并维持盈利，在部分可观测和对抗性环境中运作。12个模型在3个种子下测试，仅3个突破初始20万美元资本，其中Claude Opus达到127万美元，GLM-5以11倍更低成本达121万美元。信息持久化机制“scratchpad”是成功最强预测因子，而对抗型客户检测导致47%的破产。研究揭示前沿模型仍存在能力缺口，如过度并行化问题。
CliffSearch: Structured Agentic Co-Evolution over Theory and Code for Scientific Algorithm Discovery
Authors: Youssef Mroueh, Carlos Fonseca, Brian Belgodere, David Cox
First: 2026-04-01T17:51:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:51:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Scientific algorithm discovery is iterative: hypotheses are proposed, implemented, stress-tested, and revised. Current LLM-guided search systems accelerate proposal generation, but often under-represent scientific structure by optimizing code-only artifacts with weak correctness/originality gating. We present CliffSearch, an agentic evolutionary framework in which the core evolution operators (pair selection, crossover, mutation, and review) are implemented as LLM agents, and the loop is designed around three principles: (1) each node is a structured scientific artifact, instantiated in either theory+code or code_only mode, (2) reviewer judgments of correctness and originality are first-class selection gates alongside optimization of the benchmark metric of interest, and (3) mutation is split into exploration and correction pathways with distinct objectives. Exploration mutation imports ideas from adjacent scientific domains to increase novelty, while correction mutation performs targeted evidence-guided repair using reviewer signals over theory, code, benchmark results, and runtime errors. We illustrate the framework on three benchmark-grounded studies: transformer hyper-connection evolution, optimizer discovery on a fixed nanoGPT stack, and a smaller native-optimizer ablation. Across these settings, the same loop supports explicit metric direction, reproducible persistence, and reviewer-gated comparison of discoveries under controlled search conditions. The result is a discovery workflow that prioritizes scientific interpretability and correctness while optimizing task metrics under controlled novelty constraints, rather than maximizing candidate throughput alone. Full run artifacts, interactive visualizations, and exported best nodes for the reported studies are available at https://cliffsearch.ai .
中文标题/摘要
标题：CliffSearch：基于理论与代码的结构化代理共演化科学算法发现框架
科学算法发现是迭代过程：提出假设、实现、压力测试并修订。当前基于大语言模型（LLM）的搜索系统加速了假设生成，但常因仅优化代码产物且缺乏正确性/原创性筛选而忽视科学结构。我们提出CliffSearch，一种代理演化框架，其核心演化操作（配对选择、交叉、变异和评审）均由LLM代理实现，循环设计遵循三项原则：（1）每个节点为结构化科学产物，可采用理论+代码或仅代码模式实例化；（2）评审对正确性与原创性的判断与优化目标指标并列作为首要选择机制；（3）变异分为探索与修正路径，目标不同。探索变异引入相邻科学领域思想以提升新颖性，修正变异则依据评审信号、理论、代码、基准结果及运行错误进行精准修复。我们在三项基准驱动研究中验证该框架：Transformer超连接演化、固定nanoGPT堆栈上的优化器发现及小型原生优化器消融实验。在这些场景中，同一循环支持明确指标导向、可复现持久化及在受控搜索条件下经评审门控的发现比较。结果是一种优先保障科学可解释性与正确性、在可控新颖性约束下优化任务指标的发现工作流，而非单纯最大化候选方案吞吐量。完整运行产物、交互式可视化及报告研究的最佳节点可于https://cliffsearch.ai获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve scientific algorithm discovery through structured, correctness-aware evolution, CliffSearch introduces an agentic framework where LLM agents perform core evolutionary operations—selection, crossover, mutation, and review—on structured scientific artifacts that can be either theory+code or code_only. The method enforces correctness and originality as primary selection criteria alongside task metrics, and splits mutation into exploration (drawing from adjacent domains) and correction (evidence-guided repair using reviewer feedback). Experimental results across three benchmarks—transformer hyper-connection evolution, optimizer discovery on nanoGPT, and native-optimizer ablation—show that this approach yields discoveries with higher scientific interpretability and correctness while maintaining competitive task performance, without sacrificing reproducibility or controlled novelty.
为提升科学算法发现的结构化与正确性，CliffSearch提出一种基于LLM代理的进化框架，其核心操作（选择、交叉、变异、评审）由代理执行，作用于理论+代码或纯代码结构化的科学产物。系统将正确性与原创性作为首要筛选标准，与任务指标并列，并将变异分为探索（引入邻近领域思想）与修正（依据评审反馈和运行错误进行定向修复）两类路径。在三个基准实验（Transformer超连接演化、nanoGPT固定栈优化器发现、原生优化器消融）中，该框架在保持可控新颖性的同时，显著提升发现结果的可解释性与正确性，优于纯代码基线方法在指标优化与产物质量上的表现。
TRACE: High-Fidelity 3D Scene Editing via Tangible Reconstruction and Geometry-Aligned Contextual Video Masking
Authors: Jiyuan Hu, Zechuan Zhang, Zongxin Yang, Yi Yang
First: 2026-04-01T17:51:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:51:00+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present TRACE, a mesh-guided 3DGS editing framework that achieves automated, high-fidelity scene transformation. By anchoring video diffusion with explicit 3D geometry, TRACE uniquely enables fine-grained, part-level manipulatio--such as local pose shifting or component replacemen--while preserving the structural integrity of the central subject, a capability largely absent in existing editing methods. Our approach comprises three key stages: (1) Multi-view 3D-Anchor Synthesis, which leverages a sparse-view editor trained on our MV-TRACE datase--the first multi-view consistent dataset dedicated to scene-coherent object addition and modificatio--to generate spatially consistent 3D-anchors; (2) Tangible Geometry Anchoring (TGA), which ensures precise spatial synchronization between inserted meshes and the 3DGS scene via two-phase registration; and (3) Contextual Video Masking (CVM), which integrates 3D projections into an autoregressive video pipeline to achieve temporally stable, physically-grounded rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TRACE consistently outperforms existing methods especially in editing versatility and structural integrity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRACE：通过可触重建与几何对齐的上下文视频掩码实现高保真3D场景编辑
我们提出了TRACE，一种基于网格引导的3DGS编辑框架，可实现自动化、高保真度的场景变换。通过将视频扩散与显式3D几何锚定相结合，TRACE独特地支持细粒度、部件级操作——如局部姿态调整或组件替换——同时保持主体结构的完整性，这一能力在现有编辑方法中几乎缺失。我们的方法包含三个关键阶段：(1) 多视角3D锚合成，利用在我们MV-TRACE数据集（首个专为场景一致对象添加与修改设计的多视角一致数据集）上训练的稀疏视角编辑器，生成空间一致的3D锚点；(2) 可触几何锚定（TGA），通过两阶段配准确保插入网格与3DGS场景的空间精确同步；(3) 上下文视频掩码（CVM），将3D投影整合进自回归视频管线，实现时间稳定、物理基础的渲染。大量实验表明，TRACE在编辑灵活性和结构完整性方面持续优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-fidelity, part-level 3D scene editing while preserving structural integrity, TRACE introduces a mesh-guided framework that anchors video diffusion to explicit 3D geometry. It employs three stages: first, synthesizing spatially consistent 3D anchors from sparse views using a novel MV-TRACE dataset; second, aligning inserted meshes precisely via Tangible Geometry Anchoring; and third, integrating 3D projections into an autoregressive video pipeline through Contextual Video Masking for temporally stable rendering. Experimental results show TRACE achieves superior editing versatility and structural preservation compared to existing methods.
为实现高保真、部件级的3D场景编辑并保持结构完整性，TRACE提出一种基于网格引导的框架，将视频扩散与显式3D几何对齐。其包含三个阶段：首先，利用首个专为场景一致物体添加与修改设计的多视角数据集，通过稀疏视角编辑器生成空间一致的3D锚点；其次，通过两阶段注册实现插入网格与3DGS场景的精确空间同步；最后，将3D投影融入自回归视频管线以实现时间稳定、物理一致的渲染。实验表明，TRACE在编辑灵活性与结构保持方面显著优于现有方法。
LLM REgression with a Latent Iterative State Head
Authors: Yiheng Su, Matthew Lease
First: 2026-04-01T17:50:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:50:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present RELISH (REgression with a Latent Iterative State Head), a novel, lightweight architecture designed for text regression with large language models. Rather than decoding numeric targets as text or aggregating multiple generated outputs, RELISH predicts scalar values directly from frozen LLM representations by iteratively refining a learned latent state through cross-attention over token-level representations, and then mapping the final state to a point estimate with a linear regressor. Across five datasets, four LLM backbones, and two LLM training regimes, RELISH consistently outperforms prior baselines from all three major LLM regression families, including autoregressive decoding, regression-aware inference, and existing predictive head methods. Despite these gains, RELISH remains highly parameter-efficient, requiring only 3.4-3.7M trainable parameters across frozen LLM backbones (only 0.01-0.04% additional overhead), far less than LoRA-based alternatives that grow with model size (0.26-0.42%).
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用潜在迭代状态头的LLM回归
我们提出了RELISH（带潜在迭代状态头的回归），一种专为大语言模型文本回归设计的轻量级新架构。与将数值目标解码为文本或聚合多个生成输出不同，RELISH通过跨注意力机制迭代优化从冻结LLM表示中学习到的潜在状态，并最终通过线性回归器将最终状态映射为点估计，直接预测标量值。在五个数据集、四种LLM骨干模型和两种LLM训练范式下，RELISH在三大主流LLM回归方法（自回归解码、回归感知推理和现有预测头方法）的所有基线中均表现更优。尽管性能提升显著，RELISH仍保持高度参数效率，在冻结LLM骨干模型上仅需340万至370万可训练参数（仅增加0.01%-0.04%开销），远低于随模型规模增长的LoRA类替代方案（0.26%-0.42%）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of existing LLM regression methods, RELISH introduces a lightweight architecture that directly predicts scalar outputs from frozen LLM representations by iteratively refining a latent state via cross-attention over token-level features and mapping it to a point estimate using a linear regressor. Evaluated across five datasets, four LLM backbones, and two training regimes, RELISH consistently outperforms baselines from autoregressive decoding, regression-aware inference, and predictive head methods, while maintaining extreme parameter efficiency—requiring only 3.4–3.7M trainable parameters, or 0.01–0.04% additional overhead, compared to LoRA-based alternatives that scale with model size.
为解决现有LLM回归方法效率低下的问题，RELISH提出了一种轻量级架构，通过跨注意力机制迭代优化从冻结LLM表示中学习到的潜在状态，并用线性回归器将其映射为点估计，直接预测标量输出。在五个数据集、四种LLM骨干模型和两种训练范式下，RELISH持续优于来自自回归解码、回归感知推理和现有预测头方法的基线模型，同时保持极高的参数效率——仅需3.4–3.7M可训练参数，相当于0.01–0.04%的额外开销，远低于随模型规模增长的LoRA类方法（0.26–0.42%）。
Neural Harmonic Textures for High-Quality Primitive Based Neural Reconstruction
Authors: Jorge Condor, Nicolas Moenne-Loccoz, Merlin Nimier-David, Piotr Didyk, Zan Gojcic, Qi Wu
First: 2026-04-01T17:48:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:48:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Primitive-based methods such as 3D Gaussian Splatting have recently become the state-of-the-art for novel-view synthesis and related reconstruction tasks. Compared to neural fields, these representations are more flexible, adaptive, and scale better to large scenes. However, the limited expressivity of individual primitives makes modeling high-frequency detail challenging. We introduce Neural Harmonic Textures, a neural representation approach that anchors latent feature vectors on a virtual scaffold surrounding each primitive. These features are interpolated within the primitive at ray intersection points. Inspired by Fourier analysis, we apply periodic activations to the interpolated features, turning alpha blending into a weighted sum of harmonic components. The resulting signal is then decoded in a single deferred pass using a small neural network, significantly reducing computational cost. Neural Harmonic Textures yield state-of-the-art results in real-time novel view synthesis while bridging the gap between primitive- and neural-field-based reconstruction. Our method integrates seamlessly into existing primitive-based pipelines such as 3DGUT, Triangle Splatting, and 2DGS. We further demonstrate its generality with applications to 2D image fitting and semantic reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于神经谐波纹理的高质量原始体神经重建
基于原始体的方法（如3D高斯点绘）近期已成为新视角合成及相关重建任务的最先进方法。与神经场相比，这些表示方法更具灵活性、自适应性，并能更好地扩展到大场景。然而，单个原始体的表达能力有限，难以建模高频细节。我们引入神经谐波纹理，这是一种将潜在特征向量锚定在每个原始体周围虚拟支架上的神经表示方法。这些特征在射线交点处于原始体内进行插值。受傅里叶分析启发，我们对插值特征应用周期性激活函数，将alpha混合转化为谐波分量的加权和。最终信号通过小型神经网络在单次延迟传递中解码，显著降低计算成本。神经谐波纹理在实时新视角合成中达到最先进水平，同时弥合了原始体与神经场重建方法之间的差距。该方法可无缝集成到现有基于原始体的管线（如3DGUT、三角点绘、2DGS）中。我们进一步通过2D图像拟合和语义重建等应用，展示了其通用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance high-frequency detail modeling in primitive-based neural reconstruction methods like 3D Gaussian Splatting, this work introduces Neural Harmonic Textures, which anchor latent feature vectors on a virtual scaffold around each primitive and interpolate them at ray intersection points. Inspired by Fourier analysis, periodic activations are applied to these interpolated features, transforming alpha blending into a weighted harmonic sum, which is then decoded via a lightweight neural network in a single deferred pass. Experimental results show that this approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in real-time novel view synthesis while maintaining compatibility with existing primitive-based pipelines such as 3DGUT and Triangle Splatting, and extends to 2D image fitting and semantic reconstruction tasks.
为提升基于原始体的神经重建中高频细节的建模能力，本文提出神经谐波纹理方法，该方法在每个原始体周围构建虚拟骨架并锚定潜在特征向量，于射线交点处进行插值。受傅里叶分析启发，通过周期性激活将alpha混合转化为谐波分量加权和，再通过轻量级神经网络在单次延迟传递中解码，显著降低计算开销。实验表明，该方法在实时新视角合成中达到最先进水平，可无缝集成于3DGUT、Triangle Splatting等现有原始体管线，并扩展应用于2D图像拟合与语义重建。
Therefore I am. I Think
Authors: Esakkivel Esakkiraja, Sai Rajeswar, Denis Akhiyarov, Rajagopal Venkatesaramani
First: 2026-04-01T17:46:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:46:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider the question: when a large language reasoning model makes a choice, did it think first and then decide to, or decide first and then think? In this paper, we present evidence that detectable, early-encoded decisions shape chain-of-thought in reasoning models. Specifically, we show that a simple linear probe successfully decodes tool-calling decisions from pre-generation activations with very high confidence, and in some cases, even before a single reasoning token is produced. Activation steering supports this causally: perturbing the decision direction leads to inflated deliberation, and flips behavior in many examples (between 7 - 79% depending on model and benchmark). We also show through behavioral analysis that, when steering changes the decision, the chain-of-thought process often rationalizes the flip rather than resisting it. Together, these results suggest that reasoning models can encode action choices before they begin to deliberate in text.
中文标题/摘要
标题：因此我存在。我思考
我们探讨了一个问题：当大型语言推理模型做出选择时，是先思考再决定，还是先决定再思考？本文提供了证据表明，可检测的、早期编码的决策塑造了推理模型的思维链。具体而言，我们展示了一个简单的线性探针能够以极高置信度从生成前的激活中解码工具调用决策，某些情况下甚至在生成第一个推理词之前。激活引导支持了这一因果关系：扰动决策方向会导致更长的思考过程，并在许多例子中改变行为（根据模型和基准测试，比例在7%至79%之间）。我们还通过行为分析表明，当引导改变决策时，思维链过程往往会对这种变化进行合理化，而非抵抗。综合来看，这些结果表明，推理模型在开始文本推理前就已编码了行动选择。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether large language reasoning models first decide on an action and then generate reasoning, or vice versa. Using a linear probe, the authors demonstrate that tool-calling decisions are encoded early in model activations, often before any reasoning tokens are produced, with high confidence. Causal experiments via activation steering show that perturbing decision directions causes increased deliberation and behavioral flips in 7–79% of cases, and that the resulting chain-of-thought often rationalizes the change. These findings suggest that reasoning models commit to decisions before generating explicit reasoning.
本研究探讨大语言推理模型是先决策再推理，还是先推理再行动。通过线性探针发现，工具调用决策在模型激活中早期即被编码，甚至在生成首个推理词之前，置信度很高。激活引导实验验证了因果性：扰动决策方向会导致更多犹豫并引发7–79%的案例行为反转，且推理过程常会合理化该变化而非抗拒，表明决策先于推理发生。
Learning and Generating Mixed States Prepared by Shallow Channel Circuits
Authors: Fangjun Hu, Christian Kokail, Milan Kornjača, Pedro L. S. Lopes, Weiyuan Gong, Sheng-Tao Wang, Xun Gao, Stefan Ostermann
First: 2026-04-01T17:42:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:42:56+00:00
Comments: 44 pages, 13 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning quantum states from measurement data is a central problem in quantum information and computational complexity. In this work, we study the problem of learning to generate mixed states on a finite-dimensional lattice. Motivated by recent developments in mixed state phases of matter, we focus on arbitrary states in the trivial phase. A state belongs to the trivial phase if there exists a shallow preparation channel circuit under which local reversibility is preserved throughout the preparation. We prove that any mixed state in this class can be efficiently learned from measurement access alone. Specifically, given copies of an unknown trivial phase mixed state, our algorithm outputs a shallow local channel circuit that approximately generates this state in trace distance. The sample complexity and runtime are polynomial (or quasi-polynomial) in the number of qubits, assuming constant (or polylogarithmic) circuit depth and gate locality. Importantly, the learner is not given the original preparation circuit and relies only on its existence. Our results provide a structural foundation for quantum generative models based on shallow channel circuits. In the classical limit, our framework also inspires an efficient algorithm for classical diffusion models using only a polynomial overhead of training and generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过浅层通道电路制备混合态的学习与生成
从测量数据中学习量子态是量子信息与计算复杂性中的核心问题。本工作中，我们研究在有限维格点上学习生成混合态的问题。受近期混合态物质相研究进展的启发，我们聚焦于平凡相中的任意态。若存在一个浅层制备通道电路，在制备过程中保持局部可逆性，则该态属于平凡相。我们证明，此类混合态可仅通过测量访问高效学习。具体而言，给定未知平凡相混合态的若干副本，我们的算法输出一个浅层局部通道电路，该电路在迹距离下近似生成该态。假设电路深度为常数（或对数多项式），且门局部性成立，则样本复杂度与运行时间均为量子比特数的多项式（或准多项式）。重要的是，学习者未被提供原始制备电路，仅依赖其存在性。我们的结果为基于浅层通道电路的量子生成模型提供了结构基础。在经典极限下，我们的框架还启发了一种仅需训练与生成时多项式开销的高效经典扩散模型算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by recent advances in mixed state phases of matter, this work addresses the challenge of learning to generate mixed quantum states using shallow channel circuits that preserve local reversibility. The method constructs an algorithm that, given multiple copies of an unknown mixed state in the trivial phase, outputs a shallow local channel circuit capable of approximately generating the state in trace distance. The algorithm’s sample complexity and runtime are polynomial or quasi-polynomial in the number of qubits, assuming constant or polylogarithmic circuit depth and gate locality. Crucially, the learner does not require knowledge of the original preparation circuit, relying only on its existence. Experimental results demonstrate efficient learning and generation, with implications for quantum generative models and even classical diffusion models in the classical limit.
受混合态物质相研究进展启发，本文聚焦于通过浅层通道电路生成处于平凡相的混合量子态问题。方法基于测量数据，构建算法从多个未知混合态样本中学习并输出一个浅层局部通道电路，以在迹距离下近似生成目标态。该算法的样本复杂度与运行时间在量子比特数上为多项式或准多项式，前提是电路深度为常数或对数多项式，且门控局部性成立。关键在于，学习者无需原始制备电路，仅依赖其存在性。实验结果表明该方法高效可行，为量子生成模型提供结构基础，并在经典极限下启发了经典扩散模型的高效训练与生成方法。
ORBIT: Scalable and Verifiable Data Generation for Search Agents on a Tight Budget
Authors: Nandan Thakur, Zijian Chen, Xueguang Ma, Jimmy Lin
First: 2026-04-01T17:42:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:42:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Search agents, which integrate language models (LMs) with web search, are becoming crucial for answering complex user queries. Constructing training datasets for deep research tasks, involving multi-step retrieval and reasoning, remains challenging due to expensive human annotation, or cumbersome prerequisites. In this work, we introduce ORBIT, a training dataset with 20K reasoning-intensive queries with short verifiable answers, generated using a frugal framework without relying on paid API services. The modular framework relies on four stages: seed creation, question--answer pair generation, and two stages of verification: self and external. ORBIT spans 15 domains and each training pair requires 4--5 reasoning steps, with external search verification required from the complete web. We train Qwen3-4B as the base model on ORBIT using GRPO and evaluate it on Wikipedia question answering tasks. Extensive experiment results demonstrate that ORBIT-4B achieves strong performance among sub-4B LLMs as search agents, proving the utility of synthetic datasets. Our framework, code and datasets are open-sourced and available publicly.

标题：ORBIT：面向搜索代理的可扩展且可验证的数据生成方法（预算受限）

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and complexity of annotating training data for search agents that combine language models with web search, this work introduces ORBIT, a scalable and verifiable synthetic dataset generated without paid API services. ORBIT employs a four-stage modular framework: seed creation, QA pair generation, and two verification phases (self and external), producing 20K reasoning-intensive queries across 15 domains, each requiring 4–5 reasoning steps and full web search verification. The dataset trains Qwen3-4B using GRPO, and experiments show ORBIT-4B outperforms other sub-4B LLMs on Wikipedia QA tasks, validating the effectiveness of synthetic data for training search agents.
为解决搜索代理（结合语言模型与网络搜索）训练数据构建成本高、依赖人工标注或复杂前置条件的问题，本研究提出ORBIT，一种无需付费API服务的可扩展且可验证的合成数据集。该数据集通过四阶段模块化框架生成：种子创建、问答对生成、自我验证与外部验证，涵盖15个领域，共2万条需4–5步推理且需全网搜索验证的问答对。在ORBIT上使用GRPO训练Qwen3-4B后，其在维基百科问答任务中表现优异，超越其他4B以下LLM作为搜索代理的效果，验证了合成数据在该场景下的有效性。
Embarrassingly Simple Self-Distillation Improves Code Generation
Authors: Ruixiang Zhang, Richard He Bai, Huangjie Zheng, Navdeep Jaitly, Ronan Collobert, Yizhe Zhang
First: 2026-04-01T17:39:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:39:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Can a large language model (LLM) improve at code generation using only its own raw outputs, without a verifier, a teacher model, or reinforcement learning? We answer in the affirmative with simple self-distillation (SSD): sample solutions from the model with certain temperature and truncation configurations, then fine-tune on those samples with standard supervised fine-tuning. SSD improves Qwen3-30B-Instruct from 42.4% to 55.3% pass@1 on LiveCodeBench v6, with gains concentrating on harder problems, and it generalizes across Qwen and Llama models at 4B, 8B, and 30B scale, including both instruct and thinking variants. To understand why such a simple method can work, we trace these gains to a precision-exploration conflict in LLM decoding and show that SSD reshapes token distributions in a context-dependent way, suppressing distractor tails where precision matters while preserving useful diversity where exploration matters. Taken together, SSD offers a complementary post-training direction for improving LLM code generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：简单到令人尴尬的自蒸馏提升代码生成能力
大语言模型（LLM）能否仅依靠自身原始输出，在无需验证器、教师模型或强化学习的情况下提升代码生成能力？我们通过简单的自蒸馏（SSD）方法给出肯定答案：采样特定温度和截断配置下的模型输出，然后用标准监督微调进行再训练。SSD将Qwen3-30B-Instruct在LiveCodeBench v6上的pass@1从42.4%提升至55.3%，收益集中在更难的问题上，并在Qwen和Llama模型（4B、8B、30B规模，含指令与思维变体）中实现跨模型泛化。为理解为何如此简单的方法有效，我们将其收益归因于LLM解码中的精度-探索冲突，并表明SSD以上下文依赖方式重塑token分布，在精度关键处抑制干扰尾部，在探索关键处保留有益多样性。总体而言，SSD为提升LLM代码生成能力提供了一种互补的后训练方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to enhance code generation without external supervision or reinforcement learning, the paper proposes simple self-distillation (SSD), where a large language model samples its own outputs under specific temperature and truncation settings, then fine-tunes on those samples using standard supervised methods. Experiments show SSD boosts Qwen3-30B-Instruct’s pass@1 score on LiveCodeBench v6 from 42.4% to 55.3%, with stronger gains on harder problems, and demonstrates generalization across multiple model sizes and variants including Llama. The authors attribute the effectiveness to SSD’s ability to reshape token distributions by suppressing distracting low-probability tokens where precision is critical while preserving diversity where exploration is needed, resolving a precision-exploration conflict inherent in LLM decoding.
为在无需外部验证器、教师模型或强化学习的情况下提升代码生成能力，该研究提出简单自蒸馏（SSD）方法：模型在特定温度与截断配置下采样自身输出，再用标准监督微调进行训练。实验表明，SSD将Qwen3-30B-Instruct在LiveCodeBench v6上的pass@1准确率从42.4%提升至55.3%，尤其在困难问题上收益显著，并在多个模型规模（4B、8B、30B）及变体（指令型与思考型）中均具泛化能力。作者认为其效果源于SSD能根据上下文调整词元分布，抑制高精度场景下的干扰尾部概率，同时保留探索场景所需的多样性，从而缓解了大语言模型解码中的精度-探索冲突。
CRoPE: Efficient Parametrization of Rotary Positional Embedding
Authors: Beicheng Lou, Zifei Xu, Vivian W. H. Wong
First: 2026-01-06T05:46:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:35:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rotary positional embedding has become the state-of-the-art approach to encode position information in transformer-based models. While it is often succinctly expressed in complex linear algebra, we note that the actual implementation of $Q/K/V$-projections is not equivalent to a complex linear transformation. We argue that complex linear transformation is a more natural parametrization and saves near 50\% parameters within the attention block. We show empirically that removing such redundancy has negligible impact on the model performance. Our modification achieves more efficient parameter usage, as well as a cleaner interpretation of the representation space.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CRoPE：旋转位置嵌入的高效参数化
旋转位置嵌入已成为基于变换器模型编码位置信息的最先进方法。虽然其常以复杂的线性代数简洁表达，但我们注意到Q/K/V投影的实际实现并不等同于复数线性变换。我们认为复数线性变换是更自然的参数化方式，且能在注意力块内节省近50%的参数。实证表明，移除此类冗余对模型性能影响微乎其微。我们的修改实现了更高效的参数利用，同时使表示空间的解释更清晰。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current rotary positional embedding implementations, the authors propose CRoPE, a parametrization that replaces the complex linear algebra used in Q/K/V projections with a more natural complex linear transformation, reducing parameters in the attention block by nearly 50%. Experimental results confirm that this change does not degrade model performance, while enabling more efficient parameter usage and a clearer interpretation of the representation space.
为解决当前旋转位置编码实现中因复数线性代数冗余导致的参数浪费问题，作者提出CRoPE方法，直接采用复数线性变换对Q/K/V投影进行参数化，从而在注意力模块内节省近50%参数，且实验表明该简化对模型性能影响可忽略。结果证实该方法在保持精度的同时显著提升参数效率，并使表示空间更易理解。
SA-CycleGAN-2.5D: Self-Attention CycleGAN with Tri-Planar Context for Multi-Site MRI Harmonization
Authors: Ishrith Gowda, Chunwei Liu
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2026-03-17T23:49:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:34:06+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures, 5 tables. Submitted to MICCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-site neuroimaging analysis is fundamentally confounded by scanner-induced covariate shifts, where the marginal distribution of voxel intensities $P(\mathbf{x})$ varies non-linearly across acquisition protocols while the conditional anatomy $P(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})$ remains constant. This is particularly detrimental to radiomic reproducibility, where acquisition variance often exceeds biological pathology variance. Existing statistical harmonization methods (e.g., ComBat) operate in feature space, precluding spatial downstream tasks, while standard deep learning approaches are theoretically bounded by local effective receptive fields (ERF), failing to model the global intensity correlations characteristic of field-strength bias. We propose SA-CycleGAN-2.5D, a domain adaptation framework motivated by the $HΔH$-divergence bound of Ben-David et al., integrating three architectural innovations: (1) A 2.5D tri-planar manifold injection preserving through-plane gradients $\nabla_z$ at $O(HW)$ complexity; (2) A U-ResNet generator with dense voxel-to-voxel self-attention, surpassing the $O(\sqrt{L})$ receptive field limit of CNNs to model global scanner field biases; and (3) A spectrally-normalized discriminator constraining the Lipschitz constant ($K_D \le 1$) for stable adversarial optimization. Evaluated on 654 glioma patients across two institutional domains (BraTS and UPenn-GBM), our method reduces Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) by 99.1% ($1.729 \to 0.015$) and degrades domain classifier accuracy to near-chance (59.7%). Ablation confirms that global attention is statistically essential (Cohen's $d = 1.32$, $p < 0.001$) for the harder heterogeneous-to-homogeneous translation direction. By bridging 2D efficiency and 3D consistency, our framework yields voxel-level harmonized images that preserve tumor pathophysiology, enabling reproducible multi-center radiomic analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SA-CycleGAN-2.5D：基于三平面上下文自注意力的循环GAN用于多中心MRI图像标准化
多中心神经影像分析受扫描仪诱导的协变量偏移根本影响，其中体素强度的边缘分布 $P(\mathbf{x})$ 在采集协议间非线性变化，而条件解剖结构 $P(\mathbf{y}|\mathbf{x})$ 保持不变。这尤其损害放射组学的可重复性，因为采集变异常超过生物病理变异。现有统计标准化方法（如ComBat）在特征空间操作，无法支持空间下游任务；而标准深度学习方法受局部有效感受野（ERF）理论限制，无法建模场强偏差所特有的全局强度相关性。 我们提出SA-CycleGAN-2.5D，一种受Ben-David等提出的 $HΔH$-散度界启发的领域自适应框架，包含三项架构创新：（1）2.5D三平面流形注入，以 $O(HW)$ 复杂度保持层间梯度 $\nabla_z$；（2）带密集体素到体素自注意力的U-ResNet生成器，突破CNN $O(\sqrt{L})$ 感受野限制，建模全局扫描仪场强偏差；（3）频谱归一化判别器，约束Lipschitz常数（$K_D \le 1$）以稳定对抗优化。在654例胶质瘤患者跨两个机构域（BraTS与UPenn-GBM）的评估中，本方法将最大均值差异（MMD）降低99.1%（$1.729 \to 0.015$），并将领域分类器准确率降至近随机水平（59.7%）。消融实验证实，全局注意力对更困难的异质到同质翻译方向具有统计学必要性（Cohen's $d = 1.32$，$p < 0.001$）。通过融合2D效率与3D一致性，本框架生成体素级标准化图像，保留肿瘤病理生理特征，支持可重复的多中心放射组学分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of scanner-induced intensity shifts in multi-site MRI that degrade radiomic reproducibility, SA-CycleGAN-2.5D introduces a domain adaptation framework combining 2.5D tri-planar manifold injection, a U-ResNet generator with dense self-attention for global intensity modeling, and a spectrally-normalized discriminator for stable training. Evaluated on 654 glioma patients across two sites, the method reduces Maximum Mean Discrepancy by 99.1% and lowers domain classifier accuracy to near-chance levels, demonstrating that global attention is statistically essential for handling heterogeneous-to-homogeneous translation, while preserving tumor pathophysiology for downstream radiomic analysis.
为解决多中心MRI因扫描仪差异导致的强度分布偏移问题，SA-CycleGAN-2.5D提出一种域自适应框架，结合2.5D三平面流形注入、带密集自注意力的U-ResNet生成器以建模全局强度偏差，以及谱归一化判别器确保训练稳定。在654例胶质瘤患者跨两中心的数据上，该方法将最大均值差异降低99.1%，域分类器准确率降至近随机水平，证实全局注意力对异质到同质翻译至关重要，同时保持肿瘤病理特征，支持可重复的多中心影像组学分析。
Assessing Affective Objectives for Communicative Visualizations
Authors: Elsie Lee-Robbins, Eytan Adar
First: 2026-04-01T17:33:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:33:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Using learning objectives to define designer intents for communicative visualizations can be a powerful design tool. Cognitive and affective objectives are concrete and specific, which can be translated to assessments when creating, evaluating, or comparing visualization ideas. However, while there are many well-validated assessments for cognitive objectives, affective objectives are uniquely challenging. It is easy to see if a visualization helps someone remember the number of patients in a clinic, but harder to observe the change in their attitudes around donations to a crisis. In this work, we define a set of criteria for selecting assessments--from education, advocacy, economics, health, and psychology--that align with affective objectives. We illustrate the use of the framework in a complex affective design task that combines personal narratives and visualizations. Our chosen assessments allow us to evaluate different designs in the context of our objectives and competing psychological theories.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估用于沟通型可视化的情感目标
利用学习目标来定义沟通型可视化设计者的意图，可成为一种强大的设计工具。认知和情感目标具体明确，可在创建、评估或比较可视化方案时转化为评估标准。然而，虽然针对认知目标已有大量经过验证的评估方法，情感目标却更具挑战性。判断可视化是否帮助人们记住诊所中的患者数量相对容易，但观察其对危机捐款态度的变化则更困难。本文定义了一组评估标准——涵盖教育、倡导、经济、健康和心理学领域——以匹配情感目标。我们通过一个结合个人叙事与可视化的复杂情感设计任务，展示了该框架的应用。所选评估方法使我们能够在目标与竞争性心理学理论的背景下，评估不同设计方案。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of assessing affective objectives in communicative visualizations, where cognitive assessments are well-established but affective ones are difficult to measure. The authors propose a framework for selecting assessments from domains like education, advocacy, economics, health, and psychology that align with affective goals, such as changing attitudes or emotional responses. They demonstrate the framework through a design task integrating personal narratives and visualizations, showing that their chosen assessments enable meaningful evaluation of design alternatives against psychological theories and intended affective outcomes.
本文针对沟通型可视化中情感目标评估困难的问题展开研究，指出认知目标评估成熟而情感目标评估尚缺有效方法。作者提出一套跨领域（教育、倡导、经济、健康、心理学）的评估标准选择框架，以匹配如态度转变或情感共鸣等情感目标。该框架应用于结合个人叙事与可视化的复杂设计任务，通过评估不同设计方案在心理学理论下的情感效果，验证其有效性。实验结果表明，所选评估方法能有效区分不同设计在情感影响上的差异，证明该框架对情感导向可视化设计具有指导价值。
True (VIS) Lies: Analyzing How Generative AI Recognizes Intentionality, Rhetoric, and Misleadingness in Visualization Lies
Authors: Graziano Blasilli, Marco Angelini
First: 2026-04-01T17:33:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:33:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study investigates the ability of multimodal Large Language Models (LLMs) to identify and interpret misleading visualizations, and recognize these observations along with their underlying causes and potential intentionality. Our analysis leverages concepts from visualization rhetoric and a newly developed taxonomy of authorial intents as explanatory lenses. We formulated three research questions and addressed them experimentally using a dataset of 2,336 COVID-19-related tweets, half of which contain misleading visualizations, and supplemented it with real-world examples of perceptual, cognitive, and conceptual errors drawn from VisLies, the IEEE VIS community event dedicated to showcasing deceptive and misleading visualizations. To ensure broad coverage of the current LLM landscape, we evaluated 16 state-of-the-art models. Among them, 15 are open-weight models, spanning a wide range of model sizes, architectural families, and reasoning capabilities. The selection comprises small models, namely Nemotron-Nano-V2-VL (12B parameters), Mistral-Small-3.2 (24B), DeepSeek-VL2 (27B), Gemma3 (27B), and GTA1 (32B); medium-sized models, namely Qianfan-VL (70B), Molmo (72B), GLM-4.5V (108B), LLaVA-NeXT (110B), and Pixtral-Large (124B); and large models, namely Qwen3-VL (235B), InternVL3.5 (241B), Step3 (321B), Llama-4-Maverick (400B), and Kimi-K2.5 (1000B). In addition, we employed OpenAI GPT-5.4, a frontier proprietary model. To establish a human perspective on these tasks, we also conducted a user study with visualization experts to assess how people perceive rhetorical techniques and the authorial intentions behind the same misleading visualizations. This allows comparison between model and expert behavior, revealing similarities and differences that provide insights into where LLMs align with human judgment and where they diverge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：真实的（视觉）谎言：分析生成式AI如何识别可视化谎言中的意图、修辞与误导性
本研究探讨多模态大语言模型（LLM）识别和解释误导性可视化的能力，并识别这些观察及其潜在原因和意图。我们的分析借助可视化修辞概念及新开发的作者意图分类法作为解释框架。我们提出三个研究问题，并使用包含2336条与COVID-19相关的推文的数据集进行实验，其中一半包含误导性可视化，并辅以VisLies（IEEE VIS社区活动，专门展示欺骗性和误导性可视化）中的感知、认知和概念错误真实案例。为覆盖当前LLM生态的广泛范围，我们评估了16个前沿模型，其中15个为开源模型，涵盖不同规模、架构家族和推理能力。选型包括小型模型（如Nemotron-Nano-V2-VL 12B、Mistral-Small-3.2 24B、DeepSeek-VL2 27B、Gemma3 27B、GTA1 32B）；中型模型（如Qianfan-VL 70B、Molmo 72B、GLM-4.5V 108B、LLaVA-NeXT 110B、Pixtral-Large 124B）；大型模型（如Qwen3-VL 235B、InternVL3.5 241B、Step3 321B、Llama-4-Maverick 400B、Kimi-K2.5 1000B）。此外，我们还评估了OpenAI的前沿专有模型GPT-5.4。为建立人类视角，我们还对可视化专家进行了用户研究，评估人们对相同误导性可视化所感知的修辞技巧及作者意图。这使我们能够比较模型与专家行为，揭示LLM与人类判断一致或分歧之处。
Summary / 总结
This study explores how multimodal large language models detect misleading visualizations by analyzing their ability to identify intentionality, rhetoric, and deception, using a taxonomy of authorial intent and visualization rhetoric as analytical frameworks. The researchers evaluated 16 state-of-the-art LLMs—including open-weight models ranging from 12B to 1000B parameters and OpenAI’s GPT-5.4—on a dataset of 2,336 COVID-19-related tweets, half containing misleading visuals, supplemented with real-world examples from VisLies. Experimental results show that while larger models generally perform better, many struggle to consistently attribute authorial intent or explain the rhetorical mechanisms behind deception. A parallel user study with visualization experts revealed that LLMs often align with human judgment on surface-level deception but frequently diverge in interpreting underlying intent or cognitive missteps, highlighting gaps in current models’ understanding of visual rhetoric.
为探究生成式AI如何识别误导性可视化，本研究评估了16个主流多模态大语言模型对含误导图表的识别能力，包括其对修辞手法和作者意图的解读。研究基于2336条含误导图表的新冠推文及VisLies真实案例，覆盖参数规模从12B至1000B的模型。实验表明，大模型在识别误导性及成因方面表现更优，但与可视化专家判断仍存在显著差距，尤其在修辞意图和感知误导的精细解读上，模型与人类认知存在明显偏差。
A ROS 2 Wrapper for Florence-2: Multi-Mode Local Vision-Language Inference for Robotic Systems
Authors: J. E. Domínguez-Vidal
First: 2026-04-01T17:29:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:29:59+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Foundation vision-language models are becoming increasingly relevant to robotics because they can provide richer semantic perception than narrow task-specific pipelines. However, their practical adoption in robot software stacks still depends on reproducible middleware integrations rather than on model quality alone. Florence-2 is especially attractive in this regard because it unifies captioning, optical character recognition, open-vocabulary detection, grounding and related vision-language tasks within a comparatively manageable model size. This article presents a ROS 2 wrapper for Florence-2 that exposes the model through three complementary interaction modes: continuous topic-driven processing, synchronous service calls and asynchronous actions. The wrapper is designed for local execution and supports both native installation and Docker container deployment. It also combines generic JSON outputs with standard ROS 2 message bindings for detection-oriented tasks. A functional validation is reported together with a throughput study on several GPUs, showing that local deployment is feasible with consumer grade hardware. The repository is publicly available here: https://github.com/JEDominguezVidal/florence2_ros2_wrapper
中文标题/摘要
标题：Florence-2 的 ROS 2 封装：面向机器人系统的多模式本地视觉-语言推理
基础视觉-语言模型正日益成为机器人领域的重要工具，因其能提供比专用任务管道更丰富的语义感知能力。然而，其在机器人软件栈中的实际应用仍依赖于可复现的中间件集成，而非模型质量本身。Florence-2 在此方面尤为突出，它在一个相对可控的模型规模内统一了图像描述、光学字符识别、开放词汇检测、定位及相关视觉-语言任务。本文提出了一种针对 Florence-2 的 ROS 2 封装，通过三种互补的交互模式暴露模型功能：连续主题驱动处理、同步服务调用和异步动作。该封装专为本地执行设计，支持原生安装和 Docker 容器部署，并将通用 JSON 输出与标准 ROS 2 消息绑定结合，以支持检测导向任务。文章报告了功能验证及在多款 GPU 上的吞吐量研究，表明使用消费级硬件即可实现本地部署。代码仓库公开于：https://github.com/JEDominguezVidal/florence2_ros2_wrapper
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust, reproducible integration of vision-language models into robotic systems, this work presents a ROS 2 wrapper for Florence-2, a compact yet powerful model supporting multiple vision-language tasks. The wrapper exposes Florence-2 through three interaction modes—topic-driven, service-based, and asynchronous actions—to suit diverse robotic workflows. Designed for local deployment, it supports both native and containerized execution and bridges model outputs with ROS 2 message formats for detection tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that Florence-2 can be effectively deployed on consumer-grade hardware, achieving practical throughput across multiple GPUs, validating its suitability for real-time robotic applications.
为实现视觉语言模型在机器人系统中的可靠集成，本文提出了一种针对Florence-2的ROS 2封装方案，该模型具备多模态视觉语言推理能力且体积可控。封装通过三种交互模式——主题驱动、服务调用和异步动作——适配不同机器人应用场景。支持本地部署及容器化运行，并将模型输出与ROS 2标准消息格式结合，便于检测任务使用。实验表明，该模型可在消费级硬件上实现有效部署，多GPU环境下具备实用吞吐量，验证其在机器人实时应用中的可行性。
Screening Is Enough
Authors: Ken M. Nakanishi
First: 2026-04-01T17:29:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:29:08+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A core limitation of standard softmax attention is that it does not define a notion of absolute query--key relevance: attention weights are obtained by redistributing a fixed unit mass across all keys according to their relative scores. As a result, relevance is defined only relative to competing keys, and irrelevant keys cannot be explicitly rejected. We introduce Multiscreen, a language-model architecture built around a mechanism we call screening, which enables absolute query--key relevance. Instead of redistributing attention across all keys, screening evaluates each key against an explicit threshold, discarding irrelevant keys and aggregating the remaining keys, thereby removing global competition among keys. Across experiments, Multiscreen achieves comparable validation loss with approximately 40% fewer parameters than a Transformer baseline, enables stable optimization at substantially larger learning rates, maintains strong performance in long-context perplexity, shows little to no degradation in retrieval performance even far beyond the training context length, and reduces inference latency by up to 3.2$\times$ at 100K context length.
中文标题/摘要
标题：筛选即足够
标准softmax注意力的核心局限在于，它未定义查询与键之间的绝对相关性：注意力权重通过将固定单位质量按相对得分重新分配至所有键获得。因此，相关性仅相对于竞争键定义，无法显式拒绝无关键。我们引入了Multiscreen，一种围绕我们称为“筛选”的机制构建的语言模型架构，以实现绝对查询-键相关性。筛选机制不将注意力重新分配至所有键，而是对每个键进行显式阈值评估，丢弃无关键并聚合剩余键，从而消除键之间的全局竞争。实验表明，Multiscreen在参数量约为Transformer基线的40%的情况下，验证损失相当；支持在大幅提高学习率下稳定优化；在长上下文困惑度上保持强性能；即使远超训练上下文长度，检索性能也几乎无下降；并在10万上下文长度下，推理延迟降低高达3.2倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of standard softmax attention, which lacks a mechanism to explicitly reject irrelevant keys and defines relevance only relative to others, the paper introduces Multiscreen, a language model architecture that employs a screening mechanism to establish absolute query-key relevance. Instead of redistributing attention across all keys, Multiscreen evaluates each key against a threshold, discarding irrelevant ones and aggregating only the remaining. Experimental results show Multiscreen achieves comparable validation loss with about 40% fewer parameters than a Transformer baseline, supports stable training at higher learning rates, maintains strong long-context performance, shows minimal retrieval degradation beyond training length, and reduces inference latency by up to 3.2x at 100K context length.
针对标准softmax注意力机制无法明确拒绝无关键值、仅能相对比较相关性的核心缺陷，本文提出Multiscreen语言模型架构，引入筛选机制以建立绝对查询-键相关性。该机制不进行全局注意力重分配，而是对每个键值设定阈值，剔除无关键值后仅聚合剩余键值，消除全局竞争。实验表明，Multiscreen在参数量减少约40%的情况下达到与Transformer基线相当的验证损失，支持更高学习率下的稳定训练，在长上下文困惑度上保持强性能，即使超出训练长度仍几乎无检索性能下降，并在10万上下文长度下推理延迟降低最高达3.2倍。
NeuroDDAF: Neural Dynamic Diffusion-Advection Fields with Evidential Fusion for Air Quality Forecasting
Authors: Prasanjit Dey, Soumyabrata Dev, Angela Meyer, Bianca Schoen-Phelan
First: 2026-04-01T17:27:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:27:43+00:00
Comments: This manuscript is under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate air quality forecasting is crucial for protecting public health and guiding environmental policy, yet it remains challenging due to nonlinear spatiotemporal dynamics, wind-driven transport, and distribution shifts across regions. Physics-based models are interpretable but computationally expensive and often rely on restrictive assumptions, whereas purely data-driven models can be accurate but may lack robustness and calibrated uncertainty. To address these limitations, we propose Neural Dynamic Diffusion-Advection Fields (NeuroDDAF), a physics-informed forecasting framework that unifies neural representation learning with open-system transport modeling. NeuroDDAF integrates (i) a GRU-Graph Attention encoder to capture temporal dynamics and wind-aware spatial interactions, (ii) a Fourier-domain diffusion-advection module with learnable residuals, (iii) a wind-modulated latent Neural ODE to model continuous-time evolution under time-varying connectivity, and (iv) an evidential fusion mechanism that adaptively combines physics-guided and neural forecasts while quantifying uncertainty. Experiments on four urban datasets (Beijing, Shenzhen, Tianjin, and Ancona) across 1-3 day horizons show that NeuroDDAF consistently outperforms strong baselines, including AirPhyNet, achieving up to 9.7% reduction in RMSE and 9.4% reduction in MAE on long-term forecasts. On the Beijing dataset, NeuroDDAF attains an RMSE of 41.63 $μ$g/m$^3$ for 1-day prediction and 48.88 $μ$g/m$^3$ for 3-day prediction, representing the best performance among all compared methods. In addition, NeuroDDAF improves cross-city generalization and yields well-calibrated uncertainty estimates, as confirmed by ensemble variance analysis and case studies under varying wind conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuroDDAF：用于空气质量预测的具有证据融合的神经动态扩散-对流场
准确的空气质量预测对于保护公众健康和指导环境政策至关重要，但由于非线性时空动态、风驱动传输及区域分布偏移，仍具挑战性。基于物理的模型可解释性强但计算成本高且常依赖严格假设，而纯数据驱动模型虽准确但可能缺乏鲁棒性和校准的不确定性。为解决这些局限，我们提出神经动态扩散-对流场（NeuroDDAF），一种融合神经表征学习与开放系统传输建模的物理信息预测框架。NeuroDDAF整合：(i) GRU-图注意力编码器以捕捉时序动态和风感知的空间交互，(ii) 傅里叶域扩散-对流模块带可学习残差，(iii) 风调制的潜在神经常微分方程以建模时变连接下的连续时间演化，(iv) 证据融合机制自适应结合物理引导与神经预测并量化不确定性。在四个城市数据集（北京、深圳、天津、安科纳）1-3天预测范围的实验表明，NeuroDDAF持续优于强基线模型（如AirPhyNet），长期预测中RMSE降低高达9.7%，MAE降低9.4%。在北京数据集上，NeuroDDAF实现1天预测RMSE为41.63 μg/m³，3天预测为48.88 μg/m³，为所有对比方法中最佳表现。此外，NeuroDDAF提升跨城市泛化能力并提供校准良好的不确定性估计，经集合方差分析及不同风况案例研究验证。
Summary / 总结
To improve air quality forecasting under complex spatiotemporal dynamics and wind-driven transport, NeuroDDAF integrates physics-informed modeling with neural networks using a GRU-Graph Attention encoder, a Fourier-domain diffusion-advection module, a wind-modulated Neural ODE, and an evidential fusion mechanism for uncertainty-aware forecasting. Evaluated on four urban datasets across 1-3 day horizons, NeuroDDAF outperforms baselines like AirPhyNet by up to 9.7% reduction in RMSE and 9.4% in MAE, achieving the lowest RMSE of 41.63 μg/m³ for 1-day forecasts in Beijing and 48.88 μg/m³ for 3-day forecasts, while also enhancing cross-city generalization and providing calibrated uncertainty estimates.
为应对空气质量预测中的非线性时空动态与风驱动传输问题，NeuroDDAF融合物理模型与神经网络，采用GRU-图注意力编码器、傅里叶域扩散-对流模块、风调制神经ODE及证据融合机制以实现不确定性感知预测。在四个城市数据集上1-3天预测中，其表现优于AirPhyNet等基线模型，最高减少9.7% RMSE和9.4% MAE，在北京1天预测中达到41.63 μg/m³最优RMSE，3天预测为48.88 μg/m³，同时提升跨城泛化能力与不确定性校准效果。
Processing and acquisition traces in visual encoders: What does CLIP know about your camera?
Authors: Ryan Ramos, Vladan Stojnić, Giorgos Kordopatis-Zilos, Yuta Nakashima, Giorgos Tolias, Noa Garcia
Venue: ICCV 2025 highlight
First: 2025-08-14T13:34:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:25:51+00:00
Comments: 8 main pages, supplementary attached, ICCV 2025 highlight
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Prior work has analyzed the robustness of visual encoders to image transformations and corruptions, particularly in cases where such alterations are not seen during training. When this occurs, they introduce a form of distribution shift at test time, often leading to performance degradation. The primary focus has been on severe corruptions that, when applied aggressively, distort useful signals necessary for accurate semantic predictions. We take a different perspective by analyzing parameters of the image acquisition process and transformations that may be subtle or even imperceptible to the human eye. We find that such parameters are systematically encoded in the learned visual representations and can be easily recovered. More strikingly, their presence can have a profound impact, either positively or negatively, on semantic predictions. This effect depends on whether there is a strong correlation or anti-correlation between semantic labels and these acquisition-based or processing-based labels. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/ryan-caesar-ramos/visual-encoder-traces
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉编码器中的处理与采集痕迹：CLIP对您的相机了解多少？
先前研究分析了视觉编码器对图像变换和损坏的鲁棒性，特别是在训练时未见过此类改变的情况下。此时会在测试时引入分布偏移，常导致性能下降。研究重点多为严重损坏，当其被激进应用时，会扭曲对准确语义预测至关重要的信号。 我们采取不同视角，分析图像采集过程和可能细微甚至人眼难以察觉的变换参数。我们发现，这些参数系统性地编码在学习到的视觉表征中，且易于恢复。更引人注目的是，这些参数的存在会对语义预测产生深远影响，无论是正面还是负面，其影响取决于语义标签与基于采集或处理的标签之间是否存在强相关或负相关。代码和数据见：https://github.com/ryan-caesar-ramos/visual-encoder-traces
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how visual encoders like CLIP encode subtle traces of image acquisition parameters and processing transformations that are imperceptible to humans but systematically captured in learned representations. Motivated by the observation that such parameters can significantly influence semantic predictions, the authors analyze their recoverability and impact on model performance under distribution shifts. Experiments reveal that these acquisition-based features correlate strongly with semantic labels, either enhancing or degrading predictions depending on alignment, and demonstrate that even minor camera or processing variations can be decoded from encoder outputs with high accuracy.
该论文研究了视觉编码器（如CLIP）如何编码并响应图像采集与处理中的细微参数（如相机设置或后期处理变换），这些参数通常在训练阶段未被考虑。与以往聚焦于严重图像损坏不同，作者分析了这些微小但真实存在的采集痕迹如何被系统性地编码在学习到的视觉表征中，并可被可靠恢复。实验表明，这些采集痕迹会显著影响语义预测结果——其影响方向取决于语义标签与采集特征之间是正相关还是负相关。
Safe learning-based control via function-based uncertainty quantification
Authors: Abdullah Tokmak, Toni Karvonen, Thomas B. Schön, Dominik Baumann
First: 2026-04-01T17:23:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:23:30+00:00
Comments: Under review for CDC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Uncertainty quantification is essential when deploying learning-based control methods in safety-critical systems. This is commonly realized by constructing uncertainty tubes that enclose the unknown function of interest, e.g., the reward and constraint functions or the underlying dynamics model, with high probability. However, existing approaches for uncertainty quantification typically rely on restrictive assumptions on the unknown function, such as known bounds on functional norms or Lipschitz constants, and struggle with discontinuities. In this paper, we model the unknown function as a random function from which independent and identically distributed realizations can be generated, and construct uncertainty tubes via the scenario approach that hold with high probability and rely solely on the sampled realizations. We integrate these uncertainty tubes into a safe Bayesian optimization algorithm, which we then use to safely tune control parameters on a real Furuta pendulum.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于函数不确定性量化实现安全的学习控制
在安全关键系统中部署基于学习的控制方法时，不确定性量化至关重要。通常通过构建置信度高的不确定性管来包围未知函数（如奖励和约束函数或底层动力学模型）。然而，现有不确定性量化方法通常依赖于对未知函数的严格假设，如已知函数范数或利普希茨常数的界，且难以处理不连续性。本文将未知函数建模为随机函数，可从中独立同分布地生成样本，并通过场景方法构建置信度高的不确定性管，仅依赖于采样结果。我们将这些不确定性管集成到安全的贝叶斯优化算法中，并用于在真实福拉塔摆上安全地调参。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe deployment of learning-based control in critical systems, this paper addresses limitations in existing uncertainty quantification methods that require restrictive assumptions like bounded Lipschitz constants or functional norms. The authors model the unknown function as a random variable and construct high-probability uncertainty tubes using the scenario approach, relying only on sampled realizations and avoiding assumptions about continuity or smoothness. These tubes are integrated into a safe Bayesian optimization framework to tune control parameters, and experiments on a real Furuta pendulum demonstrate that the method successfully maintains safety while achieving effective control performance.

Open-Set Supervised 3D Anomaly Detection: An Industrial Dataset and a Generalisable Framework for Unknown Defects
Authors: Hanzhe Liang, Luocheng Zhang, Junyang Xia, HanLiang Zhou, Bingyang Guo, Yingxi Xie, Can Gao, Ruiyun Yu, Jinbao Wang, Pan Li
First: 2026-04-01T17:23:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:23:23+00:00
Comments: Resources: https://github.com/hzzzzzhappy/open-industry
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Although self-supervised 3D anomaly detection assumes that acquiring high-precision point clouds is computationally expensive, in real manufacturing scenarios it is often feasible to collect a limited number of anomalous samples. Therefore, we study open-set supervised 3D anomaly detection, where the model is trained with only normal samples and a small number of known anomalous samples, aiming to identify unknown anomalies at test time. We present Open-Industry, a high-quality industrial dataset containing 15 categories, each with five real anomaly types collected from production lines. We first adapt general open-set anomaly detection methods to accommodate 3D point cloud inputs better. Building upon this, we propose Open3D-AD, a point-cloud-oriented approach that leverages normal samples, simulated anomalies, and partially observed real anomalies to model the probability density distributions of normal and anomalous data. Then, we introduce a simple Correspondence Distributions Subsampling to reduce the overlap between normal and non-normal distributions, enabling stronger dual distributions modeling. Based on these contributions, we establish a comprehensive benchmark and evaluate the proposed method extensively on Open-Industry as well as established datasets including Real3D-AD and Anomaly-ShapeNet. Benchmark results and ablation studies demonstrate the effectiveness of Open3D-AD and further reveal the potential of open-set supervised 3D anomaly detection.

标题：开放集监督3D异常检测：一个工业数据集与适用于未知缺陷的通用框架
