Where-to-Learn: Analytical Policy Gradient Directed Exploration for On-Policy Robotic Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Leixin Chang, Xinchen Yao, Ben Liu, Liangjing Yang, Hua Chen
First: 2026-03-28T15:50:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:22:43+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
On-policy reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms have demonstrated great potential in robotic control, where effective exploration is crucial for efficient and high-quality policy learning. However, how to encourage the agent to explore the better trajectories efficiently remains a challenge. Most existing methods incentivize exploration by maximizing the policy entropy or encouraging novel state visiting regardless of the potential state value. We propose a new form of directed exploration that uses analytical policy gradients from a differentiable dynamics model to inject task-aware, physics-guided guidance, thereby steering the agent towards high-reward regions for accelerated and more effective policy learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Where-to-Learn：基于分析策略梯度的定向探索用于在线机器人强化学习
在线强化学习算法在机器人控制中展现出巨大潜力，其中有效的探索对高效且高质量的策略学习至关重要。然而，如何高效引导智能体探索更优轨迹仍是一个挑战。现有大多数方法通过最大化策略熵或鼓励访问新状态来激励探索，而忽视潜在状态价值。我们提出一种新的定向探索形式，利用可微动力学模型的分析策略梯度，注入任务感知、物理引导的指导，从而引导智能体朝高奖励区域移动，实现更快速、更有效的策略学习。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient exploration in on-policy robotic reinforcement learning, this paper introduces a directed exploration method that leverages analytical policy gradients derived from a differentiable dynamics model to guide the agent toward high-reward trajectories. Unlike entropy-based or novelty-driven approaches, the proposed method injects task-aware, physics-guided signals to steer exploration more effectively. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach accelerates policy learning and improves performance compared to baseline methods without compromising sample efficiency.
为提升机器人强化学习中策略学习的效率，本文提出一种基于可微动力学模型的分析性策略梯度引导探索方法，通过注入任务感知的物理引导信号，引导智能体探索高回报轨迹。与基于熵或新颖性激励的现有方法不同，该方法更精准地引导探索方向。实验表明，该方法能显著加速策略学习并提升性能，同时保持样本效率。
Robust Geospatial Coordination of Multi-Agent Communications Networks Under Attrition
Authors: Jonathan S. Kent, Eliana Stefani, Brian Plancher
First: 2025-11-30T22:13:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:21:59+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables, accepted to IEEE RA-L
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Coordinating emergency responses in extreme environments, such as wildfires, requires resilient and high-bandwidth communication backbones. While autonomous aerial swarms can establish ad-hoc networks to provide this connectivity, the high risk of individual node attrition in these settings often leads to network fragmentation and mission-critical downtime. To overcome this challenge, we introduce and formalize the problem of Robust Task Networking Under Attrition (RTNUA), which extends connectivity maintenance in multi-robot systems to explicitly address proactive redundancy and attrition recovery. We then introduce Physics-Informed Robust Employment of Multi-Agent Networks ($Φ$IREMAN), a topological algorithm leveraging physics-inspired potential fields to solve this problem. In our evaluations, $Φ$IREMAN consistently outperforms baselines, and is able to maintain greater than $99.9\%$ task uptime despite substantial attrition in simulations with up to 100 tasks and 500 drones, demonstrating both effectiveness and scalability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在损耗环境下多智能体通信网络的鲁棒地理协调
在极端环境（如野火）中协调应急响应，需要具备弹性和高带宽的通信骨干网络。虽然自主空中编队可以建立临时网络以提供这种连通性，但这些环境中单个节点高损耗率常导致网络碎片化和关键任务中断。 为解决此问题，我们提出并形式化了“损耗环境下鲁棒任务网络”（RTNUA）问题，将多机器人系统中的连通性维护扩展至明确处理主动冗余和损耗恢复。随后，我们引入了“物理启发式鲁棒多智能体网络部署”（ΦIREMAN）拓扑算法，该算法利用物理启发势场求解此问题。在评估中，ΦIREMAN持续优于基线方法，即使在模拟中高达100个任务和500架无人机且损耗显著的情况下，仍能保持超过99.9%的任务可用性，证明其有效性和可扩展性。

Evaluated in simulations with up to 500 drones and 100 tasks, ΦIREMAN consistently outperformed baseline methods, maintaining over 99.9% task uptime even under substantial attrition, demonstrating both robustness and scalability.
为保障高风险环境（如野火）下应急响应通信的可靠性，该文提出并形式化了“抗损耗鲁棒任务网络”（RTNUA）问题，并设计了基于物理势场的拓扑算法ΦIREMAN，以主动管理冗余并应对节点损耗。在包含最多500架无人机和100项任务的仿真中，ΦIREMAN持续优于基线方法，即使在显著损耗下仍保持99.9%以上的任务可用性，展现出强鲁棒性与可扩展性。
Online Reasoning Calibration: Test-Time Training Enables Generalizable Conformal LLM Reasoning
Authors: Cai Zhou, Zekai Wang, Menghua Wu, Qianyu Julie Zhu, Flora C. Shi, Chenyu Wang, Ashia Wilson, Tommi Jaakkola, Stephen Bates
First: 2026-04-01T17:21:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:21:50+00:00
Comments: 20 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
While test-time scaling has enabled large language models to solve highly difficult tasks, state-of-the-art results come at exorbitant compute costs. These inefficiencies can be attributed to the miscalibration of post-trained language models, and the lack of calibration in popular sampling techniques. Here, we present Online Reasoning Calibration (ORCA), a framework for calibrating the sampling process that draws upon conformal prediction and test-time training. Specifically, we introduce a meta-learning procedure that updates the calibration module for each input. This allows us to provide valid confidence estimates under distributional shift, e.g. in thought patterns that occur across different stages of reasoning, or in prompt distributions between model development and deployment. ORCA not only provides theoretical guarantees on conformal risks, but also empirically shows higher efficiency and generalization across different reasoning tasks. At risk level $δ=0.1$, ORCA improves Qwen2.5-32B efficiency on in-distribution tasks with savings up to 47.5% with supervised labels and 40.7% with self-consistency labels. Under zero-shot out-of-domain settings, it improves MATH-500 savings from 24.8% of the static calibration baseline to 67.0% while maintaining a low empirical error rate, and the same trend holds across model families and downstream benchmarks. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/wzekai99/ORCA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在线推理校准：测试时训练实现可泛化的置信LLM推理
尽管测试时扩展使大语言模型能够解决高度困难的任务，但当前最先进结果需耗费高昂计算成本。这些低效性源于后训练语言模型的校准偏差，以及主流采样技术缺乏校准。本文提出在线推理校准（ORCA）框架，结合置信预测与测试时训练，校准采样过程。具体而言，我们引入元学习过程，为每个输入更新校准模块，从而在分布偏移下（如不同推理阶段的思维模式，或模型开发与部署间的提示分布）提供有效置信估计。ORCA不仅在置信风险上提供理论保证，实证上也展现出更高效率与跨任务泛化能力。在置信水平δ=0.1时，ORCA在分布内任务中，使用监督标签节省47.5%，使用自一致性标签节省40.7%。在零样本分布外设置下，其在MATH-500任务中将静态校准基线的节省率从24.8%提升至67.0%，同时保持较低实证错误率，且该趋势在不同模型家族及下游基准中一致。代码公开于https://github.com/wzekai99/ORCA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and miscalibration of large language models under test-time scaling, this paper introduces Online Reasoning Calibration (ORCA), a framework that combines conformal prediction with test-time training to dynamically calibrate sampling during reasoning. ORCA employs a meta-learning approach to update a calibration module per input, enabling valid confidence estimates even under distributional shifts in thought patterns or prompt distributions. Experiments show that at δ=0.1, ORCA achieves up to 47.5% compute savings on in-distribution tasks with supervised labels and 67.0% improvement over static calibration in zero-shot out-of-domain settings on MATH-500, while maintaining low empirical error rates across models and benchmarks.
为解决当前大语言模型推理方法因校准不足导致的计算效率低下问题，作者提出在线推理校准（ORCA）框架，结合置信区间预测与元学习，在推理时动态校准采样过程。该方法针对每个输入更新校准模块，支持在推理阶段分布偏移（如思维模式变化或提示分布差异）下仍提供有效置信度估计。实验表明，在δ=0.1时，ORCA在分布内任务中可节省最多47.5%的计算量（监督标签）或40.7%（自一致性标签），在零样本跨域场景下效率提升从24.8%增至67.0%，同时保持较低错误率，且效果在不同模型和下游基准上具有一致性。
Bridging the Simulation-to-Experiment Gap with Generative Models using Adversarial Distribution Alignment
Authors: Kai Nelson, Tobias Kreiman, Sergey Levine, Aditi S. Krishnapriyan
First: 2026-04-01T17:21:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:21:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A fundamental challenge in science and engineering is the simulation-to-experiment gap. While we often possess prior knowledge of physical laws, these physical laws can be too difficult to solve exactly for complex systems. Such systems are commonly modeled using simulators, which impose computational approximations. Meanwhile, experimental measurements more faithfully represent the real world, but experimental data typically consists of observations that only partially reflect the system's full underlying state. We propose a data-driven distribution alignment framework that bridges this simulation-to-experiment gap by pre-training a generative model on fully observed (but imperfect) simulation data, then aligning it with partial (but real) observations of experimental data. While our method is domain-agnostic, we ground our approach in the physical sciences by introducing Adversarial Distribution Alignment (ADA). This method aligns a generative model of atomic positions -- initially trained on a simulated Boltzmann distribution -- with the distribution of experimental observations. We prove that our method recovers the target observable distribution, even with multiple, potentially correlated observables. We also empirically validate our framework on synthetic, molecular, and experimental protein data, demonstrating that it can align generative models with diverse observables. Our code is available at https://kaityrusnelson.com/ada/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用对抗分布对齐的生成模型弥合仿真与实验之间的差距
科学与工程中的一个基本挑战是仿真与实验之间的差距。虽然我们通常掌握物理定律的先验知识，但对于复杂系统，这些定律往往难以精确求解。这类系统常通过引入计算近似而建模的仿真器进行处理。同时，实验测量更真实地反映现实世界，但实验数据通常仅部分反映系统的完整底层状态。我们提出一种数据驱动的分布对齐框架，通过在完全观测（但不完美）的仿真数据上预训练生成模型，再将其与部分（但真实）的实验观测数据对齐，从而弥合这一差距。尽管我们的方法领域无关，但我们在物理科学领域中引入了对抗分布对齐（ADA）方法，该方法将原子位置的生成模型（最初在模拟的玻尔兹曼分布上训练）与实验观测分布对齐。我们证明，即使存在多个可能相关的可观测量，我们的方法仍能恢复目标可观测分布。我们还在合成、分子和实验蛋白质数据上实证验证了该框架，表明其能将生成模型与多样化的可观测量对齐。代码可在 https://kaityrusnelson.com/ada/ 获取。
Summary / 总结
To address the simulation-to-experiment gap in science and engineering, where simulations approximate physical laws but lack real-world fidelity and experiments capture partial truths, the authors propose Adversarial Distribution Alignment (ADA), a data-driven framework that pre-trains a generative model on simulated data and then aligns it with real experimental observations. ADA uses adversarial learning to match the distribution of atomic positions generated from simulated Boltzmann distributions with those observed in experiments, even when only partial measurements are available. Experimental results across synthetic, molecular, and real protein datasets show that ADA successfully recovers target observable distributions, even with multiple correlated observables, demonstrating robust alignment across diverse data types.
为解决科学与工程领域中模拟与实验数据之间的鸿沟——模拟数据虽基于物理定律但存在计算近似，实验数据虽更贴近现实却常仅反映系统部分状态——作者提出对抗分布对齐（ADA）方法。该方法先在模拟数据上预训练生成模型，再通过对抗学习使其生成分布与实验观测分布对齐，即使仅能获取部分观测数据。在合成、分子及真实蛋白质数据上的实验表明，ADA能有效恢复目标可观测量分布，即使面对多个相关可观测量，仍能实现跨数据类型鲁棒对齐。
S0 Tuning: Zero-Overhead Adaptation of Hybrid Recurrent-Attention Models
Authors: Jack Young
First: 2026-04-01T17:21:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:21:15+00:00
Comments: 15 pages (10 main + 5 appendix), 3 figures, code at https://github.com/jackyoung27/s0-tuning
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Using roughly 48 execution-verified HumanEval training solutions, tuning a single initial state matrix per recurrent layer, with zero inference overhead, outperforms LoRA by +10.8 pp (p < 0.001) on HumanEval. The method, which we call S0 tuning, optimizes one state matrix per recurrent layer while freezing all model weights. On Qwen3.5-4B (GatedDeltaNet hybrid), S0 tuning improves greedy pass@1 by +23.6 +/- 1.7 pp (10 seeds). On FalconH1-7B (Mamba-2 hybrid), S0 reaches 71.8% +/- 1.3 and LoRA reaches 71.4% +/- 2.4 (3 seeds), statistically indistinguishable at this sample size while requiring no weight merging. Cross-domain transfer is significant on MATH-500 (+4.8 pp, p = 0.00002, 8 seeds) and GSM8K (+2.8 pp, p = 0.0003, 10 seeds); a text-to-SQL benchmark (Spider) shows no transfer, consistent with the trajectory-steering mechanism. A prefix-tuning control on a pure Transformer (Qwen2.5-3B) degrades performance by -13.9 pp under all nine configurations tested. On Qwen3.5, a per-step state-offset variant reaches +27.1 pp, above both S0 and LoRA but with per-step inference cost. Taken together, the results show that recurrent state initialization is a strong zero-inference-overhead PEFT surface for hybrid language models when verified supervision is scarce. The tuned state is a ~48 MB file; task switching requires no weight merging or model reload. Code and library: https://github.com/jackyoung27/s0-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：S0调优：混合循环-注意力模型的零开销自适应
使用约48个经过执行验证的人类评估训练方案，对每个循环层仅调优一个初始状态矩阵，且无推理开销，其性能在HumanEval上比LoRA高出+10.8个百分点（p < 0.001）。该方法称为S0调优，通过冻结所有模型权重，仅优化每个循环层的一个状态矩阵。在Qwen3.5-4B（GatedDeltaNet混合架构）上，S0调优使贪心pass@1提升+23.6±1.7个百分点（10个种子）。在FalconH1-7B（Mamba-2混合架构）上，S0达到71.8%±1.3，LoRA为71.4%±2.4（3个种子），在该样本量下统计上无显著差异，且无需权重合并。在MATH-500上跨领域迁移显著（+4.8个百分点，p=0.00002，8个种子），在GSM8K上亦有+2.8个百分点（p=0.0003，10个种子）；在文本到SQL基准（Spider）上无迁移，符合轨迹引导机制。在纯Transformer（Qwen2.5-3B）上进行前缀调优控制，九种配置下均性能下降-13.9个百分点。在Qwen3.5上，每步状态偏移变体达到+27.1个百分点，超越S0和LoRA，但伴随每步推理开销。综合来看，结果表明：当监督验证稀缺时，循环状态初始化是混合语言模型中强大的零推理开销PEFT方法。调优后的状态为约48MB文件；任务切换无需权重合并或模型重载。代码与库：https://github.com/jackyoung27/s0-tuning。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, zero-inference-overhead adaptation in hybrid recurrent-attention models, the paper introduces S0 tuning—a method that optimizes a single initial state matrix per recurrent layer while freezing all model weights. Experiments show S0 tuning outperforms LoRA by +10.8 pp on HumanEval, boosts greedy pass@1 by +23.6 pp on Qwen3.5-4B, and achieves statistically similar performance to LoRA on FalconH1-7B without weight merging. It also demonstrates cross-domain transfer on MATH-500 and GSM8K, while showing no transfer on Spider, aligning with its trajectory-steering mechanism. A prefix-tuning control on a pure Transformer degraded performance, and a per-step variant achieved higher gains but at the cost of per-step inference overhead.
为在监督数据稀缺时实现高效且无推理开销的混合循环-注意力模型适配，该研究提出S0调优方法，仅优化每层循环模块的初始状态矩阵，同时冻结所有模型权重。实验表明，S0调优在HumanEval上比LoRA高出10.8个百分点，在Qwen3.5-4B上提升greedy pass@1达23.6个百分点，在FalconH1-7B上与LoRA表现相当且无需权重合并。它在MATH-500和GSM8K上表现出跨域迁移能力，但在Spider数据集上无迁移，符合其轨迹引导机制。纯Transformer上的前缀调优控制反而导致性能下降，而每步状态偏移变体虽表现更优，但需每步推理开销。
AdaLoRA-QAT: Adaptive Low-Rank and Quantization-Aware Segmentation
Authors: Prantik Deb, Srimanth Dhondy, N. Ramakrishna, Anu Kapoor, Raju S. Bapi, Tapabrata Chakraborti
Venue: ISBI 2026 Oral Presentation
First: 2026-04-01T17:18:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:18:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ISBI 2026(Oral Presentation)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Chest X-ray (CXR) segmentation is an important step in computer-aided diagnosis, yet deploying large foundation models in clinical settings remains challenging due to computational constraints. We propose AdaLoRA-QAT, a two-stage fine-tuning framework that combines adaptive low-rank encoder adaptation with full quantization-aware training. Adaptive rank allocation improves parameter efficiency, while selective mixed-precision INT8 quantization preserves structural fidelity crucial for clinical reliability. Evaluated across large-scale CXR datasets, AdaLoRA-QAT achieves 95.6% Dice, matching full-precision SAM decoder fine-tuning while reducing trainable parameters by 16.6\times and yielding 2.24\times model compression. A Wilcoxon signed-rank test confirms that quantization does not significantly degrade segmentation accuracy. These results demonstrate that AdaLoRA-QAT effectively balances accuracy, efficiency, and structural trust-worthiness, enabling compact and deployable foundation models for medical image segmentation. Code and pretrained models are available at: https://prantik-pdeb.github.io/adaloraqat.github.io/
中文标题/摘要

To address the computational burden of deploying large foundation models for chest X-ray segmentation in clinical settings, AdaLoRA-QAT introduces a two-stage fine-tuning method combining adaptive low-rank adaptation with quantization-aware training. The framework dynamically allocates low-rank parameters to enhance efficiency while applying selective INT8 quantization to preserve structural accuracy critical for medical reliability. 为解决大型基础模型在临床环境中部署胸片分割任务时的计算负担，AdaLoRA-QAT提出一种两阶段微调方法，结合自适应低秩适配与量化感知训练。框架动态分配低秩参数以提升效率，同时采用选择性INT8量化以保留对临床可靠性至关重要的结构保真度。在大规模胸片数据集上实验表明，该方法达到95.6%的Dice分数，与全精度微调性能相当，同时训练参数减少16.6倍，模型压缩率达2.24倍。Wilcoxon符号秩检验证实量化未显著降低分割精度，证明其在效率、精度与临床可信度间实现了有效平衡。
Evaluating LLM-Generated ACSL Annotations for Formal Verification
Authors: Arshad Beg, Diarmuid O'Donoghue, Rosemary Monahan
First: 2026-02-14T19:18:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:15:52+00:00
Comments: 12 pages. Formal Techniques for Judicious Programming FTfJP-2026 at ECOOP. Conditionally Accepted
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Formal specifications are crucial for building verifiable and dependable software systems, yet generating accurate and verifiable specifications for real-world C programs remains challenging. This paper empirically evaluates the extent to which formal-analysis tools can automatically generate and verify ACSL specifications without human or learning-based assistance. We conduct a controlled study on a recently released dataset of 506 C programs, repurposing it from interactive, developer-driven workflows to an automated evaluation setting. Five ACSL generation systems are compared: a rule-based Python script, Frama-C's RTE plugin, and three large language models--DeepSeek-V3.2, GPT-5.2, and OLMo 3.1 32B Instruct. All generated specifications are verified under identical conditions using the Frama-C WP plugin powered by multiple SMT solvers, allowing a direct comparison of annotation quality, solver sensitivity, and proof stability. Our results provide new empirical evidence on the capabilities and limitations of automated ACSL generation, complementing prior survey-based work.

标题：评估大语言模型生成的ACSL注释在形式验证中的表现
形式规范对于构建可验证和可靠的软件系统至关重要，但为现实世界的C程序生成准确且可验证的规范仍具挑战性。本文实证评估了形式分析工具在无需人工或基于学习的辅助情况下，自动生成并验证ACSL规范的能力。我们在一个包含506个C程序的最新数据集上开展受控研究，将该数据集从交互式、开发者驱动的工作流程重新用于自动化评估场景。比较了五种ACSL生成系统：一个基于规则的Python脚本、Frama-C的RTE插件，以及三个大语言模型——DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-5.2和OLMo 3.1 32B Instruct。所有生成的规范均在相同条件下使用Frama-C WP插件（结合多个SMT求解器）进行验证，从而可直接比较注释质量、求解器敏感性及证明稳定性。我们的结果为自动化ACSL生成的能力与局限性提供了新的实证证据，补充了此前基于调查的工作。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates the feasibility of automatically generating ACSL specifications for C programs using tools without human intervention, motivated by the need for scalable formal verification. Five methods—rule-based script, Frama-C’s RTE plugin, and three LLMs (DeepSeek-V3.2, GPT-5.2, OLMo 3.1 32B Instruct)—were evaluated on a 506-program dataset, with all generated annotations verified under identical conditions using Frama-C’s WP plugin and multiple SMT solvers. 该研究旨在评估无需人工干预的自动ACSL注解生成工具在C程序形式化验证中的可行性，动机源于对可扩展形式验证的需求。在506个程序的数据集上，对比了五种方法——规则脚本、Frama-C的RTE插件及三个大语言模型（DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-5.2、OLMo 3.1 32B Instruct），在统一的Frama-C WP插件与多种SMT求解器环境下进行验证。结果表明，LLM生成的注解比规则脚本更全面，但验证成功率受求解器和程序复杂度影响显著，其中GPT-5.2和OLMo的证明稳定性优于DeepSeek-V3.2，但整体仍不及人工编写注解的可靠性。
Latent-Y: A Lab-Validated Autonomous Agent for De Novo Drug Design
Authors: Latent Labs Team, Sebastian M. Schmon, Daniella Pretorius, Simon Mathis, Rebecca Bartke-Croughan, Aishaini Puvanendran, James Vuckovic, Henry Kenlay, Mária Vlachynská, Alex Bridgland, Ivan Grishin, Sven Over, David Li, Bridget Li, Jonathan Crabbé, Agrin Hilmkil, Alexander W. R. Nelson, David Yuan, Annette Obika, Simon A. A. Kohl
First: 2026-03-31T13:26:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:14:29+00:00
Comments: A.N. performed work as an advisor to Latent Labs. A.H. and D.Y. performed work while at Latent Labs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Drug discovery relies on iterative expert workflows that are slow to parallelize and difficult to scale. Here we introduce Latent-Y, an AI agent that autonomously executes complete antibody design campaigns from text prompts, covering literature review, target analysis, epitope identification, candidate design, computational validation, and selection of lab-ready sequences. Latent-Y is integrated into the Latent Labs Platform, where it operates in the same environment as drug-discovery experts with access to bioinformatics tools, biological databases, and scientific literature. The agent can run fully autonomously end-to-end, or collaboratively, where researchers review progress, provide feedback, and direct subsequent steps. Candidate antibodies are generated using Latent-X2, our frontier generative model for drug-like antibody design. We demonstrate the agent's capability across three distinct campaign types: epitope discovery guided by therapeutic specifications, cross-species binder design, and autonomous design from a scientific publication targeting human transferrin receptor for blood-brain barrier crossing. Across nine targets, Latent-Y produced lab-confirmed nanobody binders against six, achieving a 67% target-level success rate with binding affinities reaching the single-digit nanomolar range, without human filtering or intervention. In user studies, experts working with Latent-Y completed design campaigns 56 times faster than independent expert time estimates, compressing weeks of work into hours. Because Latent-X2 is a general-purpose atomic-level model for biologics design, the same agent architecture naturally extends to macrocyclic peptide and mini-binder design campaigns, broadening autonomous discovery across therapeutic modalities. Latent-Y is available to selected partners at https://platform.latentlabs.com.

Reasoning Shift: How Context Silently Shortens LLM Reasoning
Authors: Gleb Rodionov
First: 2026-04-01T17:14:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:14:18+00:00
Comments: Preprint, work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) exhibiting test-time scaling behavior, such as extended reasoning traces and self-verification, have demonstrated remarkable performance on complex, long-term reasoning tasks. However, the robustness of these reasoning behaviors remains underexplored. To investigate this, we conduct a systematic evaluation of multiple reasoning models across three scenarios: (1) problems augmented with lengthy, irrelevant context; (2) multi-turn conversational settings with independent tasks; and (3) problems presented as a subtask within a complex task. We observe an interesting phenomenon: reasoning models tend to produce much shorter reasoning traces (up to 50%) for the same problem under different context conditions compared to the traces produced when the problem is presented in isolation. A finer-grained analysis reveals that this compression is associated with a decrease in self-verification and uncertainty management behaviors, such as double-checking. While this behavioral shift does not compromise performance on straightforward problems, it might affect performance on more challenging tasks. We hope our findings draw additional attention to both the robustness of reasoning models and the problem of context management for LLMs and LLM-based agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理迁移：上下文如何无声地缩短大语言模型的推理过程
表现出测试时扩展行为（如延长推理轨迹和自我验证）的大语言模型（LLMs）在复杂、长期推理任务中展现了卓越性能。然而，这些推理行为的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。为探究此问题，我们对多个推理模型在三种场景下进行了系统评估：（1）添加冗长无关上下文的问题；（2）多轮对话中独立任务的设置；（3）作为复杂任务子任务呈现的问题。我们观察到一个有趣现象：在不同上下文条件下，推理模型对同一问题生成的推理轨迹显著缩短（最多达50%），而当问题独立呈现时则较长。细粒度分析表明，这种压缩与自我验证和不确定性管理行为（如双重检查）的减少相关。尽管该行为转变在简单问题上不影响性能，但在更具挑战性的任务中可能影响表现。我们希望本研究能引起对推理模型鲁棒性及大语言模型和基于LLM代理的上下文管理问题的更多关注。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how contextual changes affect the reasoning behavior of large language models, motivated by the observation that models exhibit test-time scaling behaviors like extended reasoning and self-verification, yet their robustness under varying contexts remains unexamined. The researchers systematically evaluate multiple reasoning models across three context-rich scenarios: irrelevant context augmentation, multi-turn conversational tasks, and subtasks embedded in complex problems. Their findings reveal that reasoning traces are consistently shortened—by up to 50%—when the same problem is presented within context, accompanied by reduced self-verification and uncertainty management behaviors. While performance on simple tasks remains unaffected, this compression may impair performance on harder reasoning tasks, highlighting a critical vulnerability in reasoning robustness and context handling for LLMs.
本研究探讨上下文变化如何影响大语言模型的推理行为，动机源于模型虽表现出测试时扩展推理与自我验证等行为，但其在不同上下文下的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。研究人员系统评估了多个推理模型在三种上下文变化场景下的表现：添加无关长上下文、多轮对话独立任务、以及作为复杂任务子任务呈现。实验发现，模型在有上下文时推理轨迹显著缩短（最高达50%），伴随自我验证和不确定性管理行为减少。这种压缩在简单任务上不影响性能，但在复杂任务中可能削弱表现，凸显了提升大语言模型及代理上下文管理能力的重要性。
SMASH: Mastering Scalable Whole-Body Skills for Humanoid Ping-Pong with Egocentric Vision
Authors: Junli Ren, Yinghui Li, Kai Zhang, Penglin Fu, Haoran Jiang, Yixuan Pan, Guangjun Zeng, Tao Huang, Weizhong Guo, Peng Lu, Tianyu Li, Jingbo Wang, Li Chen, Hongyang Li, Ping Luo
First: 2026-04-01T17:11:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:11:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing humanoid table tennis systems remain limited by their reliance on external sensing and their inability to achieve agile whole-body coordination for precise task execution. These limitations stem from two core challenges: achieving low-latency and robust onboard egocentric perception under fast robot motion, and obtaining sufficiently diverse task-aligned strike motions for learning precise yet natural whole-body behaviors. In this work, we present \methodname, a modular system for agile humanoid table tennis that unifies scalable whole-body skill learning with onboard egocentric perception, eliminating the need for external cameras during deployment. Our work advances prior humanoid table-tennis systems in three key aspects. First, we achieve agile and precise ball interaction with tightly coordinated whole-body control, rather than relying on decoupled upper- and lower-body behaviors. This enables the system to exhibit diverse strike motions, including explosive whole-body smashes and low crouching shots. Second, by augmenting and diversifying strike motions with a generative model, our framework benefits from scalable motion priors and produces natural, robust striking behaviors across a wide workspace. Third, to the best of our knowledge, we demonstrate the first humanoid table-tennis system capable of consecutive strikes using onboard sensing alone, despite the challenges of low-latency perception, ego-motion-induced instability, and limited field of view. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate stable and precise ball exchanges under high-speed conditions, validating scalable, perception-driven whole-body skill learning for dynamic humanoid interaction tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SMASH：基于第一人称视觉的类人机器人乒乓球敏捷全身技能掌握
现有类人机器人乒乓球系统受限于对外部传感的依赖及无法实现敏捷全身协调以精确执行任务。这些限制源于两大核心挑战：在高速机器人运动下实现低延迟且鲁棒的机载第一人称感知，以及获取足够多样且任务对齐的击球动作以学习精确而自然的全身行为。本文提出SMASH，一个模块化敏捷类人机器人乒乓球系统，将可扩展全身技能学习与机载第一人称感知统一，部署时无需外部摄像头。本工作在三个方面推进了现有类人机器人乒乓球系统：首先，通过紧密协调的全身控制实现敏捷精准的球体交互，而非依赖上、下身行为解耦；这使系统能展现多样击球动作，包括爆发式全身扣杀和低蹲击球。其次，通过生成模型扩充和多样化击球动作，框架受益于可扩展运动先验，能在广阔工作空间内生成自然鲁棒的击球行为。第三，据我们所知，本系统首次实现仅依赖机载感知连续击球，尽管面临低延迟感知、自运动诱导的不稳定性及视野受限等挑战。大量真实实验验证了高速条件下稳定精准的球体交换，证实了可扩展、感知驱动的全身技能学习在动态类人交互任务中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing humanoid table tennis systems—namely their dependence on external sensors and lack of agile whole-body coordination—the authors introduce SMASH, a modular system that integrates scalable whole-body skill learning with onboard egocentric perception, eliminating external cameras. The method combines tightly coordinated whole-body control with a generative model to diversify and augment strike motions, enabling natural and robust behaviors across varied workspace conditions. Experimental results show SMASH achieves stable, precise ball exchanges under high-speed conditions, demonstrating for the first time consecutive strikes using only onboard sensing, despite challenges like low-latency perception and motion-induced instability.
为克服现有仿人机器人乒乓球系统依赖外部传感器及缺乏敏捷全身协调的问题，作者提出SMASH系统，将可扩展的全身技能学习与机载第一人称视觉感知相结合，无需外部摄像头。该方法通过紧密协调的全身控制与生成模型增强和多样化击球动作，实现自然稳健的击球行为，覆盖广泛工作空间。实验表明，SMASH在高速条件下实现稳定精准的球交换，首次仅依靠机载感知完成连续击球，克服了低延迟感知、运动诱导不稳定及视野受限等挑战。
Property-Level Flood Risk Assessment Using AI-Enabled Street-View Lowest Floor Elevation Extraction and ML Imputation Across Texas
Authors: Xiangpeng Li, Yu-Hsuan Ho, Sam D Brody, Ali Mostafavi
First: 2026-04-01T17:08:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:08:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper argues that AI-enabled analysis of street-view imagery, complemented by performance-gated machine-learning imputation, provides a viable pathway for generating building-specific elevation data at regional scale for flood risk assessment. We develop and apply a three-stage pipeline across 18 areas of interest (AOIs) in Texas that (1) extracts LFE and the height difference between street grade and the lowest floor (HDSL) from Google Street View imagery using the Elev-Vision framework, (2) imputes missing HDSL values with Random Forest and Gradient Boosting models trained on 16 terrain, hydrologic, geographic, and flood-exposure features, and (3) integrates the resulting elevation dataset with Fathom 1-in-100 year inundation surfaces and USACE depth-damage functions to estimate property-specific interior flood depth and expected loss. Across 12,241 residential structures, street-view imagery was available for 73.4% of parcels and direct LFE/HDSL extraction was successful for 49.0% (5,992 structures). Imputation was retained for 13 AOIs where cross-validated performance was defensible, with selected models achieving R suqre values from 0.159 to 0.974; five AOIs were explicitly excluded from prediction because performance was insufficient. The results show that street-view-based elevation mapping is not universally available for every property, but it is sufficiently scalable to materially improve regional flood-risk characterization by moving beyond hazard exposure to structure-level estimates of interior inundation and expected damage. Scientifically, the study advances LFE estimation from a pilot-scale proof of concept to a regional, end-to-end workflow. Practically, it offers a replicable framework for jurisdictions that lack comprehensive Elevation Certificates but need parcel-level information to support mitigation, planning, and flood-risk management.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用AI赋能的街景最低楼层高程提取与机器学习填补技术，对德克萨斯州进行物业级洪水风险评估
本文认为，结合AI街景图像分析与性能门控机器学习填补方法，可为区域尺度的洪水风险评估生成建筑特定高程数据。我们在德克萨斯州18个研究区域（AOIs）构建并应用三阶段流程：（1）使用Elev-Vision框架从Google街景图像中提取最低楼层高程（LFE）及街道标高与最低楼层高差（HDSL）；（2）利用16个地形、水文、地理及洪水暴露特征训练的随机森林与梯度提升模型填补缺失HDSL值；（3）将所得高程数据与Fathom百年一遇淹没面及美国陆军工程兵团深度-损失函数结合，估算物业内部洪水深度与预期损失。在12,241处住宅中，73.4%地块有街景图像，其中49.0%（5,992处）成功直接提取LFE/HDSL。13个AOI因交叉验证性能可接受而保留填补，所选模型R平方值介于0.159至0.974；5个AOI因性能不足被排除。结果表明，街景高程测绘并非所有物业均可用，但其具备足够可扩展性，可显著提升区域洪水风险表征，从灾害暴露转向结构级内部淹没与预期损失评估。科学上，该研究将LFE估算从试点概念推进至区域端到端工作流；实践上，为缺乏完整高程证书但需地块级信息以支持减灾、规划与洪水风险管理的地区提供可复制框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, building-specific flood risk data in regions lacking elevation certificates, this study leverages AI to extract lowest floor elevations (LFE) from Google Street View imagery using the Elev-Vision framework, then imputes missing values via machine learning models trained on geographic and hydrologic features. Applied across 18 Texas AOIs, the method successfully extracted LFE/HDSL for 49% of 12,241 residential structures, with imputation retained in 13 AOIs where models achieved R² values from 0.159 to 0.974; five AOIs were excluded due to poor performance. The integrated flood depth and damage estimates demonstrate that while street-view data coverage is incomplete, the approach significantly enhances regional flood risk characterization by enabling structure-level inundation and loss projections.
为解决缺乏高程证书地区难以获取建筑级洪水风险数据的问题，本研究利用AI从谷歌街景图像中提取最低楼层高程（LFE），并结合机器学习模型对缺失值进行填补。在德克萨斯州18个区域中，该方法成功提取了12,241栋住宅中49%的LFE/HDSL数据，其中13个区域因模型表现良好（R²值0.159至0.974）保留了填补结果，另有5个区域因表现不足被排除。最终结果表明，尽管街景数据覆盖不全，该方法仍能显著提升区域洪水风险评估能力，实现建筑内部淹没深度与预期损失的结构级预测。
Brainstacks: Cross-Domain Cognitive Capabilities via Frozen MoE-LoRA Stacks for Continual LLM Learning
Authors: Mohammad R. Abu Ayyash
First: 2026-04-01T17:08:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:08:25+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 13 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Brainstacks, a modular architecture for continual multi-domain fine-tuning of large language models that packages domain expertise as frozen adapter stacks composing additively on a shared frozen base at inference. Five interlocking components: (1) MoE-LoRA with Shazeer-style noisy top-2 routing across all seven transformer projections under QLoRA 4-bit quantization with rsLoRA scaling; (2) an inner loop performing residual boosting by freezing trained stacks and adding new ones; (3) an outer loop training sequential domain-specific stacks with curriculum-ordered dependencies; (4) null-space projection via randomized SVD constraining new stacks to subspaces orthogonal to prior directions, achieving zero forgetting in isolation; (5) an outcome-based sigmoid meta-router trained on empirically discovered domain-combination targets that selectively weights stacks, enabling cross-domain composition. Two boundary experiments: (6) PSN pretraining on a randomly initialized model; (7) per-domain RL (DPO/GRPO) validating compatibility with post-SFT alignment. Validated on TinyLlama-1.1B (4 domains, 9 stacks) and Gemma 3 12B IT (5 domains, 10 stacks), MoE-LoRA achieves 2.5x faster convergence than parameter-matched single LoRA, residual boosting breaks through the single-stack ceiling, and the routed system recovers generation quality destroyed by ungated stack accumulation. The central finding: the outcome-based router discovers that domain stacks encode transferable cognitive primitives (instruction-following clarity, numerical reasoning, procedural logic, chain-of-thought structure) rather than domain-specific knowledge, with medical prompts routing to chat+math stacks in 97% of cases despite zero medical data in those stacks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Brainstacks：通过冻结MoE-LoRA堆栈实现跨域认知能力的持续LLM学习
我们提出了Brainstacks，一种用于持续多域微调大语言模型的模块化架构，该架构将领域专业知识封装为冻结的适配器堆栈，在推理时叠加于共享的冻结基础之上。包含五个相互关联的组件：(1) 在QLoRA 4位量化与rsLoRA缩放下，采用Shazeer风格的噪声Top-2路由机制的MoE-LoRA，覆盖所有七个Transformer投影；(2) 内循环通过冻结已训练堆栈并添加新堆栈实现残差增强；(3) 外循环按课程顺序训练具有依赖关系的领域特定堆栈序列；(4) 通过随机SVD实现零空间投影，将新堆栈约束在与先前方向正交的子空间中，从而在隔离条件下实现零遗忘；(5) 一种基于结果的Sigmoid元路由器，通过实证发现的领域组合目标进行训练，选择性加权堆栈，实现跨域组合。两个边界实验：(6) 在随机初始化模型上进行PSN预训练；(7) 每域强化学习（DPO/GRPO），验证其与后SFT对齐的兼容性。在TinyLlama-1.1B（4个域，9个堆栈）和Gemma 3 12B IT（5个域，10个堆栈）上验证，MoE-LoRA比参数匹配的单LoRA快2.5倍，残差增强突破单堆栈上限，路由系统恢复了因未门控堆栈累积而破坏的生成质量。核心发现：基于结果的路由器发现，领域堆栈编码的是可迁移的认知原语（指令遵循清晰度、数值推理、程序逻辑、思维链结构），而非领域特定知识，即使堆栈中无任何医学数据，医学提示仍97%路由至聊天+数学堆栈。
Summary / 总结
We present Brainstacks, a modular architecture for continual multi-domain fine-tuning of large language models that packages domain expertise as frozen adapter stacks composing additively on a shared frozen base at inference.
Detecting Multi-Agent Collusion Through Multi-Agent Interpretability
Authors: Aaron Rose, Carissa Cullen, Brandon Gary Kaplowitz, Christian Schroeder de Witt
First: 2026-04-01T17:08:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:08:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As LLM agents are increasingly deployed in multi-agent systems, they introduce risks of covert coordination that may evade standard forms of human oversight. While linear probes on model activations have shown promise for detecting deception in single-agent settings, collusion is inherently a multi-agent phenomenon, and the use of internal representations for detecting collusion between agents remains unexplored. We introduce NARCBench, a benchmark for evaluating collusion detection under environment distribution shift, and propose five probing techniques that aggregate per-agent deception scores to classify scenarios at the group level. Our probes achieve 1.00 AUROC in-distribution and 0.60--0.86 AUROC when transferred zero-shot to structurally different multi-agent scenarios and a steganographic blackjack card-counting task. We find that no single probing technique dominates across all collusion types, suggesting that different forms of collusion manifest differently in activation space. We also find preliminary evidence that this signal is localised at the token level, with the colluding agent's activations spiking specifically when processing the encoded parts of their partner's message. This work takes a step toward multi-agent interpretability: extending white-box inspection from single models to multi-agent contexts, where detection requires aggregating signals across agents. These results suggest that model internals provide a complementary signal to text-level monitoring for detecting multi-agent collusion, particularly for organisations with access to model activations. Code and data are available at https://github.com/aaronrose227/narcbench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多智能体可解释性检测多智能体串通
随着大语言模型智能体在多智能体系统中的部署日益增多，它们可能引入隐蔽协作的风险，而此类风险可能逃避标准的人类监督形式。虽然在单智能体设置中，对模型激活的线性探针已被证明能有效检测欺骗行为，但串通本质上是多智能体现象，利用内部表征检测智能体间的串通尚未被探索。我们提出了NARCBench，一个用于评估在环境分布偏移下串通检测能力的基准，并提出了五种探针技术，通过聚合各智能体的欺骗得分对群体场景进行分类。我们的探针在分布内达到1.00 AUROC，在零样本迁移至结构不同的多智能体场景及隐写式二十一点计牌任务时，AUROC为0.60至0.86。我们发现，没有单一探针技术能主导所有串通类型，表明不同形式的串通在激活空间中表现各异。我们还发现初步证据表明，该信号在词元级别局部化，当串通智能体处理其伙伴消息的编码部分时，其激活值会显著升高。本工作迈出了多智能体可解释性的重要一步：将白盒检查从单模型扩展至多智能体情境，其中检测需要跨智能体聚合信号。这些结果表明，模型内部表征为检测多智能体串通提供了与文本级监控互补的信号，尤其对能访问模型激活的组织而言。代码和数据见https://github.com/aaronrose227/narcbench。
Summary / 总结
As large language model agents are deployed in multi-agent systems, covert collusion poses a risk that standard human oversight may miss. The authors introduce NARCBench, a benchmark for collusion detection under distribution shifts, and propose five probing techniques that aggregate per-agent deception scores to classify group-level collusion. These probes achieve perfect AUROC (1.00) in-distribution and 0.60–0.86 AUROC in zero-shot transfers to structurally different environments and a steganographic blackjack task. No single probe consistently outperforms others across collusion types, indicating varied activation patterns for different collusion forms. Evidence also suggests token-level localization of collusion signals, with colluding agents showing activation spikes when processing their partner’s encoded messages. This work advances multi-agent interpretability by extending white-box analysis to multi-agent contexts, demonstrating that internal model activations offer a valuable, complementary signal to text-level monitoring for detecting collusion.
为应对LLM多智能体系统中隐蔽合谋带来的风险，该研究提出NARCBench基准，用于评估在分布偏移下合谋检测能力，并设计五种聚合各智能体欺骗分数的探针方法以识别群体级合谋。实验表明，这些探针在分布内达到1.00 AUROC，在零样本迁移至结构不同环境及隐写式二十一点任务时AUROC为0.60–0.86。结果表明无单一探针方法在所有合谋类型上最优，暗示合谋在激活空间中表现形式各异，且初步证据显示合谋信号在词元级别局部化，合谋智能体在处理伙伴消息编码部分时激活显著升高。
Automated Generation of High-Quality Bug Reports for Android Applications
Authors: Antu Saha, Atish Kumar Dipongkor, Sam Bennett, Kevin Moran, Andrian Marcus, Oscar Chaparro
First: 2026-04-01T17:05:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T17:05:11+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most defects in mobile applications are visually observable on the device screen. To track these defects, users, testers, and developers must manually submit bug reports, especially in the absence of crashes. However, these reports are frequently ambiguous or inaccurate, often omitting essential components such as the Observed Behavior (OB), Expected Behavior (EB), or Steps to Reproduce (S2Rs). Low-quality reports hinder developers' ability to understand and reproduce defects, delaying resolution and leading to incorrect or unresolvable fixes. In this paper, we posit that providing specific app-related information (e.g., GUI interactions or specific screens where bugs appear) to LLMs as key points of context can assist in automatically generating clear, detailed, and accurate OB, EB, and S2Rs. We built and evaluated a novel approach, BugScribe, that generates bug reports in this way. To support the evaluation, we introduce a unified quality framework that defines correctness and completeness dimensions for OB, EB, and S2Rs. Using 48 bug reports from 26 Android apps, we show that BugScribe produces higher-quality and more accurate components than the original reports and outperforms recent LLM-based baselines. We envision that BugScribe can serve as a practical assistant for testers and developers by enhancing incomplete bug reports with reliable and accurate OB, EB, and S2Rs, thereby streamlining bug resolution and improving mobile app quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向Android应用的高质量缺陷报告自动生成
移动应用中的大多数缺陷均可在设备屏幕上直观观察。为追踪这些缺陷，用户、测试人员和开发者需手动提交缺陷报告，尤其在无崩溃情况下。然而，这些报告常模糊或不准确，常遗漏关键要素如观察到的行为（OB）、预期行为（EB）或重现步骤（S2Rs）。低质量报告阻碍开发者理解与重现缺陷，延缓修复并导致错误或无法解决的修复方案。本文提出，将特定应用相关信息（如GUI交互或缺陷出现的具体界面）作为上下文关键点提供给大语言模型（LLM），可辅助自动生成清晰、详细且准确的OB、EB和S2Rs。我们构建并评估了名为BugScribe的新型方法，以实现此目标。为支持评估，我们引入统一质量框架，定义OB、EB和S2Rs的正确性与完整性维度。基于26个Android应用的48份缺陷报告，我们证明BugScribe生成的组件质量更高、更准确，优于近期基于LLM的基线方法。我们期望BugScribe可作为测试人员和开发者的实用助手，通过补充可靠且准确的OB、EB和S2Rs，完善不完整报告，从而加速缺陷修复并提升移动应用质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the frequent incompleteness and ambiguity of manually submitted bug reports for Android apps, especially when no crashes occur, this paper proposes BugScribe, a method that leverages LLMs with app-specific context—such as GUI interactions and screen states—to automatically generate high-quality Observed Behavior, Expected Behavior, and Steps to Reproduce. Evaluated using a unified quality framework across 48 reports from 26 apps, BugScribe consistently outperforms existing LLM-based baselines in both correctness and completeness, producing more accurate and actionable bug reports that aid developers in faster, more reliable defect resolution.
鉴于手动提交的安卓应用缺陷报告常因缺失关键信息（如观察行为、预期行为或重现步骤）而模糊不清，本文提出BugScribe方法，通过向大语言模型提供应用界面交互与屏幕状态等上下文信息，自动生成高质量的缺陷报告组件。在26个应用的48份报告上评估，BugScribe在正确性和完整性维度均优于现有大模型基线方法，生成的报告更准确、更具可操作性，有助于开发者更高效、更准确地定位和修复缺陷。
Deep Reinforcement Learning for Robotic Manipulation under Distribution Shift with Bounded Extremum Seeking
Authors: Shaifalee Saxena, Rafael Fierro, Alexander Scheinker
First: 2026-04-01T16:59:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:59:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning has shown strong performance in robotic manipulation, but learned policies often degrade in performance when test conditions differ from the training distribution. This limitation is especially important in contact-rich tasks such as pushing and pick-and-place, where changes in goals, contact conditions, or robot dynamics can drive the system out-of-distribution at inference time. In this paper, we investigate a hybrid controller that combines reinforcement learning with bounded extremum seeking to improve robustness under such conditions. In the proposed approach, deep deterministic policy gradient (DDPG) policies are trained under standard conditions on the robotic pushing and pick-and-place tasks, and are then combined with bounded ES during deployment. The RL policy provides fast manipulation behavior, while bounded ES ensures robustness of the overall controller to time variations when operating conditions depart from those seen during training. The resulting controller is evaluated under several out-of-distribution settings, including time-varying goals and spatially varying friction patches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在分布偏移条件下使用有界极值搜索的深度强化学习用于机器人操作
强化学习在机器人操作中表现出色，但学习到的策略在测试条件与训练分布不同时性能常会下降。这一局限在接触密集型任务（如推动物体和抓取放置）中尤为关键，因为目标变化、接触条件或机器人动力学的变化可能导致系统在推理时处于分布外。本文研究了一种结合强化学习与有界极值搜索的混合控制器，以提升此类条件下的鲁棒性。该方法中，深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）策略在标准条件下训练用于机器人推动物体和抓取放置任务，部署时再与有界极值搜索结合。强化学习策略提供快速操作行为，而有界极值搜索确保整体控制器在操作条件偏离训练条件时对时间变化的鲁棒性。所提出的控制器在多种分布外场景下进行了评估，包括时变目标和空间变化摩擦区域。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of reinforcement learning policies under distribution shifts in robotic manipulation tasks, this work proposes a hybrid controller combining deep deterministic policy gradient (DDPG) with bounded extremum seeking to enhance robustness. The DDPG policy is trained under standard conditions for pushing and pick-and-place tasks, then augmented with bounded ES during deployment to handle time-varying goals and spatially varying friction. Experimental results show that this hybrid approach maintains stable performance under out-of-distribution conditions where standard RL policies fail.
为了解决强化学习策略在机器人操作任务中因分布偏移导致性能下降的问题，本文提出一种结合深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）与有界极值搜索（ES）的混合控制器。DDPG策略在标准条件下训练用于推和抓取任务，部署时叠加有界ES以应对目标随时间变化和摩擦区域空间变化等分布外情况。实验表明，该混合控制器在分布外条件下表现稳定，而标准RL策略则失效。
Looking into a Pixel by Nonlinear Unmixing -- A Generative Approach
Authors: Maofeng Tang, Hairong Qi
First: 2026-04-01T16:58:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:58:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Due to the large footprint of pixels in remote sensing imagery, hyperspectral unmixing (HU) has become an important and necessary procedure in hyperspectral image analysis. Traditional HU methods rely on a prior spectral mixing model, especially for nonlinear mixtures, which has largely limited the performance and generalization capacity of the unmixing approach. In this paper, we address the challenging problem of hyperspectral nonlinear unmixing (HNU) without explicit knowledge of the mixing model. Inspired by the principle of generative models, where images of the same distribution can be generated as that of the training images without knowing the exact probability distribution function of the image, we develop an invertible mixing-unmixing process via a bi-directional GAN framework, constrained by both the cycle consistency and the linkage between linear and nonlinear mixtures. The combination of cycle consistency and linear linkage provides powerful constraints without requiring an explicit mixing model. We refer to the proposed approach as the linearly-constrained CycleGAN unmixing net, or LCGU net. Experimental results indicate that the proposed LCGU net exhibits stable and competitive performance across different datasets compared with other state-of-the-art model-based HNU methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过非线性解混窥探像素——一种生成式方法
由于遥感影像中像素的较大覆盖范围，高光谱解混（HU）已成为高光谱图像分析中重要且必要的步骤。传统HU方法依赖先验光谱混合模型，尤其对于非线性混合情况，这在很大程度上限制了解混方法的性能和泛化能力。本文针对在无明确混合模型知识的情况下解决高光谱非线性解混（HNU）这一挑战性问题。受生成模型原理启发，即无需知道图像的确切概率分布函数，即可生成与训练图像分布相同的图像，我们通过双向GAN框架开发了一种可逆的混合-解混过程，该过程受循环一致性约束以及线性与非线性混合之间的关联约束。循环一致性与线性关联的结合提供了强大的约束，而无需显式混合模型。我们将该方法称为线性约束循环GAN解混网络（LCGU网络）。实验结果表明，所提出的LCGU网络在不同数据集上均表现出稳定且具有竞争力的性能，优于其他最先进的基于模型的HNU方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional hyperspectral unmixing methods that rely on explicit spectral mixing models, especially for nonlinear mixtures, this paper proposes a generative approach to address hyperspectral nonlinear unmixing without prior knowledge of the mixing model. The method employs a bi-directional GAN framework, termed LCGU net, which enforces cycle consistency and links linear and nonlinear mixing processes to provide strong constraints without requiring explicit modeling. Experimental results demonstrate that LCGU net achieves stable and competitive performance across multiple datasets, outperforming or matching state-of-the-art model-based methods in nonlinear unmixing tasks.
由于传统高光谱解混方法依赖于明确的光谱混合模型，尤其在非线性混合场景下性能受限，本文提出一种无需显式混合模型的生成式方法解决高光谱非线性解混问题。该方法构建双向GAN框架——线性约束循环GAN解混网络（LCGU net），通过循环一致性约束及线性与非线性混合过程的关联，提供强约束而无需显式建模。实验表明，LCGU net在多个数据集上表现稳定且竞争力强，优于或媲美当前主流的基于模型的非线性解混方法。
ActErase: A Training-Free Paradigm for Precise Concept Erasure via Activation Redirection
Authors: Yi Sun, Xinhao Zhong, Hongyan Li, Yimin Zhou, Junhao Li, Bin Chen, Xuan Wang
First: 2026-01-01T09:11:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:54:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in text-to-image diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable generation capabilities, yet they raise significant concerns regarding safety, copyright, and ethical implications. Existing concept erasure methods address these risks by removing sensitive concepts from pre-trained models, but most of them rely on data-intensive and computationally expensive fine-tuning, which poses a critical limitation. To overcome these challenges, inspired by the observation that the model's activations are predominantly composed of generic concepts, with only a minimal component can represent the target concept, we propose a novel training-free method (ActErase) for efficient concept erasure. Specifically, the proposed method operates by identifying activation difference regions via prompt-pair analysis, extracting target activations and dynamically replacing input activations during forward passes. Comprehensive evaluations across three critical erasure tasks (nudity, artistic style, and object removal) demonstrates that our training-free method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) erasure performance, while effectively preserving the model's overall generative capability. Our approach also exhibits strong robustness against adversarial attacks, establishing a new plug-and-play paradigm for lightweight yet effective concept manipulation in diffusion models.

标题：ActErase：一种通过激活重定向实现精准概念擦除的无训练范式
文本到图像扩散模型的最新进展展示了卓越的生成能力，但同时也引发了关于安全、版权和伦理问题的担忧。现有概念擦除方法通过从预训练模型中移除敏感概念来应对这些风险，但大多数方法依赖数据密集且计算成本高昂的微调，存在关键限制。为克服这些挑战，我们受模型激活主要由通用概念组成、仅少量成分可表示目标概念的观察启发，提出了一种新颖的无训练方法（ActErase）以高效实现概念擦除。具体而言，该方法通过提示对分析识别激活差异区域，提取目标激活并在前向传播过程中动态替换输入激活。在三个关键擦除任务（裸露、艺术风格、物体移除）的全面评估中，我们的无训练方法实现了最先进的擦除性能，同时有效保持模型整体生成能力。该方法还表现出对对抗攻击的强鲁棒性，建立了一种轻量且有效的扩散模型概念操作的即插即用新范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe and efficient concept erasure in text-to-image diffusion models without costly fine-tuning, ActErase introduces a training-free method that redirects activations to suppress target concepts. It identifies activation difference regions through prompt-pair analysis, extracts the target concept’s activations, and dynamically replaces them during inference. Experimental results across three tasks—nudity removal, artistic style erasure, and object deletion—show that ActErase achieves state-of-the-art performance while preserving overall generative quality and resisting adversarial attacks, offering a lightweight, plug-and-play solution.
为在无需训练的情况下高效实现扩散模型中的概念擦除，ActErase提出一种通过激活重定向的训练免方法。该方法通过提示对分析识别激活差异区域，提取目标概念激活并动态替换前向传播中的输入激活。在裸露、艺术风格和物体移除三项任务中，实验表明该方法达到最先进性能，同时保持模型整体生成能力，并具备对抗攻击的鲁棒性，提供轻量级、即插即用的概念操控方案。
VRUD: A Drone Dataset for Complex Vehicle-VRU Interactions within Mixed Traffic
Authors: Ziyu Wang, Hongrui Kou, Cheng Wang, Ruochen Li, Hubert P. H. Shum, Amir Atapour-Abarghouei, Yuxin Zhang
First: 2026-04-01T16:53:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:53:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The Operational Design Domain (ODD) of urbanoriented Level 4 (L4) autonomous driving, especially for autonomous robotaxis, confronts formidable challenges in complex urban mixed traffic environments. These challenges stem mainly from the high density of Vulnerable Road Users (VRUs) and their highly uncertain and unpredictable interaction behaviors. However, existing open-source datasets predominantly focus on structured scenarios such as highways or regulated intersections, leaving a critical gap in data representing chaotic, unstructured urban environments. To address this, this paper proposes an efficient, high-precision method for constructing drone-based datasets and establishes the Vehicle-Vulnerable Road User Interaction Dataset (VRUD), as illustrated in Figure 1. Distinct from prior works, VRUD is collected from typical "Urban Villages" in Shenzhen, characterized by loose traffic supervision and extreme occlusion. The dataset comprises 4 hours of 4K/30Hz recording, containing 11,479 VRU trajectories and 1,939 vehicle trajectories. A key characteristic of VRUD is its composition: VRUs account for about 87% of all traffic participants, significantly exceeding the proportions in existing benchmarks. Furthermore, unlike datasets that only provide raw trajectories, we extracted 4,002 multi-agent interaction scenarios based on a novel Vector Time to Collision (VTTC) threshold, supported by standard OpenDRIVE HD maps. This study provides valuable, rare edge-case resources for enhancing the safety performance of ADS in complex, unstructured urban environments. To facilitate further research, we have made the VRUD dataset open-source at: https://zzi4.github.io/VRUD/.

Toward Personalized Darts Training: A Data-Driven Framework Based on Skeleton-Based Biomechanical Analysis and Motion Modeling
Authors: Zhantao Chen, Dongyi He, Jin Fang, Xi Chen, Yisuo Liu, Xiaozhen Zhong, Xuejun Hu
First: 2026-04-01T16:51:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:51:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As sports training becomes more data-driven, traditional dart coaching based mainly on experience and visual observation is increasingly inadequate for high-precision, goal-oriented movements. Although prior studies have highlighted the importance of release parameters, joint motion, and coordination in dart throwing, most quantitative methods still focus on local variables, single-release metrics, or static template matching. These approaches offer limited support for personalized training and often overlook useful movement variability. This paper presents a data-driven dart training assistance system. The system creates a closed-loop framework spanning motion capture, feature modeling, and personalized feedback. Dart-throwing data were collected in markerless conditions using a Kinect 2.0 depth sensor and an optical camera. Eighteen kinematic features were extracted from four biomechanical dimensions: three-link coordination, release velocity, multi-joint angular configuration, and postural stability. Two modules were developed: a personalized optimal throwing trajectory model that combines historical high-quality samples with the minimum jerk criterion, and a motion deviation diagnosis and recommendation model based on z-scores and hierarchical logic. A total of 2,396 throwing samples from professional and non-professional athletes were collected. Results show that the system generates smooth personalized reference trajectories consistent with natural human movement. Case studies indicate that it can detect poor trunk stability, abnormal elbow displacement, and imbalanced velocity control, then provide targeted recommendations. The framework shifts dart evaluation from deviation from a uniform standard to deviation from an individual's optimal control range, improving personalization and interpretability for darts training and other high-precision target sports.

ReinDriveGen: Reinforcement Post-Training for Out-of-Distribution Driving Scene Generation
Authors: Hao Zhang, Lue Fan, Weikang Bian, Zehuan Wu, Lewei Lu, Zhaoxiang Zhang, Hongsheng Li
First: 2026-04-01T16:48:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:48:20+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://drive-sim.github.io/ReinDriveGen/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present ReinDriveGen, a framework that enables full controllability over dynamic driving scenes, allowing users to freely edit actor trajectories to simulate safety-critical corner cases such as front-vehicle collisions, drifting cars, vehicles spinning out of control, pedestrians jaywalking, and cyclists cutting across lanes. Our approach constructs a dynamic 3D point cloud scene from multi-frame LiDAR data, introduces a vehicle completion module to reconstruct full 360° geometry from partial observations, and renders the edited scene into 2D condition images that guide a video diffusion model to synthesize realistic driving videos. Since such edited scenarios inevitably fall outside the training distribution, we further propose an RL-based post-training strategy with a pairwise preference model and a pairwise reward mechanism, enabling robust quality improvement under out-of-distribution conditions without ground-truth supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReinDriveGen outperforms existing approaches on edited driving scenarios and achieves state-of-the-art results on novel ego viewpoint synthesis.

Paper Reconstruction Evaluation: Evaluating Presentation and Hallucination in AI-written Papers
Authors: Atsuyuki Miyai, Mashiro Toyooka, Zaiying Zhao, Kenta Watanabe, Toshihiko Yamasaki, Kiyoharu Aizawa
First: 2026-04-01T16:48:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:48:04+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://agent4science-utokyo.github.io/PaperRecon_HP/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
This paper introduces the first systematic evaluation framework for quantifying the quality and risks of papers written by modern coding agents. While AI-driven paper writing has become a growing concern, rigorous evaluation of the quality and potential risks of AI-written papers remains limited, and a unified understanding of their reliability is still lacking. We introduce Paper Reconstruction Evaluation (PaperRecon), an evaluation framework in which an overview (overview.md) is created from an existing paper, after which an agent generates a full paper based on the overview and minimal additional resources, and the result is subsequently compared against the original paper. PaperRecon disentangles the evaluation of the AI-written papers into two orthogonal dimensions, Presentation and Hallucination, where Presentation is evaluated using a rubric and Hallucination is assessed via agentic evaluation grounded in the original paper source. For evaluation, we introduce PaperWrite-Bench, a benchmark of 51 papers from top-tier venues across diverse domains published after 2025. Our experiments reveal a clear trade-off: while both ClaudeCode and Codex improve with model advances, ClaudeCode achieves higher presentation quality at the cost of more than 10 hallucinations per paper on average, whereas Codex produces fewer hallucinations but lower presentation quality. This work takes a first step toward establishing evaluation frameworks for AI-driven paper writing and improving the understanding of its risks within the research community.
中文标题/摘要
标题：论文重构评估：评估AI撰写论文的呈现质量与幻觉风险
本文提出了首个系统性评估框架，用于量化现代编码代理所撰写论文的质量与风险。尽管AI驱动的论文写作日益引起关注，但对其质量与潜在风险的严格评估仍显不足，学术界对其可靠性缺乏统一理解。我们引入论文重构评估（PaperRecon）框架：首先从现有论文生成概要（overview.md），随后由代理基于该概要及少量附加资源生成完整论文，并与原始论文进行对比。PaperRecon将AI论文评估分解为两个正交维度：呈现质量与幻觉风险，其中呈现质量通过评分标准评估，幻觉风险则基于原始论文源进行代理评估。为评估，我们构建了PaperWrite-Bench基准，包含2025年后来自顶级会议的51篇跨领域论文。实验表明存在明显权衡：尽管ClaudeCode与Codex均随模型进步而提升，但ClaudeCode呈现质量更高，平均每篇论文幻觉数量超过10个；而Codex幻觉更少，但呈现质量较低。本工作首次为AI驱动论文写作建立评估框架，并推动研究界对其风险的理解。
