Using PaperWrite-Bench, a benchmark of 51 recent top-tier papers, experiments show that while both ClaudeCode and Codex improve with model updates, ClaudeCode delivers superior presentation but introduces over 10 hallucinations per paper on average, whereas Codex produces fewer hallucinations at the expense of presentation quality.
鉴于AI写作在学术领域的兴起及缺乏统一评估标准，本文提出PaperRecon框架，通过从摘要重建全文并与原文对比来评估AI论文质量，将评估拆分为“呈现质量”（通过评分量表）和“幻觉内容”（通过源材料锚定评估）两个维度。基于包含51篇2025年后发表的顶级会议论文的PaperWrite-Bench基准，实验表明，尽管ClaudeCode与Codex均随模型进步而提升，ClaudeCode呈现质量更高但平均每篇幻觉超10处，而Codex幻觉更少但呈现质量较低。
When Agents Persuade: Rhetoric Generation and Mitigation in LLMs
Authors: Julia Jose, Ritik Roongta, Rachel Greenstadt
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-04T21:56:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:44:39+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the ICLR 2026 Workshop on Agents in the Wild (AgentWild). 20 pages including appendix, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite their wide-ranging benefits, LLM-based agents deployed in open environments can be exploited to produce manipulative material. In this study, we task LLMs with propaganda objectives and analyze their outputs using two domain-specific models: one that classifies text as propaganda or non-propaganda, and another that detects rhetorical techniques of propaganda (e.g., loaded language, appeals to fear, flag-waving, name-calling). Our findings show that, when prompted, LLMs exhibit propagandistic behaviors and use a variety of rhetorical techniques in doing so. We also explore mitigation via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), and ORPO (Odds Ratio Preference Optimization). We find that fine-tuning significantly reduces their tendency to generate such content, with ORPO proving most effective.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当代理说服时：大语言模型中的修辞生成与抑制
尽管大语言模型代理在开放环境中具有广泛益处，但它们可能被利用生成具有操纵性的内容。本研究要求大语言模型执行宣传目标，并使用两个领域特定模型分析其输出：一个用于分类文本为宣传或非宣传，另一个用于检测宣传中的修辞技巧（如情绪化语言、恐惧诉求、挥舞旗帜、人身攻击）。研究发现，当被提示时，大语言模型会表现出宣传行为，并使用多种修辞技巧。我们还通过监督微调（SFT）、直接偏好优化（DPO）和ORPO（几率比偏好优化）探索抑制方法。结果表明，微调显著降低了其生成此类内容的倾向，其中ORPO效果最佳。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that LLM-based agents in open environments may generate manipulative content, this study prompts LLMs to produce propaganda and evaluates outputs using two specialized models: one for classifying propaganda versus non-propaganda, and another for detecting rhetorical techniques like loaded language or fear appeals. Results show LLMs readily generate propagandistic text using diverse rhetorical strategies. Mitigation experiments using Supervised Fine-Tuning, DPO, and ORPO reveal that fine-tuning substantially reduces such outputs, with ORPO demonstrating the strongest performance.
鉴于在开放环境中部署的基于LLM的代理可能生成具有操纵性的内容，本研究通过提示LLM生成宣传材料，并使用两个专用模型进行评估：一个用于区分宣传与非宣传文本，另一个用于识别如情感化语言或恐惧诉求等修辞手法。实验结果表明，LLM在被提示时会主动生成宣传性内容并运用多种修辞策略。通过监督微调、DPO和ORPO三种方法进行缓解，发现微调能显著降低此类内容生成，其中ORPO效果最佳。
But what is your honest answer? Aiding LLM-judges with honest alternatives using steering vectors
Authors: Leon Eshuijs, Archie Chaudhury, Alan McBeth, Ethan Nguyen
First: 2025-05-23T11:34:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:42:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-as-a-judge is widely used as a scalable substitute for human evaluation, yet current approaches rely on black-box access and struggle to detect subtle dishonesty, such as sycophancy and manipulation. We introduce Judge Using Safety-Steered Alternatives (JUSSA), a framework that leverages a model's internal representations to optimize an honesty-promoting steering vector from a single training example, generating contrastive alternatives that give judges a reference point for detecting dishonesty. We test JUSSA on a novel manipulation benchmark with human-validated response pairs at varying dishonesty levels, finding AUROC improvements across both GPT-4.1 (0.893 $\to$ 0.946) and Claude Haiku (0.859 $\to$ 0.929) judges, though performance degrades when task complexity is mismatched to judge capability, suggesting contrastive evaluation helps most when the task is challenging but within the judge's reach. Layer-wise analysis further shows that steering is most effective in middle layers, where model representations begin to diverge between honest and dishonest prompt processing. Our work demonstrates that steering vectors can serve as tools for evaluation rather than for improving model outputs at inference, opening a new direction for thorough white-box auditing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：但你的诚实答案是什么？利用引导向量辅助LLM法官识别诚实替代方案
LLM作为法官被广泛用作可扩展的人类评估替代方案，但现有方法依赖黑盒访问且难以检测微妙的不诚实行为，如阿谀奉承和操纵。我们提出了“使用安全引导替代方案的法官”（JUSSA）框架，该框架利用模型内部表示，从单个训练样本中优化一个促进诚实的引导向量，生成对比性替代方案，为法官提供检测不诚实行为的参照点。我们在一个新颖的操纵基准上测试JUSSA，该基准包含经人类验证、不同不诚实程度的响应对，结果在GPT-4.1（0.893 → 0.946）和Claude Haiku（0.859 → 0.929）法官上均提升AUROC，但当任务复杂度与法官能力不匹配时性能下降，表明对比评估在任务具有挑战性但仍在法官能力范围内的场景下效果最佳。分层分析进一步表明，引导在中间层最有效，因为模型在诚实与不诚实提示处理之间表示开始分化。我们的工作表明，引导向量可作为评估工具而非用于改进推理输出，开辟了全面白盒审计的新方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current LLM-judges in detecting subtle dishonesty like sycophancy, the authors propose JUSSA, a framework that uses a model’s internal representations to generate honest alternatives via a steering vector optimized from a single training example. This enables judges to compare responses against contrastive, truthful counterparts, improving their ability to detect manipulation. Experiments on a human-validated manipulation benchmark show significant AUROC gains for both GPT-4.1 and Claude Haiku, rising from 0.893 to 0.946 and 0.859 to 0.929 respectively, though performance drops when task complexity exceeds judge capacity. Layer-wise analysis indicates steering is most effective in middle layers, where honest and dishonest representations diverge, suggesting steering vectors can enhance evaluation rather than model generation.
为了解决当前黑盒LLM裁判难以识别如阿谀奉承等微妙不诚实行为的问题，该论文提出JUSSA框架，利用模型内部表示从单个训练样本优化诚实导向的引导向量，生成对比性替代回答以辅助裁判判断。在人类验证的操纵性基准测试中，GPT-4.1和Claude Haiku的AUROC分别从0.893提升至0.946和0.859提升至0.929，但当任务复杂度超出裁判能力时表现下降。层分析显示，引导向量在中间层最有效，因为诚实与不诚实提示处理在此层开始分化。
Lightweight Prompt-Guided CLIP Adaptation for Monocular Depth Estimation
Authors: Reyhaneh Ahani Manghotay, Jie Liang
First: 2026-04-01T16:41:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:41:04+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Leveraging the rich semantic features of vision-language models (VLMs) like CLIP for monocular depth estimation tasks is a promising direction, yet often requires extensive fine-tuning or lacks geometric precision. We present a parameter-efficient framework, named MoA-DepthCLIP, that adapts pretrained CLIP representations for monocular depth estimation with minimal supervision. Our method integrates a lightweight Mixture-of-Adapters (MoA) module into the pretrained Vision Transformer (ViT-B/32) backbone combined with selective fine-tuning of the final layers. This design enables spatially-aware adaptation, guided by a global semantic context vector and a hybrid prediction architecture that synergizes depth bin classification with direct regression. To enhance structural accuracy, we employ a composite loss function that enforces geometric constraints. On the NYU Depth V2 benchmark, MoA-DepthCLIP achieves competitive results, significantly outperforming the DepthCLIP baseline by improving the $δ_1$ accuracy from 0.390 to 0.745 and reducing the RMSE from 1.176 to 0.520. These results are achieved while requiring substantially few trainable parameters, demonstrating that lightweight, prompt-guided MoA is a highly effective strategy for transferring VLM knowledge to fine-grained monocular depth estimation tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：轻量级提示引导的CLIP适配用于单目深度估计
利用视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）丰富的语义特征进行单目深度估计是一个有前景的方向，但通常需要大量微调或缺乏几何精度。我们提出了一种参数高效的框架MoA-DepthCLIP，通过最小监督将预训练的CLIP表示适配于单目深度估计。该方法在预训练视觉Transformer（ViT-B/32）主干网络中集成轻量级混合适配器（MoA）模块，并结合最终层的选择性微调。该设计实现了由全局语义上下文向量引导的空间感知适配，结合深度区间分类与直接回归的混合预测架构。为提升结构精度，我们采用复合损失函数施加几何约束。在NYU Depth V2基准上，MoA-DepthCLIP取得竞争力结果，显著优于DepthCLIP基线：δ₁准确率从0.390提升至0.745，RMSE从1.176降至0.520。这些成果在参数量大幅减少的情况下实现，证明轻量级、提示引导的MoA是将VLM知识迁移到细粒度单目深度估计任务的有效策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of vision-language models like CLIP to enhance monocular depth estimation but hindered by high computational cost and geometric imprecision, the authors propose MoA-DepthCLIP, a lightweight adaptation framework that integrates a Mixture-of-Adapters module into a pretrained ViT-B/32 backbone with selective fine-tuning. The method uses a global semantic context vector and a hybrid depth bin classification plus direct regression architecture to guide spatially-aware adaptation, while a composite loss enforces geometric constraints. Experiments on NYU Depth V2 show it significantly outperforms the baseline DepthCLIP, boosting δ₁ from 0.390 to 0.745 and reducing RMSE from 1.176 to 0.520, all while using far fewer trainable parameters.
为利用CLIP等视觉语言模型的语义特征提升单目深度估计效果，同时克服其计算开销大和几何精度不足的问题，作者提出MoA-DepthCLIP框架，通过在预训练ViT-B/32主干网络中集成轻量级Mixture-of-Adapters模块并结合末层选择性微调实现高效适配。该方法借助全局语义上下文向量和混合预测架构（深度区间分类+直接回归）引导空间感知调整，并采用复合损失函数强化几何约束。在NYU Depth V2数据集上，该方法显著优于基线DepthCLIP，δ₁指标从0.390提升至0.745，RMSE从1.176降至0.520，且参数量大幅减少。
Reconsidering Dependency Networks from an Information Geometry Perspective
Authors: Kazuya Takabatake, Shotaro Akaho
First: 2026-04-01T16:40:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:40:47+00:00
Comments: 25 papers, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dependency networks (Heckerman et al., 2000) provide a flexible framework for modeling complex systems with many variables by combining independently learned local conditional distributions through pseudo-Gibbs sampling. Despite their computational advantages over Bayesian and Markov networks, the theoretical foundations of dependency networks remain incomplete, primarily because their model distributions -- defined as stationary distributions of pseudo-Gibbs sampling -- lack closed-form expressions. This paper develops an information-geometric analysis of pseudo-Gibbs sampling, interpreting each sampling step as an m-projection onto a full conditional manifold. Building on this interpretation, we introduce the full conditional divergence and derive an upper bound that characterizes the location of the stationary distribution in the space of probability distributions. We then reformulate both structure and parameter learning as optimization problems that decompose into independent subproblems for each node, and prove that the learned model distribution converges to the true underlying distribution as the number of training samples grows to infinity. Experiments confirm that the proposed upper bound is tight in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从信息几何视角重新审视依赖网络
依赖网络（Heckerman等，2000）通过伪吉布斯采样结合独立学习的局部条件分布，提供了一种灵活的复杂系统建模框架。尽管其计算效率优于贝叶斯网络和马尔可夫网络，但依赖网络的理论基础尚不完整，主要因其模型分布——定义为伪吉布斯采样的平稳分布——缺乏闭式表达。本文从信息几何角度分析伪吉布斯采样，将每一步采样解释为对全条件流形的m投影。基于此解释，我们引入全条件散度并推导出刻画平稳分布所在概率分布空间位置的上界。随后，我们将结构与参数学习重新表述为可分解为各节点独立子问题的优化问题，并证明当训练样本数量趋于无穷时，所学模型分布收敛于真实底层分布。实验表明，所提出的上界在实践中表现紧致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of theoretical grounding for dependency networks due to their non-closed-form stationary distributions, this paper introduces an information-geometric framework interpreting pseudo-Gibbs sampling as m-projections onto full conditional manifolds. It defines a full conditional divergence and derives an upper bound that locates the stationary distribution in the space of probability distributions. The authors reformulate both structure and parameter learning as decomposable optimization problems per node, proving asymptotic convergence to the true distribution with increasing data. Experiments validate that the derived upper bound is tight in practice.
由于依赖网络的平稳分布缺乏闭式表达，导致其理论基础不完整，本文从信息几何角度重新审视伪吉布斯采样，将其解释为对全条件流形的m投影，并定义全条件散度，推导出刻画平稳分布位置的上界。作者将结构与参数学习重构为按节点分解的优化问题，证明其在样本量趋于无穷时收敛于真实分布。实验表明，所提出的上界在实践中是紧致的。
ProTPS: Prototype-Guided Text Prompt Selection for Continual Learning
Authors: Jie Mei, Li-Leng Peng, Keith Fuller, Jenq-Neng Hwang
First: 2026-04-01T16:39:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:39:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
For continual learning, text-prompt-based methods leverage text encoders and learnable prompts to encode semantic features for sequentially arrived classes over time. A common challenge encountered by existing works is how to learn unique text prompts, which implicitly carry semantic information of new classes, so that the semantic features of newly arrived classes do not overlap with those of trained classes, thereby mitigating the catastrophic forgetting problem. To address this challenge, we propose a novel approach Prototype-guided Text Prompt Selection (ProTPS)'' to intentionally increase the training flexibility thus encouraging the learning of unique text prompts. Specifically, our ProTPS learns class-specific vision prototypes and text prompts. Vision prototypes guide the selection and learning of text prompts for each class. We first evaluate our ProTPS in both class incremental (CI) setting and cross-datasets continual (CDC) learning setting. Because our ProTPS achieves performance close to the upper bounds, we further collect a real-world dataset with 112 marine species collected over a span of six years, named Marine112, to bring new challenges to the community. Marine112 is authentically suited for the class and domain incremental (CDI) learning setting and is under natural long-tail distribution. The results under three settings show that our ProTPS performs favorably against the recent state-of-the-art methods. The implementation code and Marine112 dataset will be released upon the acceptance of our paper.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ProTPS：原型引导的文本提示选择用于持续学习
在持续学习中，基于文本提示的方法利用文本编码器和可学习提示，为随时间顺序到达的各类别编码语义特征。现有工作面临的一个共同挑战是如何学习独特的文本提示，这些提示隐含新类别的语义信息，从而避免新类别的语义特征与已训练类别的特征重叠，缓解灾难性遗忘问题。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种新颖的方法——原型引导的文本提示选择（ProTPS），旨在通过增加训练灵活性，鼓励学习独特的文本提示。具体而言，我们的ProTPS学习类别特定的视觉原型和文本提示，视觉原型指导每个类别的文本提示选择与学习。我们首先在类别增量（CI）设置和跨数据集持续（CDC）学习设置中评估ProTPS。由于ProTPS性能接近上限，我们进一步收集了一个包含112种海洋物种、历时六年的现实数据集，命名为Marine112，以给社区带来新挑战。Marine112天然适用于类别与域增量（CDI）学习设置，且符合自然长尾分布。在三种设置下的实验结果表明，我们的ProTPS优于近期最先进方法。代码与Marine112数据集将在论文被接受后公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of catastrophic forgetting in continual learning, where text prompts for new classes often overlap with those of previously learned classes, the paper proposes ProTPS, a prototype-guided text prompt selection method. It jointly learns class-specific vision prototypes and text prompts, using the prototypes to guide the selection and optimization of unique text prompts per class, thereby reducing semantic overlap. Evaluated across class incremental (CI), cross-dataset continual (CDC), and class-domain incremental (CDI) settings using a newly introduced real-world Marine112 dataset, ProTPS consistently outperforms recent state-of-the-art methods, achieving performance close to theoretical upper bounds.
为解决持续学习中因新类文本提示与旧类提示语义重叠导致的灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出ProTPS方法，通过视觉原型引导文本提示的选择与学习。该方法为每个类别联合学习视觉原型与文本提示，利用原型指导提示的差异化学习，从而降低语义冲突。在类增量（CI）、跨数据集持续学习（CDC）及类域增量（CDI）三种设置下，尤其在新构建的真实世界数据集Marine112（含112种海洋物种，自然长尾分布）上，ProTPS表现优于当前最先进方法，性能接近理论上限。
Trust and Reliance on AI in Education: AI Literacy and Need for Cognition as Moderators
Authors: Griffin Pitts, Neha Rani, Weedguet Mildort
First: 2026-04-01T16:38:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:38:47+00:00
Comments: Full paper accepted to the 27th International Conference on AI in Education (AIED 2026). AIED Proceedings to be released Summer 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As generative AI systems are integrated into educational settings, students often encounter AI-generated output while working through learning tasks, either by requesting help or through integrated tools. Trust in AI can influence how students interpret and use that output, including whether they evaluate it critically or exhibit overreliance. We investigate how students' trust relates to their appropriate reliance on an AI assistant during programming problem-solving tasks, and whether this relationship differs by learner characteristics. With 432 undergraduate participants, students' completed Python output-prediction problems while receiving recommendations and explanations from an AI chatbot, including accurate and intentionally misleading suggestions. We operationalize reliance behaviorally as the extent to which students' responses reflected appropriate use of the AI assistant's suggestions, accepting them when they were correct and rejecting them when they were incorrect. Pre- and post-task surveys assessed trust in the assistant, AI literacy, need for cognition, programming self-efficacy, and programming literacy. Results showed a non-linear relationship in which higher trust was associated with lower appropriate reliance, suggesting weaker discrimination between correct and incorrect recommendations. This relationship was significantly moderated by students' AI literacy and need for cognition. These findings highlight the need for future work on instructional and system supports that encourage more reflective evaluation of AI assistance during problem-solving.
中文标题/摘要
标题：教育中对人工智能的信任与依赖：人工智能素养与认知需求的调节作用
随着生成式人工智能系统融入教育场景，学生在完成学习任务时常会接触到AI生成的内容，无论是主动请求帮助还是通过集成工具。对AI的信任会影响学生如何解读和使用这些内容，包括是否批判性评估或过度依赖。本研究探讨学生对AI的信任如何影响其在编程问题解决任务中对AI助手的适当依赖，以及该关系是否因学习者特征而异。432名本科生在接收AI聊天机器人提供的建议和解释（包括准确和故意误导性建议）的情况下完成Python输出预测任务。我们通过学生对AI建议的采纳或拒绝行为来操作化“依赖”概念：采纳正确建议，拒绝错误建议。任务前后的问卷评估了学生对AI助手的信任、AI素养、认知需求、编程自我效能感和编程素养。结果表明，信任与适当依赖之间呈非线性关系：信任度越高，适当依赖越低，暗示学生对正确与错误建议的区分能力较弱。该关系显著受学生AI素养和认知需求调节。这些发现强调未来需开展更多关于教学与系统支持的研究，以促进学生在解决问题时对AI辅助进行更反思性的评估。
Summary / 总结
As generative AI becomes embedded in education, students’ trust in AI tools can shape how they engage with AI-generated content, potentially leading to overreliance or critical evaluation. This study examined how trust in an AI assistant during programming tasks correlated with appropriate reliance—defined as accepting correct suggestions and rejecting incorrect ones—and how this relationship was moderated by AI literacy and need for cognition. With 432 undergraduates solving Python problems while receiving AI-generated advice, researchers found a non-linear pattern: higher trust was linked to lower appropriate reliance, indicating reduced ability to distinguish accurate from misleading suggestions. This effect was significantly moderated by AI literacy and need for cognition, suggesting that students with higher AI literacy or cognitive engagement were better able to regulate their trust and use AI appropriately.
随着生成式AI融入教育，学生对AI的信任会影响其对AI输出的使用方式，有时导致过度依赖。本研究考察了学生在编程任务中对AI的信任与其适当依赖程度的关系，使用432名本科生在接收准确或误导性AI建议时完成任务。依赖程度通过学生是否适当接受或拒绝AI建议来衡量，问卷评估了信任度、AI素养、认知需求及编程能力。结果显示，信任度与适当依赖呈非线性关系：信任度越高，适当依赖越低，表明学生更难区分正确与错误的AI建议。该关系受AI素养和认知需求调节，说明AI素养高或认知需求强的学生更能批判性评估AI输出。
VT-Former: Efffcient Transformer-based Decoder for Varshamov-Tenengolts Codes
Authors: Yali Wei, Alan J. X. Guo, Zihui Yan, Yufan Dai, Wenjia Fan
First: 2025-02-28T13:59:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:38:21+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 10 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, widespread attention has been drawn to the challenge of correcting insertion, deletion, and substitution (IDS) errors in DNA-based data storage. Among various IDS-correcting codes, Varshamov-Tenengolts (VT) codes, originally designed for single-error correction, have been established as a central research focus. While existing decoding methods demonstrate high accuracy for single-error correction, they are typically not applicable to the correction of multiple IDS errors. In this work, the latent capability of VT codes for multiple-error correction is investigated through a statistic-enhanced Transformer-based VT decoder (VT-Former), utilizing both symbol and statistic feature embeddings. Experimental results demonstrate that VT-Former achieves nearly 100\% accuracy on correcting single errors. For multi-error decoding tasks across various codeword lengths, improvements in both frame accuracy and bit accuracy are observed, compared to conventional hard-decision and soft-in soft-out decoding algorithms. Furthermore, while lower decoding latency is exhibited by the base model compared to traditional soft decoders, the architecture is further optimized in this study to enhance decoding efficiency and reduce computational overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VT-Former：基于Transformer的高效Varshamov-Tenengolts码解码器
近年来，DNA数据存储中插入、删除和替换（IDS）错误的纠正问题受到广泛关注。在多种IDS纠错码中，最初为单错误纠正设计的Varshamov-Tenengolts（VT）码已成为核心研究对象。尽管现有解码方法在单错误纠正方面表现出高精度，但通常不适用于多IDS错误纠正。本文通过引入统计增强的Transformer基VT解码器（VT-Former），利用符号和统计特征嵌入，探究了VT码在多错误纠正方面的潜在能力。实验结果表明，VT-Former在纠正单错误时准确率接近100%。在不同码长的多错误解码任务中，与传统硬判决和软输入软输出解码算法相比，其帧准确率和比特准确率均有所提升。此外，尽管基础模型相比传统软解码器具有更低的解码延迟，本文进一步优化了架构，以提高解码效率并降低计算开销。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to correct insertion, deletion, and substitution errors in DNA-based data storage, this work explores the latent capability of Varshamov-Tenengolts codes to handle multiple errors. It proposes VT-Former, a Transformer-based decoder that integrates symbol and statistic feature embeddings to enhance decoding performance. Experimental results show VT-Former achieves nearly 100% accuracy for single-error correction and significantly improves both frame and bit accuracy for multi-error scenarios compared to conventional hard-decision and soft-in soft-out decoders. Additionally, the architecture is optimized to reduce computational overhead while maintaining low decoding latency.
为应对DNA数据存储中插入、删除和替换错误的纠正需求，本文研究了Varshamov-Tenengolts码在多错误纠正方面的潜在能力。提出VT-Former，一种结合符号与统计特征嵌入的Transformer解码器，以提升解码性能。实验表明，VT-Former在单错误纠正中准确率接近100%，在多错误场景下相较传统硬判决与软输入软输出解码器显著提升帧准确率与比特准确率，同时通过架构优化降低计算开销并保持低解码延迟。
A Player Selection Network for Scalable Game-Theoretic Prediction and Planning
Authors: Tianyu Qiu, Eric Ouano, Fernando Palafox, Christian Ellis, David Fridovich-Keil
First: 2025-04-30T23:14:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:38:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While game-theoretic planning frameworks are effective at modeling multi-agent interactions, they require solving large optimization problems where the number of variables increases with the number of agents, resulting in long computation times that limit their use in large-scale, real-time systems. To address this issue, we propose 1) PSN Game-a learning-based, game-theoretic prediction and planning framework that reduces game size by learning a Player Selection Network (PSN); and 2) a Goal Inference Network (GIN) that makes it possible to use the PSN in incomplete-information games where other agents' intentions are unknown to the ego agent. A PSN outputs a player selection mask that distinguishes influential players from less relevant ones, enabling the ego player to solve a smaller, masked game involving only selected players. By reducing the number of players included in the game, PSN shrinks the corresponding optimization problems, leading to faster solve times. Experiments in both simulated scenarios and real-world pedestrian trajectory datasets show that PSN is competitive with, and often improves upon, the evaluated explicit game-theoretic selection baselines in 1) prediction accuracy and 2) planning safety. Across scenarios, PSN typically selects substantially fewer players than are present in the full game, thereby reducing game size and planning complexity. PSN also generalizes to settings in which agents' objectives are unknown, via the GIN, without test-time fine-tuning. By selecting only the most relevant players for decision-making, PSN Game provides a practical mechanism for reducing planning complexity that can be integrated into existing multi-agent planning frameworks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于可扩展博弈论预测与规划的玩家选择网络
尽管博弈论规划框架在建模多智能体交互方面有效，但它们需要求解大型优化问题，其中变量数量随智能体数量增加，导致计算时间过长，限制其在大规模实时系统中的应用。为解决此问题，我们提出：1）PSN Game——一种基于学习的博弈论预测与规划框架，通过学习玩家选择网络（PSN）来缩小博弈规模；2）目标推断网络（GIN），使其能在其他智能体意图未知的不完全信息博弈中使用PSN。PSN输出一个玩家选择掩码，区分重要玩家与次要玩家，使主体智能体仅需解决包含选定玩家的较小掩码博弈。通过减少博弈中包含的玩家数量，PSN缩小了相应的优化问题，从而加快求解速度。在模拟场景和真实行人轨迹数据集上的实验表明，PSN在预测精度和规划安全性方面与现有显式博弈论选择基线方法相当，且通常表现更优。在各类场景中，PSN通常选择远少于完整博弈中所有玩家，从而降低博弈规模和规划复杂度。通过GIN，PSN还能在无需测试时微调的情况下，泛化到智能体目标未知的场景。通过仅选择对决策最相关的玩家，PSN Game提供了一种实用机制，可降低规划复杂度并集成到现有多智能体规划框架中。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the computational burden of large-scale game-theoretic planning, this work introduces PSN Game, a learning-based framework that uses a Player Selection Network (PSN) to identify and focus on only the most influential agents, thereby reducing game size and optimization complexity. The PSN generates a selection mask to filter out irrelevant players, enabling the ego agent to solve a smaller, masked game. Complemented by a Goal Inference Network (GIN) for handling incomplete information, PSN achieves competitive or superior performance in prediction accuracy and planning safety across simulated and real-world pedestrian datasets. It consistently selects far fewer players than the full game, leading to faster computation without sacrificing effectiveness, and generalizes to unknown agent objectives without fine-tuning.
为解决传统博弈论规划随代理数量增长而计算效率低的问题，本文提出PSN Game框架，通过玩家选择网络（PSN）识别关键代理以缩小博弈规模，配合目标推断网络（GIN）处理信息不完整场景。实验表明，PSN在预测精度与规划安全性上优于基线方法，同时显著减少所选代理数量，降低复杂度，且无需微调即可泛化至目标未知场景。
CARE: Privacy-Compliant Agentic Reasoning with Evidence Discordance
Authors: Haochen Liu, Weien Li, Rui Song, Zeyu Li, Chun Jason Xue, Xiao-Yang Liu, Sam Nallaperuma, Xue Liu, Ye Yuan
First: 2026-04-01T16:37:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:37:01+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) systems are increasingly used to support high-stakes decision-making, but they typically perform worse when the available evidence is internally inconsistent. Such a scenario exists in real-world healthcare settings, with patient-reported symptoms contradicting medical signs. To study this problem, we introduce MIMIC-DOS, a dataset for short-horizon organ dysfunction worsening prediction in the intensive care unit (ICU) setting. We derive this dataset from the widely recognized MIMIC-IV, a publicly available electronic health record dataset, and construct it exclusively from cases in which discordance between signs and symptoms exists. This setting poses a substantial challenge for existing LLM-based approaches, with single-pass LLMs and agentic pipelines often struggling to reconcile such conflicting signals. To address this problem, we propose CARE: a multi-stage privacy-compliant agentic reasoning framework in which a remote LLM provides guidance by generating structured categories and transitions without accessing sensitive patient data, while a local LLM uses these categories and transitions to support evidence acquisition and final decision-making. Empirically, CARE achieves stronger performance across all key metrics compared to multiple baseline settings, showing that CARE can more robustly handle conflicting clinical evidence while preserving privacy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CARE：基于证据矛盾的隐私保护代理推理框架
大型语言模型（LLM）系统越来越多地用于支持高风险决策，但在证据内部不一致时通常表现较差。这种情形在现实医疗环境中普遍存在，例如患者报告的症状与医学体征相互矛盾。为研究该问题，我们引入MIMIC-DOS数据集，用于预测重症监护室（ICU）中短期器官功能恶化的场景。该数据集从广受认可的公开电子健康记录数据集MIMIC-IV中构建，仅包含体征与症状矛盾的病例。此设置对现有基于LLM的方法构成重大挑战，单次推理LLM和代理流水线常难以协调此类冲突信号。为解决该问题，我们提出CARE：一种多阶段隐私保护代理推理框架。远程LLM在不接触敏感患者数据的前提下，通过生成结构化类别和转换提供指导；本地LLM则利用这些类别和转换支持证据获取与最终决策。实证表明，CARE在所有关键指标上均优于多个基线方法，证明其能更稳健地处理冲突临床证据并保护隐私。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the real-world challenge of inconsistent clinical evidence in ICU settings, this paper introduces CARE, a privacy-compliant agentic reasoning framework designed to handle conflicting patient symptoms and signs. The method employs a remote LLM to generate structured reasoning categories and transitions without accessing sensitive data, while a local LLM uses these to guide evidence acquisition and decision-making. Evaluated on MIMIC-DOS — a novel dataset derived from MIMIC-IV containing only cases with discordant signs and symptoms — CARE outperforms multiple baseline models across key metrics, demonstrating improved robustness to evidence conflict while maintaining privacy.
为应对大语言模型在临床证据冲突（如患者症状与体征矛盾）场景下表现不佳的问题，本文提出CARE框架，一种隐私保护的代理推理方法。该框架分两阶段：远程大模型生成结构化证据类别与转换规则而不接触患者数据，本地大模型据此引导证据获取与决策。在从MIMIC-IV构建的、专含证据冲突的MIMIC-DOS数据集上，CARE在多个关键指标上超越多项基线方法，证明其在处理矛盾临床证据时更具鲁棒性，同时保障隐私。
Adversarial Moral Stress Testing of Large Language Models
Authors: Saeid Jamshidi, Foutse Khomh, Arghavan Moradi Dakhel, Amin Nikanjam, Mohammad Hamdaqa, Kawser Wazed Nafi
First: 2026-04-01T16:34:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:34:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating the ethical robustness of large language models (LLMs) deployed in software systems remains challenging, particularly under sustained adversarial user interaction. Existing safety benchmarks typically rely on single-round evaluations and aggregate metrics, such as toxicity scores and refusal rates, which offer limited visibility into behavioral instability that may arise during realistic multi-turn interactions. As a result, rare but high-impact ethical failures and progressive degradation effects may remain undetected prior to deployment. This paper introduces Adversarial Moral Stress Testing (AMST), a stress-based evaluation framework for assessing ethical robustness under adversarial multi-round interactions. AMST applies structured stress transformations to prompts and evaluates model behavior through distribution-aware robustness metrics that capture variance, tail risk, and temporal behavioral drift across interaction rounds. We evaluate AMST on several state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA-3-8B, GPT-4o, and DeepSeek-v3, using a large set of adversarial scenarios generated under controlled stress conditions. The results demonstrate substantial differences in robustness profiles across models and expose degradation patterns that are not observable under conventional single-round evaluation protocols. In particular, robustness has been shown to depend on distributional stability and tail behavior rather than on average performance alone. Additionally, AMST provides a scalable and model-agnostic stress-testing methodology that enables robustness-aware evaluation and monitoring of LLM-enabled software systems operating in adversarial environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型的对抗道德压力测试
评估部署在软件系统中的大型语言模型（LLMs）的伦理稳健性仍具挑战性，特别是在持续对抗性用户交互下。现有安全基准通常依赖单轮评估和聚合指标（如毒性评分和拒绝率），难以揭示在真实多轮交互中可能出现的行为不稳定性。因此，罕见但影响重大的伦理失败及渐进退化效应可能在部署前未被发现。本文提出对抗道德压力测试（AMST），一种基于压力的评估框架，用于在对抗性多轮交互下评估伦理稳健性。AMST对提示施加结构化压力变换，并通过分布感知的稳健性指标评估模型行为，这些指标捕捉交互轮次间的行为方差、尾部风险及时间漂移。我们在多个前沿LLM（包括LLaMA-3-8B、GPT-4o和DeepSeek-v3）上评估AMST，使用在受控压力条件下生成的大量对抗场景。结果表明不同模型的稳健性特征存在显著差异，并揭示了传统单轮评估协议下不可见的退化模式。特别是，稳健性取决于分布稳定性与尾部行为，而非平均性能本身。此外，AMST提供了一种可扩展且模型无关的压力测试方法，支持在对抗环境中对LLM驱动软件系统的稳健性评估与监控。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language models' ethical robustness under sustained adversarial user interaction, this paper introduces Adversarial Moral Stress Testing (AMST), a framework that applies structured stress transformations to prompts and measures model behavior using distribution-aware metrics capturing variance, tail risk, and temporal drift across multi-turn interactions. Evaluated on LLaMA-3-8B, GPT-4o, and DeepSeek-v3 using adversarial scenarios under controlled stress, AMST reveals significant differences in robustness profiles among models and uncovers degradation patterns invisible under single-round benchmarks. Results show that robustness depends on distributional stability and tail behavior, not just average performance, and AMST offers a scalable, model-agnostic method for stress-testing LLMs in adversarial environments.
为评估大语言模型在持续对抗性用户交互下的伦理鲁棒性，本文提出对抗道德压力测试（AMST）框架，通过结构化压力变换对提示进行扰动，并利用捕捉方差、尾部风险和交互轮次间行为漂移的分布感知指标评估模型表现。在LLaMA-3-8B、GPT-4o和DeepSeek-v3等模型上，使用受控压力环境生成的对抗场景进行评估，结果揭示各模型鲁棒性差异显著，且发现单轮评估无法观测到的退化模式。研究显示鲁棒性取决于分布稳定性与尾部行为，而非平均性能，AMST提供了一种可扩展、模型无关的压力测试方法，适用于对抗环境下LLM系统的鲁棒性评估与监控。
How Motivation Relates to Generative AI Use: A Large-Scale Survey of Mexican High School Students
Authors: Echo Zexuan Pan, Danny Glick, Ying Xu
First: 2026-02-26T21:32:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:33:53+00:00
Comments: This submission has been accepted by the ICLS Conference at the ISLS Annual Meeting. It will be included as a poster in the 2026 conference proceedings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study examined how high school students with different motivational profiles use generative AI tools in math and writing. Through K-means clustering analysis of survey data from 6,793 Mexican high school students, we identified three distinct motivational profiles based on self-concept and perceived subject value. Results revealed distinct domain-specific AI usage patterns across students with different motivational profiles. Our findings challenge one-size-fits-all AI integration approaches and advocate for motivationally-informed educational interventions.

标题：动机如何与生成式人工智能使用相关：对墨西哥高中生的大规模调查

This study investigates how motivational differences among Mexican high school students influence their use of generative AI in math and writing, motivated by the need to understand personalized AI adoption in education. Inverse Design of Optical Multilayer Thin Films using Robust Masked Diffusion Models
Authors: Jonas Schaible, Asena Karolin Özdemir, Charlotte Debus, Sven Burger, Achim Streit, Christiane Becker, Klaus Jäger, Markus Götz
First: 2026-04-01T16:33:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:33:05+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 14 Figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inverse design of optical multilayer stacks seeks to infer layer materials, thicknesses, and ordering from a desired target spectrum. It is a long-standing challenge due to the large design space and non-unique solutions. We introduce \texttt{OptoLlama}, a masked diffusion language model for inverse thin-film design from optical spectra. Representing multilayer stacks as sequences of material-thickness tokens, \texttt{OptoLlama} conditions generation on reflectance, absorptance, and transmittance spectra and learns a probabilistic mapping from optical response to structure. Evaluated on a representative test set of 3,000 targets, \texttt{OptoLlama} reduces the mean absolute spectral error by 2.9-fold relative to a nearest-neighbor template baseline and by 3.45-fold relative to the state-of-the-art data-driven baseline, called \texttt{OptoGPT}. Case studies on designed and expert-defined targets show that the model reproduces characteristic spectral features and recovers physically meaningful stack motifs, including distributed Bragg reflectors. These results establish diffusion-based sequence modeling as a powerful framework for inverse photonic design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用鲁棒掩码扩散模型进行光学多层薄膜的逆向设计
光学多层堆叠的逆向设计旨在从期望的目标光谱推断各层材料、厚度及排列顺序。由于设计空间庞大且解不唯一，该问题长期存在挑战。我们提出了\texttt{OptoLlama}，一种用于从光学光谱进行逆向薄膜设计的掩码扩散语言模型。将多层堆叠表示为材料-厚度令牌序列，\texttt{OptoLlama}以反射率、吸光度和透射率光谱为条件进行生成，并学习从光学响应到结构的概率映射。在包含3000个目标的代表性测试集上评估，\texttt{OptoLlama}相较最近邻模板基线将平均光谱绝对误差降低2.9倍，相较当前最先进的数据驱动基线\texttt{OptoGPT}降低3.45倍。针对设计和专家定义的目标的案例研究表明，该模型能再现典型光谱特征并恢复具有物理意义的堆叠结构，包括分布式布拉格反射器。这些结果确立了基于扩散的序列建模作为逆向光子设计的强大框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of inferring material compositions, thicknesses, and layer orders in optical multilayer thin films from target spectra, the authors propose OptoLlama, a masked diffusion language model that treats multilayer stacks as token sequences. The model is conditioned on optical spectra and learns to probabilistically map spectral responses to structural configurations. Evaluated on 3,000 test targets, OptoLlama reduces mean absolute spectral error by 2.9x compared to a nearest-neighbor baseline and 3.45x compared to the prior state-of-the-art OptoGPT. Case studies confirm that the model generates physically meaningful structures, including distributed Bragg reflectors, and accurately reproduces spectral features.
为解决从目标光谱反推光学多层薄膜材料、厚度及层序的难题，作者提出OptoLlama，一种基于掩码扩散的语言模型，将多层结构建模为材料-厚度序列。模型以光谱响应（反射率、吸收率、透射率）为条件，学习从光谱到结构的概率映射。在3000个测试目标上，OptoLlama相较最近邻基线降低2.9倍平均光谱绝对误差，相较当前最优数据驱动模型OptoGPT降低3.45倍。案例研究表明，该模型能准确复现光谱特征并恢复物理上合理的结构，如布喇格反射器，验证了扩散序列建模在逆向光子设计中的有效性。
When Only the Final Text Survives: Implicit Execution Tracing for Multi-Agent Attribution
Authors: Yi Nian, Haosen Cao, Shenzhe Zhu, Henry Peng Zou, Qingqing Luan, Yue Zhao
First: 2026-03-18T07:34:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:30:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When a multi-agent system produces an incorrect or harmful answer, who is accountable if execution logs and agent identifiers are unavailable? In practice, generated content is often detached from its execution environment due to privacy or system boundaries, leaving the final text as the only auditable artifact. Existing attribution methods rely on full execution traces and thus become ineffective in such metadata-deprived settings. We propose Implicit Execution Tracing (IET), a provenance-by-design framework that shifts attribution from post-hoc inference to built-in instrumentation. Instead of reconstructing hidden trajectories, IET embeds agent-specific, key-conditioned statistical signals directly into the token generation process, transforming the output text into a self-verifying execution record. At inference time, we recover a linearized execution trace from the final text via transition-aware statistical scoring. Experiments across diverse multi-agent coordination settings demonstrate that IET achieves accurate segment-level attribution and reliable transition recovery under identity removal, boundary corruption, and privacy-preserving redaction, while maintaining generation quality. These results show that embedding provenance into generation provides a practical and robust foundation for accountability in multi-agent language systems when execution metadata is unavailable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：仅当最终文本存留时：面向多智能体归因的隐式执行追踪
当多智能体系统生成错误或有害答案时，若执行日志和智能体标识符不可用，应由谁负责？实践中，由于隐私或系统边界限制，生成内容常与执行环境分离，仅剩最终文本作为可审计的唯一产物。现有归因方法依赖完整执行轨迹，因此在元数据缺失环境下失效。我们提出隐式执行追踪（IET），一种设计即溯源的框架，将归因从事后推断转向内置仪器化。IET不重建隐藏轨迹，而是将智能体特异、关键条件驱动的统计信号直接嵌入到令牌生成过程中，使输出文本成为可自我验证的执行记录。推理时，通过具备转移感知的统计评分，从最终文本中恢复线性化执行轨迹。在多种多智能体协作场景的实验中，IET在身份移除、边界破坏及隐私保护式删改条件下，仍能实现准确的段落级归因与可靠的转移恢复，同时保持生成质量。结果表明，将溯源嵌入生成过程，为执行元数据缺失时多智能体语言系统的问责提供了实用且稳健的基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of attributing responsibility in multi-agent systems when execution logs and agent identities are unavailable, the paper introduces Implicit Execution Tracing (IET), a framework that embeds agent-specific statistical signals directly into token generation to create self-verifying output text. Instead of reconstructing hidden execution paths, IET instruments the generation process to encode provenance, enabling attribution via transition-aware statistical scoring on the final text. Experiments across varied coordination tasks show IET achieves accurate segment-level attribution and reliable transition recovery even under identity removal, boundary corruption, and privacy redaction, without compromising generation quality.
为了解决多智能体系统在缺乏执行日志时的责任归属难题，本文提出隐式执行追踪（IET）框架，通过在生成过程中嵌入智能体特定的统计信号，使输出文本本身成为可验证的执行记录。该方法不依赖于重建隐藏执行路径，而是在生成时内建溯源信息，通过基于状态转移的统计评分从最终文本中恢复执行轨迹。实验表明，即使在身份删除、边界破坏和隐私遮蔽等条件下，IET仍能实现精准的段落级责任归因和可靠的转移恢复，且不影响生成质量。
Genesis: Evolving Attack Strategies for LLM Web Agent Red-Teaming
Authors: Zheng Zhang, Jiarui He, Yuchen Cai, Deheng Ye, Peilin Zhao, Ruili Feng, Hao Wang
Venue: ICME 2026
First: 2025-10-21T05:49:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:25:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICME 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate complex web tasks, they boost productivity while simultaneously introducing new security risks. However, relevant studies on web agent attacks remain limited. Existing red-teaming approaches mainly rely on manually crafted attack strategies or static models trained offline. Such methods fail to capture the underlying behavioral patterns of web agents, making it difficult to generalize across diverse environments. In web agent attacks, success requires the continuous discovery and evolution of attack strategies. To this end, we propose Genesis, a novel agentic framework composed of three modules: Attacker, Scorer, and Strategist. The Attacker generates adversarial injections by integrating the genetic algorithm with a hybrid strategy representation. The Scorer evaluates the target web agent's responses to provide feedback. The Strategist dynamically uncovers effective strategies from interaction logs and compiles them into a continuously growing strategy library, which is then re-deployed to enhance the Attacker's effectiveness. Extensive experiments across various web tasks show that our framework discovers novel strategies and consistently outperforms existing attack baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/CjangCjengh/web_agent_attack.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Genesis：面向大语言模型网络代理红队攻击的进化攻击策略
随着大语言模型（LLM）代理越来越多地自动化复杂网络任务，它们提升了生产力，同时也引入了新的安全风险。然而，针对网络代理攻击的相关研究仍较为有限。现有红队攻击方法主要依赖人工设计的攻击策略或离线训练的静态模型，这些方法难以捕捉网络代理的行为模式，难以在不同环境中泛化。在代理攻击中，成功需要持续发现和演化攻击策略。为此，我们提出Genesis，一种新颖的代理框架，包含三个模块：攻击者、评分器和策略师。攻击者通过结合遗传算法与混合策略表示生成对抗注入；评分器评估目标网络代理的响应并提供反馈；策略师从交互日志中动态发现有效策略，并将其编入持续增长的策略库，再重新部署以增强攻击者效能。在多种网络任务上的广泛实验表明，我们的框架发现了新颖策略，并持续优于现有攻击基线。代码开源于 https://github.com/CjangCjengh/web_agent_attack。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to address security risks posed by increasingly autonomous LLM web agents, this paper introduces Genesis, a novel agentic framework designed to evolve attack strategies dynamically. The framework comprises three modules: an Attacker that generates adversarial inputs using a genetic algorithm with hybrid strategy representation, a Scorer that evaluates agent responses for feedback, and a Strategist that extracts and accumulates effective strategies from interaction logs into a growing library to enhance future attacks. Experimental results across multiple web tasks demonstrate that Genesis discovers novel attack strategies and consistently outperforms existing baselines in attack success rates.
鉴于大语言模型（LLM）网络代理的广泛应用及其攻击方法研究的不足，本文提出Genesis框架，通过动态演化攻击策略提升攻击效果。该框架包含三个模块：攻击者模块利用遗传算法与混合策略编码生成对抗输入，评分模块评估目标代理响应以提供反馈，策略家模块从交互日志中提取有效策略并构建持续增长的策略库以指导后续攻击。在多个网络任务上的实验表明，Genesis能发现新颖攻击模式，且在攻击成功率上持续优于静态及人工构造的基线方法。
Approximating Pareto Frontiers in Stochastic Multi-Objective Optimization via Hashing and Randomization
Authors: Jinzhao Li, Nan Jiang, Yexiang Xue
First: 2026-04-01T16:24:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:24:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic Multi-Objective Optimization (SMOO) is critical for decision-making trading off multiple potentially conflicting objectives in uncertain environments. SMOO aims at identifying the Pareto frontier, which contains all mutually non-dominating decisions. The problem is highly intractable due to the embedded probabilistic inference, such as computing the marginal, posterior probabilities, or expectations. Existing methods, such as scalarization, sample average approximation, and evolutionary algorithms, either offer arbitrarily loose approximations or may incur prohibitive computational costs. We propose XOR-SMOO, a novel algorithm that with probability $1-δ$, obtains $γ$-approximate Pareto frontiers ($γ>1$) for SMOO by querying an SAT oracle poly-log times in $γ$ and $δ$. A $γ$-approximate Pareto frontier is only below the true frontier by a fixed, multiplicative factor $γ$. Thus, XOR-SMOO solves highly intractable SMOO problems (\#P-hard) with only queries to SAT oracles while obtaining tight, constant factor approximation guarantees. Experiments on real-world road network strengthening and supply chain design problems demonstrate that XOR-SMOO outperforms several baselines in identifying Pareto frontiers that have higher objective values, better coverage of the optimal solutions, and the solutions found are more evenly distributed. Overall, XOR-SMOO significantly enhanced the practicality and reliability of SMOO solvers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过哈希与随机化近似随机多目标优化中的帕累托前沿
随机多目标优化（SMOO）在不确定环境中对多个潜在冲突的目标进行权衡决策至关重要。SMOO旨在识别帕累托前沿，其中包含所有相互非支配的决策。由于嵌入了概率推理（如计算边际、后验概率或期望），该问题高度不可行。现有方法（如标量化解、样本平均近似和进化算法）要么提供任意宽松的近似，要么可能产生高昂的计算成本。我们提出XOR-SMOO，一种新颖算法，以概率1-δ，在γ和δ的多项式对数次查询SAT预言机的情况下，获得SMOO的γ-近似帕累托前沿（γ>1）。γ-近似帕累托前沿仅比真实前沿低一个固定的乘数因子γ。因此，XOR-SMOO仅通过查询SAT预言机即可解决高度不可行的SMOO问题（#P-hard），并获得紧致的常数因子近似保证。在现实世界道路网络强化和供应链设计问题上的实验表明，XOR-SMOO在识别具有更高目标值、更优最优解覆盖度且解分布更均匀的帕累托前沿方面优于多个基线方法。总体而言，XOR-SMOO显著提升了SMOO求解器的实用性和可靠性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational intractability of stochastic multi-objective optimization (SMOO) due to probabilistic inference, the paper introduces XOR-SMOO, a novel algorithm that leverages hashing and randomization to approximate Pareto frontiers with provable guarantees. By querying an SAT oracle poly-logarithmically in the approximation factor γ and confidence δ, XOR-SMOO achieves γ-approximate Pareto frontiers (γ > 1) with high probability, offering tight multiplicative bounds. Experimental results on real-world road network and supply chain problems show that XOR-SMOO outperforms baselines by identifying higher objective values, better solution coverage, and more evenly distributed solutions, demonstrating its practical efficacy for solving #P-hard SMOO problems.
为解决因概率推理导致的随机多目标优化（SMOO）计算难题，本文提出XOR-SMOO算法，结合哈希与随机化技术，通过查询SAT预言机实现对帕累托前沿的近似求解。该算法在概率1−δ下，仅需对γ和δ的多项式对数次查询，即可获得γ-近似帕累托前沿（γ>1），确保解与真实前沿的乘法误差固定。在现实世界问题如道路网络强化与供应链设计上的实验表明，XOR-SMOO优于多个基线方法，能发现目标值更高、覆盖更优解、分布更均匀的解集，显著提升了SMOO求解器的实用性与可靠性。
Temporal Dependencies in In-Context Learning: The Role of Induction Heads
Authors: Anooshka Bajaj, Deven Mahesh Mistry, Sahaj Singh Maini, Yash Aggarwal, Billy Dickson, Zoran Tiganj
First: 2026-04-01T16:21:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:21:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong in-context learning capabilities, but how they track and retrieve information from context remains underexplored. Drawing on the free recall paradigm in cognitive science (where participants recall list items in any order), we show that several open-source LLMs consistently display a serial-recall-like pattern, assigning peak probability to tokens that immediately follow a repeated token in the input sequence. Through systematic ablation experiments, we show that induction heads, specialized attention heads that attend to the token following a previous occurrence of the current token, play an important role in this phenomenon. Removing heads with a high induction score substantially reduces the +1 lag bias, whereas ablating random heads does not reproduce the same reduction. We also show that removing heads with high induction scores impairs the performance of models prompted to do serial recall using few-shot learning to a larger extent than removing random heads. Our findings highlight a mechanistically specific connection between induction heads and temporal context processing in transformers, suggesting that these heads are especially important for ordered retrieval and serial-recall-like behavior during in-context learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：上下文学习中的时间依赖性：归纳头的作用
大型语言模型（LLMs）表现出强大的上下文学习能力，但它们如何追踪和检索上下文信息仍不为人所知。借鉴认知科学中的自由回忆范式（参与者可按任意顺序回忆列表项目），我们发现多个开源LLMs一致表现出类似序列回忆的模式，即赋予输入序列中重复词后紧随词的最高概率。通过系统性消融实验，我们表明，专门关注当前词前一次出现后下一个词的归纳头，在这一现象中起重要作用。移除高归纳得分的头显著降低+1滞后偏差，而随机移除头则无法产生相同效果。此外，移除高归纳得分头对使用少样本学习提示进行序列回忆的模型性能损害更大。我们的发现揭示了归纳头与Transformer中时间上下文处理之间具有机制特异性关联，表明这些头在上下文学习中的有序检索和序列回忆行为中尤为重要。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models track temporal sequences during in-context learning, motivated by cognitive science’s free recall paradigm. The authors find that several open-source LLMs exhibit a serial-recall-like bias, favoring tokens immediately following repeated tokens. Through ablation experiments, they identify 'induction heads'—attention heads that focus on the token after a previous occurrence—as critical for this behavior. Removing induction heads significantly reduces the +1 lag bias and degrades serial recall performance more than ablating random heads, indicating a mechanistic link between these heads and temporal context processing.
该研究旨在探究大语言模型在上下文学习中如何追踪时间序列，受认知科学自由回忆范式的启发。研究发现多个开源模型表现出类似序列回忆的偏差，倾向于选择紧随重复词后的词。通过消融实验，研究人员发现‘诱导头’——关注当前词前一次出现后下一个词的注意力头——对这一现象至关重要。移除诱导头显著降低了+1滞后偏差，且对序列回忆任务的性能损害大于随机移除头，表明这些头在处理时间上下文和有序检索中具有机制性作用。
DR-LoRA: Dynamic Rank LoRA for Fine-Tuning Mixture-of-Experts Models
Authors: Guanzhi Deng, Bo Li, Ronghao Chen, Xiujin Liu, Zhuo Han, Huacan Wang, Lijie Wen, Linqi Song
First: 2026-01-08T10:58:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:21:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become a prominent paradigm for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs). Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods, such as LoRA, are widely adopted to adapt pretrained MoE LLMs to downstream tasks. However, existing approaches typically assign identical LoRA ranks to all expert modules, ignoring the heterogeneous specialization of pretrained experts. This uniform allocation leads to a resource mismatch: task-relevant experts are under-provisioned, while less relevant ones receive redundant parameters. To address this, we propose DR-LoRA, a Dynamic Rank LoRA framework for fine-tuning pretrained MoE models. Specifically, DR-LoRA initializes all expert LoRA modules with a small active rank and uses an expert saliency score, which combines routing frequency and gradient-based rank importance, to identify which experts would benefit most from additional capacity. It then periodically expands the active ranks of the task-critical expert LoRA, progressively constructing a heterogeneous rank distribution tailored to the target task. Experiments on three MoE models across six tasks show that DR-LoRA consistently outperforms LoRA and other strong baselines, demonstrating that task-adaptive heterogeneous rank allocation is an effective strategy to improve active capacity utilization in MoE fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DR-LoRA：用于专家混合模型微调的动态秩LoRA
专家混合（MoE）已成为扩展大语言模型（LLM）的主流范式。参数高效的微调方法如LoRA被广泛用于适配预训练的MoE LLM以适应下游任务。然而，现有方法通常为所有专家模块分配相同的LoRA秩，忽视了预训练专家的异质专业化特性。这种统一分配导致资源错配：任务相关专家资源不足，而相关性较低的专家则获得冗余参数。为解决此问题，我们提出DR-LoRA，一种用于预训练MoE模型微调的动态秩LoRA框架。具体而言，DR-LoRA初始时为所有专家LoRA模块分配小的活跃秩，并通过结合路由频率与梯度基秩重要性的专家显著性得分，识别哪些专家最需额外容量。随后，它周期性扩展关键任务专家LoRA的活跃秩，逐步构建针对目标任务的异质秩分布。在六个任务上的三个MoE模型实验表明，DR-LoRA持续优于LoRA及其他强基线方法，证明任务自适应异质秩分配是提升MoE微调中活跃容量利用率的有效策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform LoRA rank allocation in MoE fine-tuning, DR-LoRA introduces a dynamic rank adjustment mechanism that adapts LoRA capacity per expert based on task relevance. It initializes all expert LoRA modules with low rank and uses a saliency score combining routing frequency and gradient-based importance to identify critical experts, then incrementally expands their active ranks. Experiments across three MoE models and six tasks show DR-LoRA consistently outperforms standard LoRA and other baselines, confirming that heterogeneous rank allocation improves capacity utilization and model performance.
为解决MoE微调中统一LoRA秩分配导致资源浪费的问题，DR-LoRA提出动态秩调整机制，根据专家任务相关性动态分配LoRA容量。它初始为所有专家分配低秩LoRA模块，并结合路由频率与梯度重要性计算专家显著性得分，优先扩展关键专家的秩。在三个MoE模型和六个任务上的实验表明，DR-LoRA持续优于标准LoRA及其他基线方法，验证了异构秩分配能有效提升容量利用率与模型性能。
TRACE: Training-Free Partial Audio Deepfake Detection via Embedding Trajectory Analysis of Speech Foundation Models
Authors: Awais Khan, Muhammad Umar Farooq, Kutub Uddin, Khalid Malik
First: 2026-04-01T16:12:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:12:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Partial audio deepfakes, where synthesized segments are spliced into genuine recordings, are particularly deceptive because most of the audio remains authentic. Existing detectors are supervised: they require frame-level annotations, overfit to specific synthesis pipelines, and must be retrained as new generative models emerge. We argue that this supervision is unnecessary. We hypothesize that speech foundation models implicitly encode a forensic signal: genuine speech forms smooth, slowly varying embedding trajectories, while splice boundaries introduce abrupt disruptions in frame-level transitions. Building on this, we propose TRACE (Training-free Representation-based Audio Countermeasure via Embedding dynamics), a training-free framework that detects partial audio deepfakes by analyzing the first-order dynamics of frozen speech foundation model representations without any training, labeled data, or architectural modification. We evaluate TRACE on four benchmarks that span two languages using six speech foundation models. In PartialSpoof, TRACE achieves 8.08% EER, competitive with fine-tuned supervised baselines. In LlamaPartialSpoof, the most challenging benchmark featuring LLM-driven commercial synthesis, TRACE surpasses a supervised baseline outright (24.12% vs. 24.49% EER) without any target-domain data. These results show that temporal dynamics in speech foundation models provide an effective, generalize signal for training-free audio forensics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRACE：通过语音基础模型嵌入轨迹分析实现无训练的局部音频深度伪造检测
局部音频深度伪造（合成片段拼接到真实录音中）尤其具有欺骗性，因为大部分音频仍为真实。现有检测器依赖监督学习：需要帧级标注、易过拟合特定合成流程，且需随新生成模型出现而重新训练。我们认为这种监督是不必要的。我们假设语音基础模型隐含编码了法证信号：真实语音形成平滑、缓慢变化的嵌入轨迹，而拼接边界则在帧级过渡中引入突变。基于此，我们提出TRACE（无训练的基于表示的音频对抗方法，通过嵌入动态分析），该框架无需训练、无需标注数据、无需架构修改，仅通过冻结的语音基础模型表示的一阶动态即可检测局部音频深度伪造。我们在涵盖两种语言的四个基准上，使用六个语音基础模型评估TRACE。在PartialSpoof中，TRACE达到8.08%的EER，与微调的监督基线相当；在最具挑战性的LlamaPartialSpoof（LLM驱动商业合成）中，TRACE无需目标域数据即超越监督基线（24.12% vs. 24.49% EER）。结果表明，语音基础模型中的时序动态提供了有效的、可泛化的无训练音频法证信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the deceptive nature of partial audio deepfakes and the limitations of supervised detection methods, the authors propose TRACE, a training-free framework that leverages the temporal dynamics of frozen speech foundation model embeddings to detect spliced synthetic audio. By analyzing abrupt changes in frame-level embedding trajectories near splice boundaries—where genuine speech exhibits smooth transitions—TRACE identifies deepfakes without requiring labeled data, retraining, or model modification. Evaluated across four benchmarks using six speech foundation models, TRACE achieves competitive performance: 8.08% EER on PartialSpoof and outperforms a supervised baseline (24.12% vs. 24.49% EER) on the challenging LlamaPartialSpoof dataset, demonstrating its generalization capability without target-domain adaptation.
针对现有监督式深伪音频检测器依赖标注数据且需针对新合成模型重训练的问题，本文提出TRACE方法，利用冻结的语音基础模型嵌入的时序动态特征进行无训练检测。该方法通过分析帧级嵌入轨迹，识别拼接边界处的突变作为取证线索，无需训练或架构修改。在四个跨语言基准测试中，使用六个语音模型评估，TRACE在PartialSpoof上达到8.08%误报率，在最具挑战性的LlamaPartialSpoof上以24.12%误报率超越监督基线（24.49%），验证了其无需目标域数据即可泛化的有效性。
ReMoGen: Real-time Human Interaction-to-Reaction Generation via Modular Learning from Diverse Data
Authors: Yaoqin Ye, Yiteng Xu, Qin Sun, Xinge Zhu, Yujing Sun, Yuexin Ma
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-01T16:12:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:12:23+00:00
Comments: accepted by CVPR 2026, project page: https://4dvlab.github.io/project_page/remogen/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Human behaviors in real-world environments are inherently interactive, with an individual's motion shaped by surrounding agents and the scene. Such capabilities are essential for applications in virtual avatars, interactive animation, and human-robot collaboration. We target real-time human interaction-to-reaction generation, which generates the ego's future motion from dynamic multi-source cues, including others' actions, scene geometry, and optional high-level semantic inputs. This task is fundamentally challenging due to (i) limited and fragmented interaction data distributed across heterogeneous single-person, human-human, and human-scene domains, and (ii) the need to produce low-latency yet high-fidelity motion responses during continuous online interaction. To address these challenges, we propose ReMoGen (Reaction Motion Generation), a modular learning framework for real-time interaction-to-reaction generation. ReMoGen leverages a universal motion prior learned from large-scale single-person motion datasets and adapts it to target interaction domains through independently trained Meta-Interaction modules, enabling robust generalization under data-scarce and heterogeneous supervision. To support responsive online interaction, ReMoGen performs segment-level generation together with a lightweight Frame-wise Segment Refinement module that incorporates newly observed cues at the frame level, improving both responsiveness and temporal coherence without expensive full-sequence inference. Extensive experiments across human-human, human-scene, and mixed-modality interaction settings show that ReMoGen produces high-quality, coherent, and responsive reactions, while generalizing effectively across diverse interaction scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReMoGen：通过模块化学习从多样化数据中实时生成人类交互反应
现实世界中的人类行为具有内在交互性，个体运动受周围代理和场景影响。这种能力对虚拟化身、交互式动画和人机协作等应用至关重要。我们致力于实时交互到反应生成，即从动态多源线索（包括他人动作、场景几何结构及可选高层语义输入）生成自我的未来运动。该任务因以下两点根本性挑战而困难：(i) 交互数据有限且碎片化，分散在异构的单人、人-人、人-场景领域；(ii) 在持续在线交互中需生成低延迟且高保真度的运动响应。为应对这些挑战，我们提出ReMoGen（反应运动生成），一种用于实时交互到反应生成的模块化学习框架。ReMoGen利用从大规模单人运动数据集中学习到的通用运动先验，并通过独立训练的Meta-交互模块将其适配至目标交互领域，从而在数据稀缺和异构监督条件下实现稳健泛化。为支持响应式在线交互，ReMoGen结合轻量级帧级段落精炼模块，在帧级整合新观察到的线索，提升响应速度与时间一致性，无需昂贵的全序列推理。在人-人、人-场景及混合模态交互场景的广泛实验表明，ReMoGen生成高质量、连贯且响应迅速的反应，并能有效泛化至多样交互场景。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time, context-aware motion generation in interactive environments, ReMoGen addresses the challenge of generating accurate human reactions from fragmented, multi-source interaction data. It employs a modular learning framework that combines a universal motion prior from large single-person datasets with domain-specific Meta-Interaction modules trained independently for human-human and human-scene scenarios. To ensure low-latency and high-fidelity responses during continuous interaction, ReMoGen uses segment-level motion generation paired with a lightweight frame-wise refinement module that integrates new cues incrementally. Experimental results across diverse interaction settings demonstrate that ReMoGen produces temporally coherent, responsive motions while maintaining strong generalization across data-scarce and heterogeneous domains.
为实现真实环境中实时、情境感知的人类动作生成，ReMoGen针对从碎片化、异构交互数据中生成低延迟、高保真反应动作的挑战，提出模块化学习框架。该框架结合大规模单人动作数据学习的通用运动先验，与独立训练的领域特定Meta-Interaction模块，支持人类-人类、人类-场景及混合模态场景下的泛化能力。为支持在线响应，ReMoGen采用分段级动作生成，配合轻量级帧级精炼模块，逐帧整合新观测信息，保持时间连贯性而无需全序列重计算。实验表明，ReMoGen在多种交互场景下生成连贯、响应迅速的动作，且在数据稀缺和异构监督条件下仍具备良好泛化能力。
ProOOD: Prototype-Guided Out-of-Distribution 3D Occupancy Prediction
Authors: Yuheng Zhang, Mengfei Duan, Kunyu Peng, Yuhang Wang, Di Wen, Danda Pani Paudel, Luc Van Gool, Kailun Yang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-01T16:11:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:11:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/7uHeng/ProOOD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
3D semantic occupancy prediction is central to autonomous driving, yet current methods are vulnerable to long-tailed class bias and out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs, often overconfidently assigning anomalies to rare classes. We present ProOOD, a lightweight, plug-and-play method that couples prototype-guided refinement with training-free OOD scoring. ProOOD comprises (i) prototype-guided semantic imputation that fills occluded regions with class-consistent features, (ii) prototype-guided tail mining that strengthens rare-class representations to curb OOD absorption, and (iii) EchoOOD, which fuses local logit coherence with local and global prototype matching to produce reliable voxel-level OOD scores. Extensive experiments on five datasets demonstrate that ProOOD achieves state-of-the-art performance on both in-distribution 3D occupancy prediction and OOD detection. On SemanticKITTI, it surpasses baselines by +3.57% mIoU overall and +24.80% tail-class mIoU; on VAA-KITTI, it improves AuPRCr by +19.34 points, with consistent gains across benchmarks. These improvements yield more calibrated occupancy estimates and more reliable OOD detection in safety-critical urban driving. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/7uHeng/ProOOD.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ProOOD：原型引导的分布外3D语义占据预测
3D语义占据预测是自动驾驶的核心任务，但现有方法易受长尾类别偏差和分布外（OOD）输入影响，常过度自信地将异常分配给稀有类别。我们提出ProOOD，一种轻量级、即插即用的方法，结合原型引导的精炼与无训练OOD评分。ProOOD包含：(i) 原型引导语义填充，用类别一致特征填补遮挡区域；(ii) 原型引导尾部挖掘，强化稀有类别表征以抑制OOD吸收；(iii) EchoOOD，融合局部logit一致性与局部及全局原型匹配，生成可靠的体素级OOD评分。在五个数据集上的大量实验表明，ProOOD在分布内3D占据预测和OOD检测上均达到最先进性能。在SemanticKITTI上，整体mIoU提升3.57%，尾部类别mIoU提升24.80%；在VAA-KITTI上，AuPRCr提升19.34点，各基准测试均保持一致增益。这些改进提升了安全关键城市驾驶中的占据估计校准度和OOD检测可靠性。源代码公开于https://github.com/7uHeng/ProOOD。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust 3D occupancy prediction in autonomous driving that handles rare classes and out-of-distribution inputs without overconfidence, ProOOD introduces a lightweight, plug-and-play framework combining prototype-guided refinement and training-free OOD scoring. It employs semantic imputation to fill occluded regions with class-consistent features, tail mining to enhance rare-class representations and reduce OOD absorption, and EchoOOD to generate voxel-level OOD scores via local logit coherence and prototype matching. Experiments across five datasets show ProOOD achieves state-of-the-art results: +3.57% overall mIoU and +24.80% tail-class mIoU on SemanticKITTI, and +19.34 points in AuPRCr on VAA-KITTI, with consistent gains across benchmarks, leading to more calibrated predictions and reliable OOD detection in urban driving scenarios.
为解决3D语义占据预测模型在长尾类别偏差和分布外输入下的脆弱性，ProOOD提出了一种轻量级、即插即用框架，结合原型引导的精炼与无训练分布外评分。该方法通过原型引导的语义填充填补遮挡区域、原型引导的尾部挖掘强化稀有类别表征以减少分布外误分类，并通过EchoOOD融合局部logit一致性与局部全局原型匹配生成体素级分布外评分。在五个数据集上的实验表明，ProOOD达到当前最优性能：在SemanticKITTI上整体mIoU提升3.57%，尾部类别mIoU提升24.80%；在VAA-KITTI上AuPRCr提升19.34点，各基准测试均保持稳定增益，从而实现更校准的占据预测与更可靠的分布外检测，适用于城市驾驶等安全关键场景。
LG-HCC: Local Geometry-Aware Hierarchical Context Compression for 3D Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Xuan Deng, Xiandong Meng, Hengyu Man, Qiang Zhu, Tiange Zhang, Debin Zhao, Xiaopeng Fan
First: 2026-03-30T13:39:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:10:38+00:00
Comments: 10
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity real-time rendering, its prohibitive storage overhead severely hinders practical deployment. Recent anchor-based 3DGS compression schemes reduce gaussian redundancy through some advanced context models. However, they overlook explicit geometric dependencies, leading to structural degradation and suboptimal ratedistortion performance. In this paper, we propose a Local Geometry-aware Hierarchical Context Compression framework for 3DGS(LG-HCC) that incorporates inter-anchor geometric correlations into anchor pruning and entropy coding for compact representation. Specifically, we introduce an Neighborhood-Aware Anchor Pruning (NAAP) strategy, which evaluates anchor importance via weighted neighborhood feature aggregation and then merges low-contribution anchors into salient neighbors, yielding a compact yet geometry-consistent anchor set. Moreover, we further develop a hierarchical entropy coding scheme, in which coarse-to-fine priors are exploited through a lightweight Geometry-Guided Convolution(GG-Conv) operator to enable spatially adaptive context modeling and rate-distortion optimization. Extensive experiments show that LG-HCC effectively alleviates structural preservation issues,achieving superior geometric integrity and rendering fidelity while reducing storage by up to 30.85x compared to the Scaffold-GS baseline on the Mip-NeRF360 dataset
中文标题/摘要
标题：LG-HCC：面向3D高斯点渲染的局部几何感知分层上下文压缩
尽管3D高斯点渲染（3DGS）支持高保真实时渲染，但其存储开销过大严重阻碍了实际部署。近期基于锚点的3DGS压缩方案通过高级上下文模型减少高斯冗余，但忽略了显式的几何依赖关系，导致结构退化和率失真性能不佳。本文提出一种局部几何感知分层上下文压缩框架LG-HCC，将锚点间的几何相关性融入锚点剪枝与熵编码，以实现紧凑表示。具体而言，我们引入邻域感知锚点剪枝（NAAP）策略，通过加权邻域特征聚合评估锚点重要性，再将低贡献锚点合并至显著邻域，生成紧凑且几何一致的锚点集。此外，我们进一步开发分层熵编码方案，通过轻量级几何引导卷积（GG-Conv）操作利用粗到细先验，实现空间自适应上下文建模与率失真优化。大量实验表明，LG-HCC有效缓解结构保持问题，在Mip-NeRF360数据集上相比Scaffold-GS基线存储量减少高达30.85倍，同时保持更优的几何完整性与渲染保真度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high storage cost of 3D Gaussian Splatting despite its rendering quality, this paper introduces LG-HCC, a framework that enhances compression by leveraging local geometric dependencies. It employs Neighborhood-Aware Anchor Pruning to selectively merge low-importance anchors into salient neighbors, preserving structural integrity, and uses a hierarchical entropy coding scheme with Geometry-Guided Convolution to adaptively model context and optimize rate-distortion. Experiments on Mip-NeRF360 show LG-HCC achieves up to 30.85x storage reduction while maintaining superior geometric fidelity and rendering quality compared to Scaffold-GS.
为解决3D高斯点渲染存储开销过大的问题，本文提出LG-HCC框架，通过引入局部几何感知机制提升压缩效果。该框架采用邻域感知锚点剪枝策略，将低贡献锚点合并至关键邻域点，保留结构完整性，并结合层次化熵编码与几何引导卷积算子实现自适应上下文建模与率失真优化。实验表明，LG-HCC在Mip-NeRF360数据集上较Scaffold-GS实现最高30.85倍的存储压缩，同时保持更优的几何保真度与渲染质量。
Learning When the Concept Shifts: Confounding, Invariance, and Dimension Reduction
Authors: Kulunu Dharmakeerthi, YoonHaeng Hur, Tengyuan Liang
First: 2024-06-22T17:43:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:08:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Practitioners often face the challenge of deploying prediction models in new environments with shifted distributions of covariates and responses. With observational data, such shifts are often driven by unobserved confounding, and can in fact alter the concept of which model is best. This paper studies distribution shifts in the domain adaptation problem with unobserved confounding. We postulate a linear structural causal model to account for endogeneity and unobserved confounding, and we leverage exogenous invariant covariate representations to cure concept shifts and improve target prediction. We propose a data-driven representation learning method that optimizes for a lower-dimensional linear subspace and a prediction model confined to that subspace. This method operates on a non-convex objective -- that interpolates between predictability and stability -- constrained to the Stiefel manifold, using an analog of projected gradient descent. We analyze the optimization landscape and prove that, provided sufficient regularization, nearly all local optima align with an invariant linear subspace resilient to distribution shifts. This method achieves a nearly ideal gap between target and source risk. We validate the method and theory with real-world data sets to illustrate the tradeoffs between predictability and stability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：概念迁移时的学习：混淆、不变性与降维
从业者常面临在协变量和响应变量分布发生偏移的新环境中部署预测模型的挑战。在观测数据中，此类偏移通常由未观测混淆因素驱动，甚至可能改变最优模型的概念。本文研究了在存在未观测混淆因素的领域自适应问题中的分布偏移。我们假设线性结构因果模型以解释内生性与未观测混淆因素，并利用外生不变协变量表示来解决概念偏移并提升目标预测性能。我们提出一种数据驱动的表示学习方法，该方法优化低维线性子空间及限定于该子空间的预测模型。该方法在Stiefel流形上操作一个非凸目标——在可预测性与稳定性之间进行插值——使用投影梯度下降的类比方法。我们分析了优化景观并证明：在充分正则化条件下，几乎所有局部最优解均与一个对分布偏移鲁棒的不变线性子空间一致。该方法实现了目标风险与源风险之间近乎理想的差距。我们通过真实数据集验证了该方法与理论，以说明可预测性与稳定性之间的权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of deploying models in environments with distribution shifts caused by unobserved confounding, this paper proposes a method based on linear structural causal models to identify invariant covariate representations that remain stable across domains. The approach learns a low-dimensional linear subspace via a non-convex optimization over the Stiefel manifold, balancing predictability and stability using projected gradient descent. Theoretical analysis shows that with sufficient regularization, most local optima correspond to invariant subspaces resilient to distribution shifts, leading to nearly ideal performance on target data while maintaining robustness to source-target discrepancies.
为应对因未观测混杂因素导致的分布偏移问题，本文提出基于线性结构因果模型的方法，通过学习不变的协变量表示来缓解概念漂移。该方法在Stiefel流形上优化非凸目标函数，平衡预测能力和稳定性，采用投影梯度下降类算法求解低维线性子空间。理论分析表明，在充分正则化下，大部分局部最优解对应于对分布偏移鲁棒的不变子空间，从而实现源域与目标域风险接近理想差距，实验证实了该方法在真实数据集上的有效性。
Narrative Fingerprints: Multi-Scale Author Identification via Novelty Curve Dynamics
Authors: Fred Zimmerman, Hilmar AI
First: 2026-04-01T16:07:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:07:58+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 6 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We test whether authors have characteristic "fingerprints" in the information-theoretic novelty curves of their published works. Working with two corpora -- Books3 (52,796 books, 759 qualifying authors) and PG-19 (28,439 books, 1,821 qualifying authors) -- we find that authorial voice leaves measurable traces in how novelty unfolds across a text. The signal is multi-scale: at book level, scalar dynamics (mean novelty, speed, volume, circuitousness) identify 43% of authors significantly above chance; at chapter level, SAX motif patterns in sliding windows achieve 30x-above-chance attribution, far exceeding the scalar features that dominate at book level. These signals are complementary, not redundant. We show that the fingerprint is partly confounded with genre but persists within-genre for approximately one-quarter of authors. Classical authors (Twain, Austen, Kipling) show fingerprints comparable in strength to modern authors, suggesting the phenomenon is not an artifact of contemporary publishing conventions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：叙事指纹：通过新颖性曲线动态实现多尺度作者识别
我们检验作者在其出版作品的信息论新颖性曲线中是否具有特征性的“指纹”。通过对两个语料库——Books3（52,796本书，759位合格作者）和PG-19（28,439本书，1,821位合格作者）——的研究，我们发现作者风格在文本新颖性展开方式中留下了可测量的痕迹。该信号具有多尺度特性：在书本层面，标量动态（平均新颖性、速度、体量、迂回度）可显著高于随机水平地识别43%的作者；在章节层面，滑动窗口中的SAX模式可实现30倍于随机水平的归因，远超书本层面主导的标量特征。这些信号互补而非冗余。我们表明，该指纹部分与体裁相关，但在体裁内仍约有四分之一作者的指纹持续存在。经典作家（如马克·吐温、奥斯汀、吉卜林）的指纹强度与现代作家相当，表明该现象并非当代出版惯例的产物。
