This study investigates whether authors exhibit unique 'fingerprints' in the information-theoretic novelty curves of their texts, using two large corpora: Books3 and PG-19. The authors analyze scalar metrics like mean novelty and speed at the book level, which identify 43% of authors significantly above chance, and SAX motif patterns at the chapter level, which achieve 30x-above-chance attribution. These multi-scale signals are complementary, not redundant, and while partially confounded by genre, they persist for about a quarter of authors within genre. Notably, classical authors such as Twain and Austen show fingerprint strength comparable to modern authors, indicating the phenomenon is not tied to contemporary publishing norms.
为探究作者风格是否在文本新颖性动态中留下可测量的痕迹，本研究分析了Books3和PG-19两个大型语料库，发现作者指纹在多尺度上存在：在书级，如平均新颖性、速度等标量特征可显著识别43%以上的作者；在章级，滑动窗口中的SAX模式特征识别准确率高出随机水平30倍，优于书级特征。两类信号互补而非冗余，尽管体裁部分干扰指纹识别，但约四分之一作者在同体裁内仍具稳定指纹。经典作家如马克·吐温、奥斯汀等的指纹强度与现代作家相当，表明该现象并非当代出版的产物。
BAT: Balancing Agility and Stability via Online Policy Switching for Long-Horizon Whole-Body Humanoid Control
Authors: Donghoon Baek, Sang-Hun Kim, Sehoon Ha
First: 2026-04-01T16:03:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:03:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite recent advances in control, reinforcement learning, and imitation learning, developing a unified framework that can achieve agile, precise, and robust whole-body behaviors, particularly in long-horizon tasks, remains challenging. Existing approaches typically follow two paradigms: coupled whole-body policies for global coordination and decoupled policies for modular precision. However, without a systematic method to integrate both, this trade-off between agility, robustness, and precision remains unresolved. In this work, we propose BAT, an online policy-switching framework that dynamically selects between two complementary whole-body RL controllers to balance agility and stability across different motion contexts. Our framework consists of two complementary modules: a switching policy learned via hierarchical RL with an expert guidance from sliding-horizon policy pre-evaluation, and an option-aware VQ-VAE that predicts option preference from discrete motion token sequences for improved generalization. The final decision is obtained via confidence-weighted fusion of two modules. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot demonstrate that BAT enables versatile long-horizon loco-manipulation and outperforms prior methods across diverse tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BAT：通过在线策略切换在长时域全身人形机器人控制中平衡敏捷性与稳定性
尽管控制、强化学习和模仿学习领域取得了最新进展，但构建一个能够实现敏捷、精准且鲁棒的全身行为的统一框架，特别是在长时域任务中，仍具挑战性。现有方法通常遵循两种范式：耦合全身策略用于全局协调，解耦策略用于模块化精度。然而，缺乏系统方法整合二者，导致敏捷性、鲁棒性与精度之间的权衡仍未解决。本文提出BAT，一种在线策略切换框架，动态选择两种互补的全身强化学习控制器，以在不同运动情境下平衡敏捷性与稳定性。该框架包含两个互补模块：一种通过分层强化学习结合滑动窗口策略预评估专家指导学习的切换策略；以及一种基于离散运动令牌序列预测选项偏好的选项感知VQ-VAE，以提升泛化能力。最终决策通过两个模块的置信度加权融合获得。大量仿真与Unitree G1人形机器人真实实验表明，BAT实现了多功能长时域移动-操作能力，并在多种任务中优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of achieving agile, precise, and robust whole-body control in long-horizon tasks despite advances in RL and imitation learning, this work introduces BAT, an online policy-switching framework that dynamically balances agility and stability by selecting between two complementary RL controllers. The method combines a hierarchical RL-based switching policy guided by expert pre-evaluation and an option-aware VQ-VAE that predicts motion preferences from discrete tokens, with final decisions fused via confidence weighting. Experimental results on the Unitree G1 robot show BAT enables versatile long-horizon locomotion and manipulation, outperforming prior approaches across diverse tasks.
为解决长时程全身体控制中敏捷性与稳定性难以兼顾的问题，本文提出BAT框架，通过在线切换两种互补的全身体强化学习控制器实现动态平衡。该方法结合基于分层强化学习的切换策略（由滑动窗口策略预评估引导）与基于离散运动令牌序列的选项感知VQ-VAE（提升泛化能力），最终决策通过置信度加权融合得出。在Unitree G1人形机器人上的实验表明，BAT在多种长时程运动任务中显著优于现有方法。
Data-Model Co-Driven Continuous Channel Map Construction: A Perceptive Foundation for Embodied Intelligent Agents in 6G Networks
Authors: Tianrun Qi, Cheng-Xiang Wang, Chen Huang, Junling Li, John S Thompson
First: 2026-04-01T16:00:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T16:00:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Future 6G networks will host massive numbers of embodied intelligent agents, which require real-time channel awareness over continuous-space for autonomous decision-making. By pre-obtaining location-specific channel state information (CSI), channel map can be served as a foundational world model for embodied intelligence to achieve wireless channel perception. However, acquiring CSI via measurements is costly, so in practice only sparse observations are available, leaving agents blind to channel conditions at unvisited locations. Meanwhile, purely model-driven channel maps can provide dense CSI but often yields unsatisfactory accuracy and robustness, while purely data-driven interpolation from sparse measurements is computationally prohibitive for real-time updates. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a data-model co-driven (DMcD) framework that performs a two-stage interpolation toward a space-time continuous channel map, First, a hybrid ray tracing and geometry-based channel model (H-RT/GBSM) is developed to capture dynamic scatterers, providing dense, time-variant channel properties that match measurement statistics as a physically consistent prior. Then, an inductive edge-conditioned graph neural network (InductE-GNN) fuses the prior with sparse measurements to perform real-time spatial interpolation, enabling rapid online adaptation without retraining, ensuring the synchronization with the dynamic physical reality. Evaluations with measured datasets show that the proposed DMcD framework significantly outperforms data-only and model-only baselines, providing accurate and queryable channel information for embodied intelligent agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：数据-模型协同驱动的连续信道图构建：6G网络中具身智能体的感知基础
未来6G网络将承载大量具身智能体，其自主决策需实时感知连续空间中的信道状态。通过预先获取位置特定的信道状态信息（CSI），信道图可作为具身智能的基础世界模型以实现无线信道感知。然而，通过测量获取CSI成本高昂，实践中仅能获得稀疏观测，导致智能体对未访问位置的信道状况无从知晓。同时，纯模型驱动的信道图虽可提供密集CSI，但常存在精度与鲁棒性不足的问题；而纯数据驱动的稀疏测量插值则因计算开销大，难以满足实时更新需求。为此，本文提出一种数据-模型协同驱动（DMcD）框架，通过两阶段插值构建时空连续信道图：首先，构建混合射线追踪与几何基础信道模型（H-RT/GBSM），捕捉动态散射体，提供与测量统计相匹配的物理一致先验的密集、时变信道特性；其次，采用归纳式边条件图神经网络（InductE-GNN），融合先验与稀疏测量，实现实时空间插值，支持无需重训练的快速在线自适应，确保与动态物理现实同步。实验表明，所提DMcD框架显著优于纯数据或纯模型基线，为具身智能体提供准确且可查询的信道信息。
Summary / 总结
To support real-time channel awareness for embodied agents in future 6G networks, this paper addresses the challenge of sparse channel measurements by proposing a data-model co-driven framework. It combines a hybrid ray tracing and geometry-based channel model (H-RT/GBSM) to generate dense, time-variant channel priors that align with real-world statistics, and an inductive edge-conditioned graph neural network (InductE-GNN) to fuse these priors with sparse measurements for real-time spatial interpolation. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach significantly outperforms purely data-driven or model-driven baselines in accuracy and robustness, enabling efficient, queryable channel maps for embodied agents operating in dynamic environments.
为使6G网络中的具身智能体能实现基于实时信道感知的自主决策，本文提出一种数据-模型协同驱动（DMcD）框架，解决从稀疏测量构建连续空间信道地图的难题。该框架结合混合射线追踪与几何信道模型（H-RT/GBSM）生成密集、时变的物理一致先验信道信息，并通过诱导边条件图神经网络（InductE-GNN）融合稀疏测量数据，实现在线实时空间插值。实验表明，该方法在精度与鲁棒性上显著优于纯数据驱动或纯模型驱动基线，可为动态环境中运行的智能体提供高效、可查询的信道状态信息。
VMAD: Visual-enhanced Multimodal Large Language Model for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection
Authors: Huilin Deng, Hongchen Luo, Wei Zhai, Yang Cao, Yu Kang
First: 2024-09-30T09:51:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:59:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) recognizes and localizes anomalies in previously unseen objects by establishing feature mapping between textual prompts and inspection images, demonstrating excellent research value in flexible industrial manufacturing. However, existing ZSAD methods are limited by closed-world settings, struggling to unseen defects with predefined prompts. Recently, adapting Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) presents a viable solution. Unlike fixed-prompt methods, MLLMs exhibit a generative paradigm with open-ended text interpretation, enabling more adaptive anomaly analysis. However, this adaption faces inherent challenges as anomalies often manifest in fine-grained regions and exhibit minimal visual discrepancies from normal samples. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework VMAD (Visual-enhanced MLLM Anomaly Detection) that enhances MLLM with visual-based IAD knowledge and fine-grained perception, simultaneously providing precise detection and comprehensive analysis of anomalies. Specifically, we design a Defect-Sensitive Structure Learning scheme that transfers patch-similarities cues from visual branch to our MLLM for improved anomaly discrimination. Besides, we introduce a novel visual projector, Locality-enhanced Token Compression, which mines multi-level features in local contexts to enhance fine-grained detection. Furthermore, we introduce the Real Industrial Anomaly Detection (RIAD), a comprehensive IAD dataset with detailed anomaly descriptions and analyses, offering a valuable resource for MLLM-based IAD development. Extensive experiments on zero-shot benchmarks, including MVTec-AD, Visa, WFDD, and RIAD datasets, demonstrate our superior performance over state-of-the-art methods. The code and dataset will be available soon.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VMAD：视觉增强的多模态大语言模型用于零样本异常检测
零样本异常检测（ZSAD）通过建立文本提示与检测图像之间的特征映射，识别并定位之前未见过的对象，其在柔性工业制造中具有显著研究价值。然而，现有ZSAD方法受限于封闭世界设定，难以处理预定义提示无法覆盖的未知缺陷。近期，将多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）应用于工业异常检测（IAD）提供了一种可行方案。与固定提示方法不同，MLLMs具备开放式的文本生成能力，可实现更自适应的异常分析。但该适应过程面临固有挑战：异常常表现为细粒度区域且与正常样本视觉差异极小。为此，我们提出一种新颖框架VMAD（视觉增强的MLLM异常检测），通过融合基于视觉的IAD知识与细粒度感知能力，同时实现异常的精准检测与全面分析。具体而言，我们设计了一种缺陷敏感结构学习方案，将视觉分支中的块相似性线索迁移至MLLM以提升异常判别能力；此外，引入一种新型视觉投影器——局部增强令牌压缩，挖掘局部上下文中的多层次特征以增强细粒度检测能力。同时，我们构建了真实工业异常检测（RIAD）数据集，包含详尽的异常描述与分析，为基于MLLM的IAD研究提供宝贵资源。在MVTec-AD、Visa、WFDD及RIAD等多个零样本基准数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法显著优于现有最先进方法。代码与数据集将很快公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible anomaly detection in industrial settings without predefined defect prompts, this paper proposes VMAD, a visual-enhanced multimodal large language model framework designed for zero-shot anomaly detection. It leverages MLLMs’ generative capabilities while integrating visual-based industrial anomaly knowledge through a Defect-Sensitive Structure Learning scheme that transfers patch-similarities from visual branches to improve anomaly discrimination. Additionally, a Locality-enhanced Token Compression module mines multi-level local features to enhance fine-grained detection. Evaluated on multiple zero-shot benchmarks including MVTec-AD, Visa, WFDD, and a newly introduced RIAD dataset, VMAD consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in both detection accuracy and localization precision.
为解决工业场景中零样本异常检测缺乏预定义缺陷提示的问题，本文提出VMAD框架，通过融合视觉信息增强多模态大语言模型的异常检测能力。该方法设计缺陷敏感结构学习机制，将视觉块相似性信息注入MLLM以提升异常判别能力，并引入局部增强的令牌压缩模块以捕捉细粒度局部特征。在MVTec-AD、Visa、WFDD及新构建的RIAD等多个零样本基准数据集上，VMAD在检测精度与定位准确性方面均显著优于现有最先进方法。
PHASOR: Anatomy- and Phase-Consistent Volumetric Diffusion for CT Virtual Contrast Enhancement
Authors: Zilong Li, Dongyang Li, Chenglong Ma, Zhan Feng, Dakai Jin, Junping Zhang, Hao Luo, Fan Wang, Hongming Shan
First: 2026-04-01T15:57:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:57:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Contrast-enhanced computed tomography (CECT) is pivotal for highlighting tissue perfusion and vascularity, yet its clinical ubiquity is impeded by the invasive nature of contrast agents and radiation risks. While virtual contrast enhancement (VCE) offers an alternative to synthesizing CECT from non-contrast CT (NCCT), existing methods struggle with anatomical heterogeneity and spatial misalignment, leading to inconsistent enhancement patterns and incorrect details. This paper introduces PHASOR, a volumetric diffusion framework for high-fidelity CT VCE. By treating CT volumes as coherent sequences, we leverage a video diffusion model to enhance structural coherence and volumetric accuracy. To ensure anatomy-phase consistent synthesis, we introduce two complementary modules. First, anatomy-routed mixture-of-experts (AR-MoE) anchors distinct enhancement patterns to anatomical semantics, with organ-specific memory to capture salient details. Second, intensity-phase aware representation alignment (IP-REPA) highlights intricate contrast signals while mitigating the impact of imperfect spatial alignment. Extensive experiments across three datasets demonstrate that PHASOR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both synthesis quality and enhancement accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PHASOR：基于解剖与相位一致的体体积扩散CT虚拟增强对比度
对比增强计算机断层扫描（CECT）对突出组织灌注和血管性至关重要，但其临床广泛应用受限于对比剂的侵入性和辐射风险。虽然虚拟对比增强（VCE）可从非对比CT（NCCT）合成CECT，但现有方法难以应对解剖异质性和空间错位，导致增强模式不一致和细节错误。本文提出PHASOR，一种高保真CT VCE的体体积扩散框架。将CT体数据视为连贯序列，利用视频扩散模型提升结构一致性和体积精度。为确保解剖-相位一致合成，引入两个互补模块：首先，解剖导向混合专家（AR-MoE）将不同增强模式锚定至解剖语义，并通过器官特异性记忆捕捉显著细节；其次，强度-相位感知表示对齐（IP-REPA）突出复杂对比信号，同时减轻空间对齐不完美的影响。在三个数据集上的大量实验表明，PHASOR在合成质量和增强精度上显著优于现有最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the clinical limitations of contrast agents in computed tomography, this work proposes PHASOR, a volumetric diffusion model for virtual contrast enhancement. It treats CT volumes as coherent sequences and employs a video diffusion framework to preserve anatomical structure and spatial consistency. Two key modules—AR-MoE for anatomy-guided enhancement and IP-REPA for phase-aware intensity alignment—ensure accurate, organ-specific contrast synthesis despite misalignment. Experiments across three datasets confirm PHASOR’s superior performance in both visual quality and quantitative enhancement accuracy compared to existing methods.
为避免临床中对比剂注射和辐射风险，本文提出PHASOR，一种基于体素扩散的虚拟增强CT合成方法。该方法将3D CT体数据视为连贯序列，利用视频扩散框架保持结构与空间一致性。通过两个模块——基于解剖结构的混合专家模块（AR-MoE）和强度-相位感知对齐模块（IP-REPA），实现解剖学准确且相位一致的增强效果。在三个数据集上的实验表明，PHASOR在合成质量与增强精度上显著优于现有方法。
VibeGuard: A Security Gate Framework for AI-Generated Code
Authors: Ying Xie
First: 2026-04-01T15:57:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:57:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
"Vibe coding," in which developers delegate code generation to AI assistants and accept the output with little manual review, has gained rapid adoption in production settings. On March 31, 2026, Anthropic's Claude Code CLI shipped a 59.8 MB source map file in its npm package, exposing roughly 512,000 lines of proprietary TypeScript. The tool had itself been largely vibe-coded, and the leak traced to a misconfigured packaging rule rather than a logic bug. Existing static-analysis and secret-scanning tools did not cover this failure mode, pointing to a gap between the vulnerabilities AI tends to introduce and the vulnerabilities current tooling is built to find. We present VibeGuard, a pre-publish security gate that targets five such blind spots: artifact hygiene, packaging-configuration drift, source-map exposure, hardcoded secrets, and supply-chain risk. In controlled experiments on eight synthetic projects (seven vulnerable, one clean control), VibeGuard achieved 100% recall, 89.47% precision (F1 = 94.44%), and correct pass/fail gate decisions on all eight projects across three policy levels. We discuss how these results inform a defense-in-depth workflow for teams that rely on AI code generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VibeGuard：面向AI生成代码的安全门框架
“Vibe编码”指开发者将代码生成任务委托给AI助手，并仅进行少量手动审查，该模式已在生产环境中迅速普及。2026年3月31日，Anthropic的Claude Code CLI在其npm包中发布了一个59.8MB的源映射文件，泄露了约51.2万行专有TypeScript代码。该工具本身主要采用Vibe编码方式，泄露根源在于打包配置错误而非逻辑漏洞。现有静态分析和密钥扫描工具未能覆盖此类失效模式，揭示了AI倾向于引入的漏洞与当前工具设计检测的漏洞之间存在差距。我们提出VibeGuard，一种发布前的安全门机制，针对五类盲区：构件卫生、打包配置漂移、源映射暴露、硬编码密钥及供应链风险。在八个合成项目（七个存在漏洞，一个干净对照组）的受控实验中，VibeGuard在三个策略层级上实现了100%召回率、89.47%精确率（F1=94.44%），并正确判定所有八个项目通过/失败。我们探讨了这些结果如何为依赖AI代码生成的团队提供纵深防御工作流。

Motivated by the risks of AI-generated code being deployed without sufficient review, the authors introduce VibeGuard, a pre-publish security gate designed to catch five specific vulnerabilities common in AI-assisted development: artifact hygiene, packaging drift, source-map exposure, hardcoded secrets, and supply-chain risk. Evaluated on eight synthetic projects—seven vulnerable and one clean control—VibeGuard achieved 100% recall, 89.47% precision (F1 = 94.44%), and correctly classified all projects across three policy levels, demonstrating its effectiveness in identifying real-world blind spots missed by existing static analysis and secret-scanning tools.
随着开发者大量依赖AI生成代码而减少人工审查的‘vibe coding’模式兴起，研究人员发现现有工具难以检测由AI引入的特定安全漏洞，例如因配置错误导致的源码映射泄露。VibeGuard是一个发布前安全门，专门针对五类盲区：工件卫生、打包配置漂移、源码映射暴露、硬编码密钥和供应链风险。在八个合成项目（七个有漏洞，一个干净对照组）的实验中，它实现了100%召回率、89.47%精确率（F1=94.44%），并在三种策略级别下对所有项目做出正确通过或拒绝决策，证明其能有效捕捉传统静态分析工具遗漏的AI相关漏洞。
Learning Hyperparameters via a Data-Emphasized Variational Objective
Authors: Ethan Harvey, Mikhail Petrov, Michael C. Hughes
First: 2025-02-03T22:19:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:56:36+00:00
Comments: arXiv admin note: text overlap with arXiv:2410.19675
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When training large models on limited data, avoiding overfitting is paramount. Common grid search or smarter search methods rely on expensive separate runs for each candidate hyperparameter, while carving out a validation set that reduces available training data. In this paper, we study gradient-based learning of hyperparameters via the evidence lower bound (ELBO) objective from Bayesian variational methods. This avoids the need for any validation set. We focus on scenarios where the model is over-parameterized for flexibility and the approximate posterior is chosen to be Gaussian with isotropic covariance for tractability, even though it cannot match the true posterior. In such scenarios, we find the ELBO prioritizes posteriors that match the prior, leading to severe underfitting. Instead, we recommend a data-emphasized ELBO that upweights the likelihood but not the prior. In Bayesian transfer learning of image and text classifiers, our method reduces the 88+ hour grid search of past work to under 3 hours while delivering comparable accuracy. We further demonstrate how our approach enables efficient yet accurate approximations of Gaussian processes with learnable lengthscale kernels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过数据强调的变分目标学习超参数
在有限数据上训练大型模型时，避免过拟合至关重要。常见的网格搜索或更智能的搜索方法依赖于为每个候选超参数单独运行昂贵的实验，同时划分验证集会减少可用于训练的数据量。本文研究了通过贝叶斯变分方法中的证据下界（ELBO）目标进行基于梯度的超参数学习，从而无需任何验证集。我们聚焦于模型过参数化以保证灵活性，且近似后验选择为具有各向同性协方差的高斯分布（尽管无法匹配真实后验）的情形。在这种情形下，我们发现ELBO优先选择与先验匹配的后验，导致严重欠拟合。相反，我们推荐一种数据强调的ELBO，其提升似然项但不提升先验项。在图像和文本分类器的贝叶斯迁移学习中，我们的方法将过去工作88+小时的网格搜索缩短至不到3小时，同时保持相当的准确率。我们进一步展示了该方法如何实现可学习长度尺度核的高斯过程的高效且精确的近似。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional hyperparameter tuning methods that require separate validation sets and extensive computational resources, this paper proposes learning hyperparameters via a data-emphasized variational objective derived from the evidence lower bound (ELBO). The method avoids validation sets by directly optimizing hyperparameters using gradient-based updates, assuming a Gaussian approximate posterior for tractability. Experimental results show that the standard ELBO leads to underfitting by favoring priors over data, while the proposed data-emphasized ELBO improves performance by prioritizing likelihood. In Bayesian transfer learning tasks on image and text classifiers, the method reduces hyperparameter search time from over 88 hours to under 3 hours without sacrificing accuracy, and also enables efficient approximation of Gaussian processes with learnable kernels.
为了解决传统超参数调优方法需单独验证集和计算成本高的问题，本文提出通过数据强调的变分目标（ELBO）来学习超参数。该方法直接优化ELBO，使用高斯近似后验，即使其无法完全匹配真实后验。作者发现标准ELBO会因过度重视先验而导致欠拟合，因此提出增强似然项的改进ELBO。在图像和文本分类器的贝叶斯迁移学习实验中，该方法将超参数搜索时间从88小时以上缩短至3小时以内，同时保持相近精度，并支持高效近似可学习核长度尺度的高斯过程。
Unregistered Spectral Image Fusion: Unmixing, Adversarial Learning, and Recoverability
Authors: Jiahui Song, Sagar Shrestha, Xiao Fu
First: 2026-03-23T02:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:55:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper addresses the fusion of a pair of spatially unregistered hyperspectral image (HSI) and multispectral image (MSI) covering roughly overlapping regions. HSIs offer high spectral but low spatial resolution, while MSIs provide the opposite. The goal is to integrate their complementary information to enhance both HSI spatial resolution and MSI spectral resolution. While hyperspectral-multispectral fusion (HMF) has been widely studied, the unregistered setting remains challenging. Many existing methods focus solely on MSI super-resolution, leaving HSI unchanged. Supervised deep learning approaches were proposed for HSI super-resolution, but rely on accurate training data, which is often unavailable. Moreover, theoretical analyses largely address the co-registered case, leaving unregistered HMF poorly understood. In this work, an unsupervised framework is proposed to simultaneously super-resolve both MSI and HSI. The method integrates coupled spectral unmixing for MSI super-resolution with latent-space adversarial learning for HSI super-resolution. Theoretical guarantees on the recoverability of the super-resolution MSI and HSI are established under reasonable generative models -- providing, to our best knowledge, the first such insights for unregistered HMF. The approach is validated on semi-real and real HSI-MSI pairs across diverse conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：未配准光谱图像融合：解混、对抗学习与可恢复性
本文研究空间未配准的高光谱图像（HSI）与多光谱图像（MSI）的融合问题，二者覆盖大致重叠区域。HSI具有高光谱分辨率但低空间分辨率，MSI则相反。目标是整合二者互补信息，提升HSI的空间分辨率和MSI的光谱分辨率。尽管高光谱-多光谱融合（HMF）已广泛研究，但未配准情形仍具挑战性。许多现有方法仅关注MSI超分辨率，而HSI保持不变。监督深度学习方法虽被用于HSI超分辨率，但依赖精确训练数据，常难以获取。此外，理论分析多针对配准情形，未配准HMF尚缺乏深入理解。本文提出一种无监督框架，同时实现MSI与HSI的超分辨率。该方法结合耦合光谱解混用于MSI超分辨率，与潜在空间对抗学习用于HSI超分辨率。在合理生成模型下，建立了超分辨率MSI与HSI的可恢复性理论保证——据我们所知，这是首次针对未配准HMF的此类理论洞察。该方法在多种条件下，经半真实与真实HSI-MSI对验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance both spatial and spectral resolution in unregistered hyperspectral and multispectral image pairs, this work proposes an unsupervised framework that simultaneously super-resolves both modalities. It combines coupled spectral unmixing for MSI resolution enhancement with latent-space adversarial learning for HSI resolution recovery, addressing the lack of supervised training data and theoretical understanding in unregistered settings. Experimental results on semi-real and real datasets demonstrate the method’s effectiveness in recovering high-resolution MSI and HSI representations under diverse conditions, with theoretical guarantees supporting its recoverability under reasonable generative models.
为解决未配准高光谱与多光谱图像的融合问题，本文提出一种无需监督的联合超分辨率框架，同步提升两者分辨率。方法结合耦合光谱解混实现多光谱图像超分辨率，与潜在空间对抗学习实现高光谱图像超分辨率，无需配对训练数据。在合理生成模型下，建立了超分辨率结果可恢复的理论保证，首次为未配准融合提供此类分析。实验在半真实与真实数据集上验证，表现稳定，适用于多种场景。
Graph-Dependent Regret Bounds in Multi-Armed Bandits with Interference
Authors: Fateme Jamshidi, Mohammad Shahverdikondori, Negar Kiyavash
First: 2025-03-10T17:25:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:55:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study multi-armed bandits under network interference, where each unit's reward depends on its own treatment and those of its neighbors in a given graph. This induces an exponentially large action space, making standard approaches computationally impractical. We propose a novel algorithm that uses the local graph structure to minimize regret. We derive a graph-dependent upper bound on cumulative regret that improves over prior work. Additionally, we provide the first lower bounds for bandits with arbitrary network interference, where each bound involves a distinct structural property of the graph. These bounds show that for both dense and sparse graphs, our algorithm is nearly optimal, with matching upper and lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. When the interference graph is unknown, a variant of our algorithm is Pareto optimal: no algorithm can uniformly outperform it across all instances. We complement our theoretical results with numerical experiments, showing that our approach outperforms the baseline methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图依赖的多臂老虎机在干扰下的后悔界
我们研究网络干扰下的多臂老虎机问题，其中每个单位的奖励取决于其自身处理及其在给定图中的邻居处理。这导致动作空间呈指数级增长，使标准方法计算上不可行。我们提出一种新颖算法，利用局部图结构以最小化后悔。我们推导出一个图依赖的累积后悔上界，优于先前工作。此外，我们首次为任意网络干扰下的老虎机问题提供了下界，每个下界涉及图的特定结构属性。这些下界表明，对于稠密和稀疏图，我们的算法几乎最优，上下界在对数因子内匹配。当干扰图未知时，我们算法的变体是帕累托最优的：不存在任何算法能在所有实例上均优于它。我们通过数值实验补充理论结果，表明我们的方法优于基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of multi-armed bandits under network interference where rewards depend on local graph structure and lead to exponentially large action spaces, the paper proposes a novel algorithm leveraging graph locality to reduce regret. It derives a graph-dependent upper bound on cumulative regret that improves upon prior methods and establishes the first lower bounds for arbitrary interference graphs, revealing that the algorithm is nearly optimal across both dense and sparse graphs up to logarithmic factors. When the interference graph is unknown, a variant of the algorithm is Pareto optimal, and numerical experiments confirm its superior performance over baseline methods.
为了解决奖励依赖于自身及邻居节点的网络干扰多臂老虎机问题，本文提出一种利用局部图结构降低计算复杂度和遗憾的新算法。该方法推导出依赖图结构的累积遗憾上界，优于现有方法，并首次给出任意网络干扰图的下界，每个下界与图的特定结构属性相关。实验表明，该算法在稠密与稀疏图中均接近最优，上下界仅相差对数因子，且在图未知时仍为帕累托最优，优于基线方法。
CayleyPy Growth: Efficient growth computations and hundreds of new conjectures on Cayley graphs (Brief version)
Authors: A. Chervov, D. Fedoriaka, E. Konstantinova, A. Naumov, I. Kiselev, A. Sheveleva, I. Koltsov, S. Lytkin, A. Smolensky, A. Soibelman, F. Levkovich-Maslyuk, R. Grimov, D. Volovich, A. Isakov, A. Kostin, M. Litvinov, N. Vilkin-Krom, A. Bidzhiev, A. Krasnyi, M. Evseev, E. Geraseva, L. Grunwald, S. Galkin, E. Koldunov, S. Diner, A. Chevychelov, E. Kudasheva, A. Sychev, A. Kravchenko, Z. Kogan, A. Natyrova, L. Shishina, L. Cheldieva, V. Zamkovoy, D. Kovalenko, O. Papulov, S. Kudashev, D. Shiltsov, R. Turtayev, O. Nikitina, D. Mamayeva, S. Nikolenko, M. Obozov, A. Titarenko, A. Dolgorukova, A. Aparnev, O. Debeaupuis, S. Alami C., H. Isambert
First: 2025-09-23T15:40:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:54:42+00:00
Comments: 46 pages, 30 figures; v2: typos fixed
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This is the third paper of the CayleyPy project applying artificial intelligence to problems in group theory. We announce the first public release of CayleyPy, an open source Python library for computations with Cayley and Schreier graphs. Compared with systems such as GAP and Sage, CayleyPy handles much larger graphs and performs several orders of magnitude faster. Using CayleyPy we obtained about 200 new conjectures on Cayley and Schreier graphs, focused on diameters and growth. For many Cayley graphs of symmetric groups Sn we observe quasi polynomial diameter formulas: a small set of quadratic or linear polynomials indexed by n mod s. We conjecture that this is a general phenomenon, giving efficient diameter computation despite the problem being NP hard. We propose a refinement of the Babai type conjecture on diameters of Sn: n^2/2 + 4n upper bounds in the undirected case, compared to previous O(n^2) bounds. We also provide explicit generator families, related to involutions in a square with whiskers pattern, conjectured to maximize the diameter; search confirms this for all n up to 15. We further conjecture an answer to a question posed by V M Glushkov in 1968 on directed Cayley graphs generated by a cyclic shift and a transposition. For nilpotent groups we conjecture an improvement of J S Ellenberg's results on upper unitriangular matrices over Z/pZ, showing linear dependence of diameter on p. Some conjectures are LLM friendly, naturally stated as sorting problems verifiable by algorithms or Python code. To benchmark path finding we created more than 10 Kaggle datasets. CayleyPy works with arbitrary permutation or matrix groups and includes over 100 predefined generators. Our growth computation code outperforms GAP and Sage up to 1000 times in speed and size.

标题：CayleyPy增长：高效的增长计算与数百个关于Cayley图的新猜想（简版）
这是CayleyPy项目第三篇将人工智能应用于群论问题的论文。我们宣布首次公开发布CayleyPy，这是一个开源的Python库，用于Cayley图和Schreier图的计算。与GAP和Sage等系统相比，CayleyPy可处理规模更大的图，并快几个数量级。 使用CayleyPy，我们获得了约200个关于Cayley图和Schreier图的新猜想，主要聚焦于直径与增长。对于许多对称群Sn的Cayley图，我们观察到准多项式直径公式：由n mod s索引的一组二次或线性多项式。我们推测这是普遍现象，尽管问题本身是NP难，仍能高效计算直径。我们提出对Babai型猜想关于Sn直径的改进：无向情况下上界为n²/2 + 4n，优于之前的O(n²)界。我们还提供了与正方形带触须模式的逆元相关的生成元族，推测其可最大化直径；搜索验证了n≤15时的正确性。我们进一步对V M Glushkov于1968年提出的问题给出猜想：关于由循环移位和对换生成的有向Cayley图。 对于幂零群，我们推测改进了J S Ellenberg关于Z/pZ上上三角单位矩阵的成果，表明直径与p呈线性关系。 部分猜想适合LLM，自然表述为可由算法或Python代码验证的排序问题。为基准路径查找，我们创建了超过10个Kaggle数据集。CayleyPy支持任意置换或矩阵群，并包含100多个预定义生成元。我们的增长计算代码在速度和规模上比GAP和Sage快1000倍。

Using this tool, they computed growth and diameter properties for large graphs, generating around 200 new conjectures, particularly for symmetric group Cayley graphs where diameters follow quasi-polynomial patterns based on n mod s. Adversarial Attacks in AI-Driven RAN Slicing: SLA Violations and Recovery
Authors: Deemah H. Tashman, Soumaya Cherkaoui
First: 2026-04-01T15:54:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:54:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Next-generation (NextG) cellular networks are designed to support emerging applications with diverse data rate and latency requirements, such as immersive multimedia services and large-scale Internet of Things deployments. A key enabling mechanism is radio access network (RAN) slicing, which dynamically partitions radio resources into virtual resource blocks to efficiently serve heterogeneous traffic classes, including enhanced mobile broadband (eMBB), massive machine-type communications (mMTC), and ultra-reliable low-latency communications (URLLC). In this paper, we study the impact of adversarial attacks on AI-driven RAN slicing decisions, where a budget-constrained adversary selectively jams slice transmissions to bias deep reinforcement learning (DRL)-based resource allocation, and quantify the resulting service level agreement (SLA) violations and post-attack recovery behavior. Our results indicate that budget-constrained adversarial jamming can induce severe and slice-dependent steady-state SLA violations. Moreover, the DRL agent's reward converges toward the clean baseline only after a non-negligible recovery period.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI驱动的无线接入网切片中的对抗攻击：SLA违规与恢复
下一代（NextG）蜂窝网络旨在支持具有多样数据速率和时延需求的新兴应用，如沉浸式多媒体服务和大规模物联网部署。关键使能机制是无线接入网（RAN）切片，它通过动态划分无线资源为虚拟资源块，高效服务异构流量类型，包括增强型移动宽带（eMBB）、大规模机器类型通信（mMTC）和超可靠低时延通信（URLLC）。本文研究了对抗攻击对AI驱动RAN切片决策的影响，其中预算受限的攻击者选择性干扰切片传输，以偏倚基于深度强化学习（DRL）的资源分配，并量化由此导致的服务等级协议（SLA）违规及攻击后的恢复行为。结果表明，预算受限的对抗性干扰可引发严重且切片依赖的稳态SLA违规。此外，DRL代理的奖励仅在非可忽略的恢复期后才收敛至干净基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of AI-driven RAN slicing in next-generation cellular networks, this study investigates how budget-constrained adversarial jamming can manipulate DRL-based resource allocation to cause slice-dependent SLA violations. The method involves simulating targeted jamming attacks that bias the DRL agent’s learning process, followed by analysis of resulting performance degradation and recovery dynamics. Experimental results show that adversarial interference leads to severe and persistent SLA violations across different traffic slices, and that the DRL agent requires a significant recovery period to return its reward to baseline performance.
为研究AI驱动的无线接入网切片在下一代蜂窝网络中的脆弱性，本文探讨了预算受限的对抗性干扰如何通过偏置深度强化学习资源分配，导致不同切片间严重的服务等级协议违约。方法通过模拟定向干扰攻击并分析其对系统性能的影响及恢复过程。实验结果表明，对抗性干扰会引发不同切片间显著且持久的SLA违约，且深度强化学习代理需经历较长恢复期后，其奖励函数才能回归基准水平。
A global dataset of continuous urban dashcam driving
Authors: Md Shadab Alam, Olena Bazilinska, Pavlo Bazilinskyy
First: 2026-04-01T15:52:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:52:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce CROWD (City Road Observations With Dashcams), a manually curated dataset of ordinary, minute scale, temporally contiguous, unedited, front facing urban dashcam segments screened and segmented from publicly available YouTube videos. CROWD is designed to support cross-domain robustness and interaction analysis by prioritising routine driving and explicitly excluding crashes, crash aftermath, and other edited or incident-focused content. The release contains 51,753 segment records spanning 20,275.56 hours (42,032 videos), covering 7,103 named inhabited places in 238 countries and territories across all six inhabited continents (Africa, Asia, Europe, North America, South America and Oceania), with segment level manual labels for time of day (day or night) and vehicle type. To lower the barrier for benchmarking, we provide per-segment CSV files of machine-generated detections for all 80 MS-COCO classes produced with YOLOv11x, together with segment-local multi-object tracks (BoT-SORT); e.g. person, bicycle, motorcycle, car, bus, truck, traffic light, stop sign, etc. CROWD is distributed as video identifiers with segment boundaries and derived annotations, enabling reproducible research without redistributing the underlying videos.

标题：连续城市行车记录仪驾驶的全球数据集
我们介绍了CROWD（城市道路行车记录仪观测数据集），这是一个从公开YouTube视频中筛选并人工分割的普通、分钟级、时间连续、未经编辑、前向视角的城市行车记录仪片段数据集。CROWD旨在通过优先选择常规驾驶场景并明确排除事故、事故后处理及其他编辑或事件导向内容，支持跨域鲁棒性与交互分析。该数据集包含51,753个片段记录，总时长20,275.56小时（42,032个视频），覆盖全球238个国家和地区中的7,103个已命名有人居住地点，遍及六大洲（非洲、亚洲、欧洲、北美洲、南美洲和大洋洲），并提供片段级人工标注的时间段（白天或夜晚）和车辆类型。为降低基准测试门槛，我们提供由YOLOv11x生成的针对全部80个MS-COCO类别的机器检测CSV文件，以及片段级多目标跟踪（BoT-SORT）结果，例如行人、自行车、摩托车、汽车、公交车、卡车、交通灯、停车标志等。CROWD以视频标识符、片段边界及衍生标注形式发布，便于可复现研究而无需重新分发原始视频。

ONE-SHOT: Compositional Human-Environment Video Synthesis via Spatial-Decoupled Motion Injection and Hybrid Context Integration
Authors: Fengyuan Yang, Luying Huang, Jiazhi Guan, Quanwei Yang, Dongwei Pan, Jianglin Fu, Haocheng Feng, Wei He, Kaisiyuan Wang, Hang Zhou, Angela Yao
First: 2026-04-01T15:52:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:52:00+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in Video Foundation Models (VFMs) have revolutionized human-centric video synthesis, yet fine-grained and independent editing of subjects and scenes remains a critical challenge. Recent attempts to incorporate richer environment control through rigid 3D geometric compositions often encounter a stark trade-off between precise control and generative flexibility. Furthermore, the heavy 3D pre-processing still limits practical scalability. In this paper, we propose ONE-SHOT, a parameter-efficient framework for compositional human-environment video generation. Our key insight is to factorize the generative process into disentangled signals. Specifically, we introduce a canonical-space injection mechanism that decouples human dynamics from environmental cues via cross-attention. We also propose Dynamic-Grounded-RoPE, a novel positional embedding strategy that establishes spatial correspondences between disparate spatial domains without any heuristic 3D alignments. To support long-horizon synthesis, we introduce a Hybrid Context Integration mechanism to maintain subject and scene consistency across minute-level generations. Experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, offering superior structural control and creative diversity for video synthesis. Our project has been available on: https://martayang.github.io/ONE-SHOT/.

视频基础模型（VFMs）的最新进展已革新以人为中心的视频合成，但对主体与场景进行精细且独立的编辑仍是一项关键挑战。近期尝试通过刚性3D几何组合引入更丰富的环境控制，往往面临精确控制与生成灵活性之间的明显权衡。此外，繁重的3D预处理仍限制了实际可扩展性。本文提出ONE-SHOT，一种参数高效的组合式人-环境视频生成框架。我们的核心思路是将生成过程分解为解耦信号。具体而言，我们引入了一种规范空间注入机制，通过交叉注意力将人体动态与环境线索解耦。我们还提出了一种名为Dynamic-Grounded-RoPE的新型位置嵌入策略，可在无需任何启发式3D对齐的情况下建立不同空间域间的空间对应关系。为支持长时程合成，我们引入了混合上下文集成机制，以在分钟级生成中保持主体与场景的一致性。实验表明，我们的方法显著优于现有最先进方法，为视频合成提供了更优的结构控制与创意多样性。项目已发布于：https://martayang.github.io/ONE-SHOT/。

Recent advances in Video Foundation Models (VFMs) have revolutionized human-centric video synthesis, yet fine-grained and independent editing of subjects and scenes remains a critical challenge.
为实现对视频中人物与环境的精细独立编辑，同时规避传统3D方法的刚性与可扩展性限制，ONE-SHOT提出一种参数高效的框架，通过跨注意力机制将人物动态与环境线索解耦注入规范空间。该方法引入Dynamic-Grounded-RoPE，一种无需3D对齐即可建立空间对应关系的新型位置编码策略，并结合混合上下文集成机制以维持长时序生成中的人物与场景一致性。实验表明，该方法在结构控制与创意多样性方面显著优于现有最先进方法。
RoboNeuron: A Middle-Layer Infrastructure for Agent-Driven Orchestration in Embodied AI
Authors: Weifan Guan, Qinghao Hu, Huasen Xi, Chenxiao Zhang, Aosheng Li, Jian Cheng
First: 2025-12-11T07:58:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:51:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language-action (VLA) models and LLM agents have advanced rapidly, yet reliable deployment on physical robots is often hindered by an interface mismatch between agent tool APIs and robot middleware. Current implementations typically rely on ad-hoc wrappers that are difficult to reuse, and changes to the VLA backend or serving stack often necessitate extensive re-integration. We introduce RoboNeuron, a middleware layer that connects the Model Context Protocol (MCP) for LLM agents with robot middleware such as ROS2. RoboNeuron bridges these ecosystems by deriving agent-callable tools directly from ROS schemas, providing a unified execution abstraction that supports both direct commands and modular composition, and localizing backend, runtime, and acceleration-preset changes within a stable inference boundary. We evaluate RoboNeuron in simulation and on hardware through multi-platform base control, arm motion, and VLA-based grasping tasks, demonstrating that it enables modular system orchestration under a unified interface while supporting backend transitions without system rewiring. The full code implementation of this work is available at github repo: https://github.com/guanweifan/RoboNeuron
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboNeuron：用于具身AI中代理驱动编排的中间层基础设施
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型和大语言模型（LLM）代理发展迅速，但其在物理机器人上的可靠部署常因代理工具API与机器人中间件之间的接口不匹配而受阻。当前实现通常依赖临时封装，难以复用，且VLA后端或服务栈的变更往往需要大量重新集成。我们提出RoboNeuron，一个连接LLM代理的模型上下文协议（MCP）与机器人中间件（如ROS2）的中间层。RoboNeuron通过从ROS架构直接导出可调用的代理工具，弥合这两个生态系统的鸿沟，提供统一的执行抽象，支持直接命令与模块化组合，并在稳定的推理边界内本地化后端、运行时和加速预设的变更。我们在仿真和硬件平台上通过多平台基础控制、机械臂运动和基于VLA的抓取任务评估RoboNeuron，证明其可在统一接口下实现模块化系统编排，并支持后端切换而无需系统重连。本工作的完整代码实现可在GitHub仓库获取：https://github.com/guanweifan/RoboNeuron
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of integrating rapidly advancing vision-language-action models and LLM agents with physical robot systems, the authors propose RoboNeuron, a middleware layer that bridges the Model Context Protocol (MCP) for LLM agents with robot middleware like ROS2. By deriving agent-callable tools directly from ROS schemas, RoboNeuron provides a unified execution abstraction supporting both direct commands and modular composition, while isolating backend, runtime, and acceleration changes within a stable inference boundary. Experimental results across simulation and hardware platforms—including base control, arm motion, and VLA-based grasping—show that RoboNeuron enables modular orchestration under a consistent interface and supports backend transitions without requiring system reconfiguration.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型与LLM代理与物理机器人因接口不兼容而难以部署的问题，RoboNeuron提出了一种中间件层，连接LLM代理的模型上下文协议与机器人系统（如ROS2）。它从ROS架构自动生成可被代理调用的工具，支持统一执行抽象与模块化组合，同时将后端、运行时和加速配置变更隔离在稳定推理边界内。实验在仿真与真实硬件上验证了其在多平台控制、机械臂运动和基于VLA的抓取任务中的有效性，表明其可在不重写系统架构的前提下支持后端平滑切换。
OSGym: Scalable OS Infra for Computer Use Agents
Authors: Zengyi Qin, Jinyuan Chen, Yunze Man, Shengcao Cao, Ziqi Pang, Zhuoyuan Wang, Han Fang, Ling Zhu, Zixin Xie, Zibu Wei, Tianshu Ran, Haoran Geng, Ray Pan, Qizhen Sun, Zachary Bright, Yuyang Cai, Chongye Yang, Jiace Zhao, Tianrui Liu, Han Cao, Yeyang Zhou, Rui Wang, Song Wang, Xiang Ren, Bo Zhang, Yutong Ban, Pieter Abbeel, Brian Anthony
First: 2025-11-11T20:03:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:51:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training computer use agents requires full-featured OS sandboxes with GUI environments, which consume substantial hardware resources as the number of sandboxes scales. Stochastic errors arising from diverse software execution within these sandboxes further demand robust infrastructure design and reliable error recovery. We present OSGym, a scalable OS environment infrastructure for computer use agents, built around these key optimization strategies: (1) Decentralized OS state management, which isolates failures to individual replicas and significantly enhances overall system reliability; (2) Hardware-aware OS replica orchestration, which addresses CPU-bounded scaling bottlenecks and substantially reduces compute overhead; (3) KVM virtualization with copy-on-write disk management, which shares a common bootable disk across VM instances and provisions only instance-specific modifications, reducing physical disk consumption by 88% and increasing disk provisioning speed by 37 times; and (4) Robust container pool with multi-layer fault recovery. Together, these optimizations yield strong scalability and resource efficiency: OSGym manages over a thousand OS replicas under constrained resources, supports parallel trajectory generation at 1420 multi-turn trajectories per minute, and reduces per-replica cost to 0.2-0.3 USD per day, a 90% reduction over standard deployment. Our experiments validate OSGym across end-to-end pipelines for data collection and training for computer use agents. We believe OSGym establishes a new foundation for scalable, general-purpose computer use agent research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OSGym：面向计算机使用代理的可扩展操作系统基础设施
训练计算机使用代理需要具备完整功能的操作系统沙箱及图形用户界面环境，随着沙箱数量增加，其硬件资源消耗显著上升。沙箱内多样软件执行产生的随机错误，进一步要求基础设施设计具备鲁棒性及可靠的错误恢复能力。我们提出OSGym，一种面向计算机使用代理的可扩展操作系统环境基础设施，其核心优化策略包括：（1）去中心化操作系统状态管理，将故障隔离至单个副本，显著提升系统整体可靠性；（2）硬件感知的操作系统副本编排，解决CPU受限的扩展瓶颈，大幅降低计算开销；（3）基于KVM虚拟化与写时复制磁盘管理，多个虚拟机实例共享同一可启动磁盘，仅对实例特定修改进行配置，物理磁盘消耗降低88%，磁盘配置速度提升37倍；（4）具备多层故障恢复能力的容器池。上述优化共同实现强扩展性与资源效率：OSGym在受限资源下管理超千个操作系统副本，支持每分钟并行生成1420条多轮轨迹，单副本成本降至每日0.2-0.3美元，较标准部署降低90%。我们的实验验证了OSGym在计算机使用代理数据采集与训练端到端流程中的有效性。我们相信，OSGym为可扩展、通用型计算机使用代理研究奠定了新基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high resource cost and reliability challenges of training computer use agents in OS sandboxes, OSGym introduces a scalable infrastructure using four core optimizations: decentralized OS state management to isolate failures, hardware-aware replica orchestration to avoid CPU bottlenecks, KVM with copy-on-write disk sharing to cut disk usage by 88% and speed up provisioning 37x, and a fault-tolerant container pool. Experiments show OSGym can manage over a thousand OS replicas under constrained resources, generate 1420 multi-turn trajectories per minute, and reduce daily per-replica cost to 0.2–0.3 USD — a 90% improvement over standard setups — while supporting end-to-end agent training pipelines.
为解决训练计算机使用代理时GUI沙箱带来的高硬件成本与可靠性挑战，OSGym采用四项核心优化：分布式OS状态管理以隔离故障、硬件感知的副本调度以避免CPU瓶颈、KVM配合写时复制磁盘共享以降低磁盘使用88%并加速部署37倍、以及多层容错容器池。实验表明，OSGym可在资源受限条件下高效管理超千个OS副本，每分钟生成1420条多轮轨迹，单副本日均成本降至0.2–0.3美元（较传统部署降低90%），并支持完整的端到端代理训练流程。
Ego-Foresight: Self-supervised Learning of Agent-Aware Representations for Improved RL
Authors: Manuel Serra Nunes, Atabak Dehban, Yiannis Demiris, José Santos-Victor
First: 2024-05-27T13:32:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:51:54+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 8 figures, conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the significant advances in Deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) observed in the last decade, the amount of training experience necessary to learn effective policies remains one of the primary concerns in both simulated and real environments. Looking to solve this issue, previous work has shown that improved efficiency can be achieved by separately modeling the agent and environment, but usually requires a supervisory signal. In contrast to RL, humans can perfect a new skill from a small number of trials and often do so without a supervisory signal, making neuroscientific studies of human development a valuable source of inspiration for RL. In particular, we explore the idea of motor prediction, which states that humans develop an internal model of themselves and of the consequences that their motor commands have on the immediate sensory inputs. Our insight is that the movementofthe agent provides a cue that allows the duality between the agent and environment to be learned. To instantiate this idea, we present Ego-Foresight (EF), a self-supervised method for disentangling agent information based on motion and prediction. Our main finding is that, when used as an auxiliary task in feature learning, self-supervised agent awareness improves the sample-efficiency and performance of the underlying RL algorithm. To test our approach, we study the ability of EF to predict agent movement and disentangle agent information. Then, we integrate EF with model-free and model based RL algorithms to solve simulated control tasks, showing improved sample-efficiency and performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自我前瞻：用于提升强化学习的自监督代理感知表征学习
尽管过去十年深度强化学习（RL）取得了显著进展，但学习有效策略所需的训练经验量仍是在模拟和真实环境中的一大主要问题。为解决此问题，先前研究显示，通过分别建模代理与环境可提升效率，但通常需要监督信号。与RL不同，人类仅需少量尝试即可掌握新技能，且常无需监督信号，因此人类神经科学研究为RL提供了宝贵灵感。特别是，我们探讨了运动预测思想，即人类会建立自身及运动指令对即时感官输入影响的内部模型。我们的洞见是，代理的运动提供了线索，可学习代理与环境之间的二元性。为实现这一思想，我们提出了Ego-Foresight（EF），一种基于运动与预测的自监督方法，用于解耦代理信息。主要发现是，当作为特征学习的辅助任务时，自监督代理感知可提升底层RL算法的样本效率与性能。为验证该方法，我们研究了EF预测代理运动及解耦代理信息的能力，随后将其与无模型和基于模型的RL算法结合，解决模拟控制任务，显示其提升了样本效率与性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of reinforcement learning in requiring large amounts of training data, this work draws inspiration from human motor learning to propose Ego-Foresight, a self-supervised method that learns agent-aware representations by predicting the agent's own motion and disentangling agent-related information from environmental signals. The method leverages the agent’s movement as a cue to implicitly model the duality between agent and environment, without requiring external supervision. Experimental results show that integrating Ego-Foresight as an auxiliary task enhances sample efficiency and performance across both model-free and model-based RL algorithms in simulated control tasks.
为了解决强化学习训练数据需求量大的问题，该研究受人类无需监督即可快速掌握运动技能的启发，提出了Ego-Foresight方法，通过利用自身运动信息实现对代理与环境信息的解耦，从而学习代理感知表征。实验表明，将该自监督方法作为辅助任务融入模型无关和模型依赖的强化学习算法中，可显著提升样本效率和最终任务表现，验证了自监督代理建模在强化学习中的有效性。
Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Survey on Agentic RAG
Authors: Aditi Singh, Abul Ehtesham, Saket Kumar, Tala Talaei Khoei, Athanasios V. Vasilakos
First: 2025-01-15T20:40:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:51:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced artificial intelligence by enabling human-like text generation and natural language understanding. However, their reliance on static training data limits their ability to respond to dynamic, real-time queries, resulting in outdated or inaccurate outputs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a solution, enhancing LLMs by integrating real-time data retrieval to provide contextually relevant and up-to-date responses. Despite its promise, traditional RAG systems are constrained by static workflows and lack the adaptability required for multi-step reasoning and complex task management. Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Agentic RAG) transcends these limitations by embedding autonomous AI agents into the RAG pipeline. These agents leverage agentic design patterns reflection, planning, tool use, and multi-agent collaboration to dynamically manage retrieval strategies, iteratively refine contextual understanding, and adapt workflows through operational structures ranging from sequential steps to adaptive collaboration. This integration enables Agentic RAG systems to deliver flexibility, scalability, and context-awareness across diverse applications. This paper presents an analytical survey of Agentic RAG systems. It traces the evolution of RAG paradigms, introduces a principled taxonomy of Agentic RAG architectures based on agent cardinality, control structure, autonomy, and knowledge representation, and provides a comparative analysis of design trade-offs across existing frameworks. The survey examines applications in healthcare, finance, education, and enterprise document processing, and distills practical lessons for system designers and practitioners. Finally, it identifies key open research challenges related to evaluation, coordination, memory management, efficiency, and governance, outlining directions for future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理增强生成：代理RAG综述
大语言模型（LLMs）通过实现类人文本生成和自然语言理解推动了人工智能的发展。然而，其对静态训练数据的依赖限制了其应对动态实时查询的能力，导致输出过时或不准确。检索增强生成（RAG）作为一种解决方案，通过整合实时数据检索增强LLMs，提供上下文相关且最新的响应。尽管如此，传统RAG系统受限于静态工作流，缺乏多步推理和复杂任务管理所需的适应性。代理增强生成（Agentic RAG）通过将自主AI代理嵌入RAG流程，超越了这些限制。这些代理利用代理设计模式，如反思、规划、工具使用和多代理协作，动态管理检索策略，迭代优化上下文理解，并通过从顺序步骤到自适应协作的运营结构调整工作流。这种集成使Agentic RAG系统在多样化应用中实现灵活性、可扩展性和上下文感知能力。本文对Agentic RAG系统进行分析性综述，追溯RAG范式的演变，基于代理基数、控制结构、自主性和知识表示提出Agentic RAG架构的原理性分类，并对现有框架的设计权衡进行比较分析。综述探讨了医疗、金融、教育和企业文档处理等领域的应用，并为系统设计者和实践者提炼实用经验。最后，本文识别了与评估、协调、内存管理、效率和治理相关的关键开放研究挑战，并指明未来研究方向。

为解决静态大语言模型和传统RAG系统在处理动态查询与复杂推理时的局限性，本文调研了代理增强型RAG（Agentic RAG），其将自主AI代理嵌入RAG流程，以支持自适应的多步工作流。这些代理通过规划、反思、工具使用和多代理协作动态优化检索与上下文理解。文章基于代理数量、控制结构、自主性与知识表示构建了Agentic RAG架构分类法，并对比分析了现有框架的设计权衡。跨医疗、金融、企业文档处理等领域的实证表明，该方法提升了灵活性、可扩展性与上下文感知能力，同时指出评估、协调、记忆管理、效率与治理等关键开放挑战。
Spatial Reasoning is Not a Free Lunch: A Controlled Study on LLaVA
Authors: Nahid Alam, Leema Krishna Murali, Siddhant Bharadwaj, Patrick Liu, Timothy Chung, Drishti Sharma, Akshata A., Kranthi Kiran, Wesley Tam, Bala Krishna S Vegesna
Venue: ICLR 2026 poster
First: 2026-03-13T01:11:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:49:01+00:00
Comments: Accepted as a poster at ICLR 2026 workshop ICBINB, typo fixed
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced rapidly, yet they still struggle with basic spatial reasoning. Despite strong performance on general benchmarks, modern VLMs remain brittle at understanding 2D spatial relationships such as relative position, layout, and counting. We argue that this failure is not merely a data problem, but is closely tied to dominant design choices in current VLM pipelines: reliance on CLIP-style image encoders and the flattening of images into 1D token sequences with 1D positional encoding. We present a controlled diagnostic study within the LLaVA framework to isolate how these choices affect spatial grounding. We evaluate frontier models and LLaVA variants on a suite of spatial benchmarks, comparing CLIP-based encoders against alternatives trained with denser or generative objectives, as well as variants augmented with 2D positional encoding. Our results show consistent spatial performance gaps across models, and indicate that encoder objectives and positional structure shape spatial behavior, but do not fully resolve it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：空间推理并非免费午餐：针对LLaVA的对照研究
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）发展迅速，但仍难以处理基本的空间推理。尽管在通用基准上表现强劲，现代VLMs在理解二维空间关系（如相对位置、布局和计数）方面仍显脆弱。我们认为，这一失败并非单纯数据问题，而是与当前VLM流程中的主导设计选择密切相关：依赖CLIP风格的图像编码器，以及将图像扁平化为1D token序列并使用1D位置编码。我们在LLaVA框架内开展了一项对照诊断研究，以分离这些选择对空间定位的影响。我们评估前沿模型及LLaVA变体在一系列空间基准上的表现，比较基于CLIP的编码器与采用更密集或生成式目标训练的替代方案，以及增加2D位置编码的变体。结果表明，各模型在空间性能上存在一致差距，编码器目标和位置结构塑造了空间行为，但并未完全解决该问题。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why vision-language models still struggle with basic spatial reasoning despite strong general performance, arguing that the issue stems from architectural choices rather than data scarcity. The authors conduct a controlled experiment within the LLaVA framework, comparing models using CLIP-style encoders against those trained with denser or generative objectives, and evaluating variants with 2D positional encoding. Results show persistent spatial performance gaps across models, indicating that encoder design and positional encoding significantly influence spatial reasoning ability, but do not fully eliminate the problem.
该研究探讨了视觉语言模型（VLMs）在基本空间推理上表现不佳的原因，认为这源于架构设计，如CLIP风格的图像编码器和一维位置编码。通过在LLaVA框架内进行控制实验，作者比较了不同编码器训练目标和位置编码的模型在空间基准测试上的表现。结果表明，不同模型在空间推理上存在持续性能差距，说明编码器设计和位置结构显著影响空间推理能力，但这些改进尚不能完全解决该问题。
TeFlow: Enabling Multi-frame Supervision for Self-Supervised Feed-forward Scene Flow Estimation
Authors: Qingwen Zhang, Chenhan Jiang, Xiaomeng Zhu, Yunqi Miao, Yushan Zhang, Olov Andersson, Patric Jensfelt
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-02-22T05:50:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:46:03+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026; 16 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Self-supervised feed-forward methods for scene flow estimation offer real-time efficiency, but their supervision from two-frame point correspondences is unreliable and often breaks down under occlusions. Multi-frame supervision has the potential to provide more stable guidance by incorporating motion cues from past frames, yet naive extensions of two-frame objectives are ineffective because point correspondences vary abruptly across frames, producing inconsistent signals. In the paper, we present TeFlow, enabling multi-frame supervision for feed-forward models by mining temporally consistent supervision. TeFlow introduces a temporal ensembling strategy that forms reliable supervisory signals by aggregating the most temporally consistent motion cues from a candidate pool built across multiple frames. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TeFlow establishes a new state-of-the-art for self-supervised feed-forward methods, achieving performance gains of up to 33\% on the challenging Argoverse 2 and nuScenes datasets. Our method performs on par with leading optimization-based methods, yet speeds up 150 times. The code is open-sourced at https://github.com/Kin-Zhang/TeFlow along with trained model weights.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TeFlow：为自监督前馈场景流估计启用多帧监督
自监督前馈场景流估计方法具有实时效率，但其基于两帧点对应关系的监督不可靠，且在遮挡情况下常失效。多帧监督可通过引入过去帧的运动线索提供更稳定的引导，但直接扩展两帧目标无效，因为点对应关系在帧间突变，产生不一致信号。本文提出TeFlow，通过挖掘时序一致的监督信号，使前馈模型支持多帧监督。TeFlow引入时序集成策略，从跨多帧构建的候选池中聚合最时序一致的运动线索，形成可靠监督信号。大量评估表明，TeFlow在自监督前馈方法中建立新SOTA，在挑战性Argoverse 2和nuScenes数据集上性能提升最高达33%。我们的方法性能媲美主流优化方法，但速度提升150倍。代码及训练模型权重开源于https://github.com/Kin-Zhang/TeFlow。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of two-frame supervision in self-supervised feed-forward scene flow estimation, especially under occlusions, the paper introduces TeFlow, a method that enables multi-frame supervision by mining temporally consistent motion cues. TeFlow employs a temporal ensembling strategy that aggregates the most temporally consistent signals from a multi-frame candidate pool to form reliable supervision. Experiments show TeFlow achieves up to 33% performance gains over prior self-supervised feed-forward methods on Argoverse 2 and nuScenes, matching optimization-based methods while accelerating inference by 150x.
为了解决自监督前馈场景流估计中两帧监督不稳定、易受遮挡影响的问题，本文提出TeFlow方法，通过挖掘多帧间时间一致的运动线索实现多帧监督。该方法采用时间聚合策略，从多帧候选池中选取最一致的点对应关系构建可靠监督信号。实验表明，TeFlow在Argoverse 2和nuScenes数据集上相较现有自监督前馈方法性能提升最高达33%，与主流优化方法表现相当，同时速度提升150倍。
Automated Framework to Evaluate and Harden LLM System Instructions against Encoding Attacks
Authors: Anubhab Sahu, Diptisha Samanta, Reza Soosahabi
First: 2026-04-01T15:45:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:45:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
System Instructions in Large Language Models (LLMs) are commonly used to enforce safety policies, define agent behavior, and protect sensitive operational context in agentic AI applications. These instructions may contain sensitive information such as API credentials, internal policies, and privileged workflow definitions, making system instruction leakage a critical security risk highlighted in the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications. Without incurring the overhead costs of reasoning models, many LLM applications rely on refusal-based instructions that block direct requests for system instructions, implicitly assuming that prohibited information can only be extracted through explicit queries. We introduce an automated evaluation framework that tests whether system instructions remain confidential when extraction requests are re-framed as encoding or structured output tasks. Across four common models and 46 verified system instructions, we observe high attack success rates (> 0.7) for structured serialization where models refuse direct extraction requests but disclose protected content in the requested serialization formats. We further demonstrate a mitigation strategy based on one-shot instruction reshaping using a Chain-of-Thought reasoning model, indicating that even subtle changes in wording and structure of system instructions can significantly reduce attack success rate without requiring model retraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于评估和加固大型语言模型系统指令抵御编码攻击的自动化框架
大型语言模型（LLM）中的系统指令常用于执行安全策略、定义代理行为，并保护代理型AI应用中的敏感操作上下文。这些指令可能包含API凭证、内部政策及特权工作流定义等敏感信息，因此系统指令泄露是OWASP LLM应用十大安全风险中突出的安全隐患。许多LLM应用在不增加推理模型开销的前提下，依赖拒绝型指令阻止对系统指令的直接请求，隐含假设禁止信息只能通过显式查询提取。我们引入一个自动化评估框架，测试当提取请求被重新表述为编码或结构化输出任务时，系统指令是否仍能保持机密性。在四个常见模型和46个已验证系统指令上，我们观察到结构化序列化攻击成功率高（>0.7），即模型虽拒绝直接提取请求，但在请求的序列化格式中却泄露了受保护内容。我们进一步提出一种基于单次指令重塑的缓解策略，利用思维链推理模型，表明即使对系统指令的措辞和结构进行细微调整，也能显著降低攻击成功率，而无需重新训练模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security risk of system instruction leakage in LLMs—highlighted in OWASP Top 10—the paper proposes an automated framework to evaluate and harden instructions against encoding attacks. It tests whether models, when refusing direct extraction requests, still inadvertently disclose sensitive content through structured output tasks. Across four models and 46 instructions, structured serialization attacks succeeded at over 70% rate. The study then introduces a mitigation via one-shot instruction reshaping using a Chain-of-Thought model, which reduced attack success significantly without retraining.
鉴于LLM系统指令泄露在OWASP Top 10中被列为关键安全风险，本文提出一种自动化框架用于评估和加固指令以抵御编码攻击。方法通过将提取请求伪装为结构化输出任务，测试模型是否在拒绝直接查询时仍会泄露敏感内容，实验覆盖四类模型和46条验证指令。结果显示，结构化序列化攻击成功率超过0.7，即使模型拒绝直接提问。通过使用Chain-of-Thought模型进行一次性指令重写，可显著降低攻击成功率，且无需重新训练模型。
Foundation Model-guided Iteratively Prompting and Pseudo-Labeling for Partially Labeled Medical Image Segmentation
Authors: Qiaochu Zhao, Wei Wei, David Horowitz, Richard Bakst, Yading Yuan
Venue: ISBI
First: 2026-04-01T15:45:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:45:48+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 5 figures. Accepted for presentation at IEEE International Symposium on Biomedical Imaging (ISBI) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated medical image segmentation has achieved remarkable progress with fully labeled data. However, site-specific clinical priorities and the high cost of manual annotation often yield scans with only a subset of organs labeled, leading to the partially labeled problem that degrades performance. To address this issue, we propose IPnP, an Iteratively Prompting and Pseudo-labeling framework, for partially labeled medical image segmentation. IPnP iteratively generates and refines pseudo-labels for unlabeled organs through collaboration between a trainable segmentation network (specialist) and a frozen foundation model (generalist), progressively recovering full-organ supervision. On the public dataset AMOS with the simulated partial-label setting, IPnP consistently improves segmentation performance over prior methods and approaches the performance of the fully labeled reference. We further evaluate on a private, partially labeled dataset of 210 head-and-neck cancer patients and demonstrate our effectiveness in real-world clinical settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于基础模型引导的迭代提示与伪标签方法用于部分标注医学图像分割
在完全标注数据下，医学图像自动分割已取得显著进展。然而，由于临床需求的特异性及人工标注成本高昂，实际扫描中常仅对部分器官进行标注，导致部分标注问题，从而降低分割性能。为解决此问题，我们提出IPnP框架，即迭代提示与伪标签方法，用于部分标注医学图像分割。IPnP通过可训练分割网络（专家）与冻结的基础模型（通用模型）协作，迭代生成并优化未标注器官的伪标签，逐步恢复全器官监督。在公开数据集AMOS（模拟部分标注设置）上，IPnP持续优于现有方法，性能接近完全标注基准。我们进一步在包含210例头颈癌患者的私有部分标注数据集上评估，验证了方法在真实临床场景中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the clinical reality that only partial organs are labeled in medical scans due to cost and priority constraints, the paper introduces IPnP, an iterative framework that collaborates between a trainable segmentation model and a frozen foundation model to generate and refine pseudo-labels for unlabeled organs. Through successive iterations, IPnP progressively restores full-organ supervision, enhancing segmentation accuracy. Experiments on the AMOS dataset under simulated partial labeling show consistent performance gains over prior methods, approaching fully labeled benchmarks, and validation on a real-world private dataset of 210 head-and-neck cancer patients confirms its clinical applicability.
鉴于临床实践中因成本和优先级限制，医学影像常仅对部分器官进行标注，本文提出IPnP框架，通过可训练分割模型与冻结的基础模型协作，迭代生成并优化未标注器官的伪标签，逐步恢复全器官监督。在模拟部分标注的AMOS数据集上，IPnP显著优于现有方法，性能接近全标注基准；在包含210例头颈癌患者的私有真实数据集上，验证了其在临床场景中的有效性与实用性。
Aligning Recommendations with User Popularity Preferences
Authors: Mona Schirmer, Anton Thielmann, Pola Schwöbel, Thomas Martynec, Giuseppe Di Benedetto, Ben London, Yannik Stein
First: 2026-04-01T15:45:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:45:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted at FAccT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Popularity bias is a pervasive problem in recommender systems, where recommendations disproportionately favor popular items. This not only results in "rich-get-richer" dynamics and a homogenization of visible content, but can also lead to misalignment of recommendations with individual users' preferences for popular or niche content. This work studies popularity bias through the lens of user-recommender alignment. To this end, we introduce Popularity Quantile Calibration, a measurement framework that quantifies misalignment between a user's historical popularity preference and the popularity of their recommendations. Building on this notion of popularity alignment, we propose SPREE, an inference-time mitigation method for sequential recommenders based on activation steering. SPREE identifies a popularity direction in representation space and adaptively steers model activations based on an estimate of each user's personal popularity bias, allowing both the direction and magnitude of steering to vary across users. Unlike global debiasing approaches, SPREE explicitly targets alignment rather than uniformly reducing popularity. Experiments across multiple datasets show that SPREE consistently improves user-level popularity alignment while preserving recommendation quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使推荐与用户流行度偏好对齐
流行度偏差是推荐系统中普遍存在的问题，推荐结果往往过度偏向热门项目。这不仅导致“强者愈强”的动态和内容同质化，还可能使推荐与用户对热门或小众内容的个人偏好产生偏差。本研究从用户-推荐系统对齐的角度研究流行度偏差。为此，我们提出了流行度分位数校准（Popularity Quantile Calibration），一种量化用户历史流行度偏好与其推荐项目流行度之间偏差的测量框架。基于这一流行度对齐概念，我们提出了SPREE，一种面向序列推荐模型的推理时缓解方法，基于激活引导。SPREE在表示空间中识别流行度方向，并根据对每位用户个人流行度偏差的估计，自适应引导模型激活，允许不同用户在方向和幅度上进行差异化引导。与全局去偏方法不同，SPREE明确针对对齐而非统一降低流行度。在多个数据集上的实验表明，SPREE在保持推荐质量的同时，持续提升用户级流行度对齐程度。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses popularity bias in recommender systems, which favors popular items and misaligns with users' diverse preferences for popular or niche content. The authors introduce Popularity Quantile Calibration to measure misalignment between users' historical popularity preferences and recommended items, then propose SPREE, an inference-time method that steers model activations toward users' personal popularity directions using activation steering. SPREE adapts both the direction and magnitude of steering per user, unlike global debiasing methods. Experiments across multiple datasets show SPREE improves user-level popularity alignment without sacrificing recommendation quality.
该论文针对推荐系统中普遍存在的流行度偏差问题，即系统过度推荐热门项目，导致与用户对热门或小众内容的真实偏好不匹配。作者提出流行度分位数校准方法，量化用户历史流行度偏好与推荐项目流行度之间的偏差，并设计SPREE方法，在推理阶段通过激活引导调整模型输出，根据每个用户的流行度偏差动态调整引导方向和强度。实验表明，SPREE在提升用户级流行度对齐的同时，未损害推荐质量。
Stein Variational Uncertainty-Adaptive Model Predictive Control
Authors: Hrishikesh Sathyanarayan, Ian Abraham
First: 2026-04-01T15:43:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:43:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a Stein variational distributionally robust controller for nonlinear dynamical systems with latent parametric uncertainty. The method is an alternative to conservative worst-case ambiguity-set optimization with a deterministic particle-based approximation of a task-dependent uncertainty distribution, enabling the controller to concentrate on parameter sensitivities that most strongly affect closed-loop performance. Our method yields a controller that is robust to latent parameter uncertainty by coupling optimal control with Stein variational inference, and avoiding restrictive parametric assumptions on the uncertainty model while preserving computational parallelism. In contrast to classical DRO, which can sacrifice nominal performance through worst-case design, we find our approach achieves robustness by shaping the control law around relevant uncertainty that are most critical to the task objective. The proposed framework therefore reconciles robust control and variational inference in a single decision-theoretic formulation for broad classes of control systems with parameter uncertainty. We demonstrate our approach on representative control problems that empirically illustrate improved performance-robustness tradeoffs over nominal, ensemble, and classical distributionally robust baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：斯坦因变分不确定性自适应模型预测控制
我们提出了一种针对非线性动态系统中隐含参数不确定性的斯坦因变分分布鲁棒控制器。该方法是保守最坏情况模糊集优化的替代方案，通过确定性粒子近似任务相关不确定性分布，使控制器聚焦于对闭环性能影响最显著的参数敏感性。该方法通过将最优控制与斯坦因变分推断相结合，实现对隐含参数不确定性的鲁棒性，同时避免对不确定性模型的严格参数假设，保持计算并行性。与经典DRO可能因最坏情况设计牺牲名义性能不同，我们的方法通过围绕对任务目标最关键的不确定性塑造控制律实现鲁棒性。因此，该框架在单一决策理论框架下，将鲁棒控制与变分推断统一，适用于广泛参数不确定的控制系统。我们在代表性控制问题上验证了该方法，实证表明其在性能-鲁棒性权衡上优于名义、集合及经典分布鲁棒基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust control in nonlinear systems with latent parametric uncertainty, this work introduces a Stein variational distributionally robust controller that approximates task-dependent uncertainty using deterministic particles, focusing on parameter sensitivities most critical to closed-loop performance. By integrating optimal control with Stein variational inference, the method avoids restrictive parametric assumptions while maintaining computational parallelism. Experimental results show that compared to nominal, ensemble, and classical DRO baselines, the proposed controller achieves superior performance-robustness tradeoffs without sacrificing nominal performance through worst-case design.
为解决非线性系统中潜参数不确定性下的鲁棒控制问题，本文提出一种基于Stein变分的分布鲁棒控制器，通过确定性粒子近似任务相关不确定性分布，使控制器聚焦于对闭环性能影响最大的参数。该方法将最优控制与Stein变分推断结合，避免对不确定性模型的强参数假设，同时保持计算并行性。实验表明，相比常规、集合和经典分布鲁棒基线方法，该方法在性能-鲁棒性权衡上表现更优，且未牺牲名义性能，有效将鲁棒控制与变分推断统一于决策理论框架中。
Sub-metre Lunar DEM Generation and Validation from Chandrayaan-2 OHRC Multi-View Imagery Using Open-Source Photogrammetry
Authors: Aaranay Aadi, Jai Singla, Nitant Dube, Oleg Alexandrov
First: 2026-04-01T15:43:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:43:04+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
High-resolution digital elevation models (DEMs) of the lunar surface are essential for surface mobility planning, landing site characterization, and planetary science. The Orbiter High Resolution Camera (OHRC) on board Chandrayaan-2 has the best ground sampling capabilities of any lunar orbital imaging currently in use by acquiring panchromatic imagery at a resolution of roughly 20-30 cm per pixel. This work presents, for the first time, the generation of sub-metre DEMs from OHRC multi-view imagery using an exclusively open-source pipeline. Candidate stereo pairs are identified from non-paired OHRC archives through geometric analysis of image metadata, employing baseline-to-height (B/H) ratio computation and convergence angle estimation. Dense stereo correspondence and ray triangulation are then applied to generate point clouds, which are gridded into DEMs at effective spatial resolutions between approximately 24 and 54 cm across five geographically distributed lunar sites. Absolute elevation consistency is established through Iterative Closest Point (ICP) alignment against Lunar Reconnaissance Orbiter Narrow Angle Camera (NAC) Digital Terrain Models, followed by constant-bias offset correction. Validation against NAC reference terrain yields a vertical RMSE of 5.85 m (at native OHRC resolution), and a horizontal accuracy of less than 30 cm assessed by planimetric feature matching.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于月球车-2号OHRC多视角影像与开源摄影测量生成和验证亚米级月面DEM
高分辨率月面数字高程模型（DEM）对表面机动规划、着陆点特征分析及行星科学至关重要。月球车-2号搭载的轨道高分辨率相机（OHRC）具有当前所有在轨月球成像系统中最佳的地面采样能力，可获取约20-30厘米/像素的全色影像。本研究首次利用完全开源的处理流程，从OHRC多视角影像生成亚米级DEM。通过分析非配对OHRC存档影像的元数据，识别候选立体像对，采用基线高度比（B/H）计算和收敛角估算进行几何分析。随后应用密集立体匹配与光线三角测量生成点云，并在五个地理分布广泛的月面区域网格化为DEM，有效空间分辨率介于约24至54厘米之间。通过迭代最近点（ICP）对齐月球勘测轨道器窄角相机（NAC）数字地形模型，建立绝对高程一致性，并进行恒定偏差偏移校正。与NAC参考地形验证结果显示，垂直均方根误差为5.85米（OHRC原分辨率），水平精度通过平面特征匹配评估小于30厘米。
