为支持月球表面移动规划与科学分析，本研究首次利用印度昌德拉扬-2号任务的高分辨率轨道相机（OHRC）多视角影像，通过开源摄影测量流程生成亚米级数字高程模型（DEM）。方法通过影像元数据几何分析筛选立体像对，计算基线高度比与收敛角，再进行密集立体匹配与光线三角化生成点云，最终栅格化为24至54厘米分辨率的DEM。与月球勘测轨道器窄角相机（NAC）参考地形模型对比验证，结果显示垂直均方根误差5.85米，水平精度优于30厘米，经ICP配准与偏差校正后实现。
Diff3R: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Uncertainty-aware Differentiable Optimization
Authors: Yueh-Cheng Liu, Jozef Hladký, Matthias Nießner, Angela Dai
Venue: www
First: 2026-04-01T15:40:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:40:20+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://liu115.github.io/diff3r, Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IxzNSAdUY70
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) present two main directions: feed-forward models offer fast inference in sparse-view settings, while per-scene optimization yields high-quality renderings but is computationally expensive. To combine the benefits of both, we introduce Diff3R, a novel framework that explicitly bridges feed-forward prediction and test-time optimization. By incorporating a differentiable 3DGS optimization layer directly into the training loop, our network learns to predict an optimal initialization for test-time optimization rather than a conventional zero-shot result. To overcome the computational cost of backpropagating through the optimization steps, we propose computing gradients via the Implicit Function Theorem and a scalable, matrix-free PCG solver tailored for 3DGS optimization. Additionally, we incorporate a data-driven uncertainty model into the optimization process by adaptively controlling how much the parameters are allowed to change during optimization. This approach effectively mitigates overfitting in under-constrained regions and increases robustness against input outliers. Since our proposed optimization layer is model-agnostic, we show that it can be seamlessly integrated into existing feed-forward 3DGS architectures for both pose-given and pose-free methods, providing improvements for test-time optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Diff3R：基于不确定性感知可微优化的前馈3D高斯点渲染
近期3D高斯点渲染（3DGS）研究呈现两大方向：前馈模型在稀疏视角下提供快速推理，而每场景优化则生成高质量渲染但计算成本高。为兼顾二者优势，我们提出Diff3R框架，显式连接前馈预测与测试时优化。通过将可微3DGS优化层直接嵌入训练循环，网络学习预测测试时优化的最优初始化而非传统零样本结果。为降低优化步骤反向传播的计算开销，我们提出基于隐函数定理计算梯度，并采用针对3DGS优化的可扩展、无矩阵PCG求解器。此外，我们通过自适应控制优化过程中参数允许变化的程度，将数据驱动的不确定性模型融入优化过程，有效缓解欠约束区域过拟合问题并增强对输入异常值的鲁棒性。由于所提优化层模型无关，可无缝集成至现有前馈3DGS架构（含姿态给定与姿态自由方法），提升测试时优化性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between speed and quality in 3D Gaussian Splatting, Diff3R bridges feed-forward prediction and per-scene optimization by embedding a differentiable 3DGS optimization layer into the training loop, enabling the network to learn optimal initializations for test-time refinement. It employs gradient computation via the Implicit Function Theorem and a matrix-free PCG solver to handle optimization backpropagation efficiently, while integrating a data-driven uncertainty model to constrain parameter updates and reduce overfitting. Experimental results demonstrate that Diff3R significantly improves rendering quality and robustness across diverse scenes without increasing inference latency, and it is compatible with both pose-given and pose-free 3DGS architectures.
为解决3D高斯点渲染中速度与质量的权衡问题，Diff3R在训练阶段嵌入可微分的3D高斯点优化层，使网络学习生成测试时优化的初始参数而非零样本预测。通过隐函数定理与矩阵自由的PCG求解器高效反向传播优化梯度，并引入数据驱动的不确定性模型动态约束参数更新，有效缓解欠约束区域过拟合并增强对输入异常的鲁棒性。实验表明，该方法显著提升渲染质量与稳定性，且兼容现有前馈架构，适用于带位姿与无位姿场景。
Revision or Re-Solving? Decomposing Second-Pass Gains in Multi-LLM Pipelines
Authors: Jingjie Ning, Xueqi Li, Chengyu Yu
First: 2026-04-01T15:39:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:39:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-LLM revision pipelines, in which a second model reviews and improves a draft produced by a first, are widely assumed to derive their gains from genuine error correction. We question this assumption with a controlled decomposition experiment that uses four matched conditions to separate second-pass gains into three additive components: re-solving, scaffold, and content. We evaluate this design across two model pairs on three benchmarks spanning knowledge-intensive MCQ and competitive programming. Our results show that the gains of multi-LLM revision are not monolithic, but depend on task structure, draft quality, and the type of draft information. On MCQ tasks, where the answer space is constrained and drafts provide little structural guidance, most gains are consistent with stronger-model re-solving, and directly routing queries to the stronger model can be more effective than revising a weak draft. On code generation tasks, however, two-stage prompting remains useful because even semantically null drafts can provide substantial structural scaffolding, while weak draft content can be harmful. Finally, role-reversed experiments show that strong drafts clearly benefit weak reviewers. Ultimately, our findings demonstrate that the utility of multi-LLM revision is dynamically bottlenecked by task structure and draft quality, necessitating more targeted pipeline designs rather than blanket revision strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：修订还是重解？多LLM流水线中第二轮增益的分解
多LLM修订流水线通常被认为通过真正的错误纠正获得增益。我们通过一个受控分解实验质疑这一假设，该实验使用四个匹配条件将第二轮增益分解为三个可加成分：重解、支架和内容。我们在三个涵盖知识密集型选择题和编程竞赛的基准上，对两组模型对进行了评估。结果表明，多LLM修订的增益并非单一整体，而是取决于任务结构、草稿质量及草稿信息类型。在选择题任务中，答案空间受限且草稿缺乏结构指导时，大部分增益符合更强模型的重解，直接将查询路由至更强模型可能比修订弱草稿更有效。但在代码生成任务中，两阶段提示仍具价值，因为即使语义为空的草稿也能提供显著结构支架，而弱草稿内容可能有害。角色反转实验表明，强草稿明显有益于弱评审者。最终，我们的研究显示，多LLM修订的效用动态受限于任务结构和草稿质量，需要更针对性的流水线设计，而非泛化的修订策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the assumption that multi-LLM revision pipelines gain from genuine error correction, this study decomposes second-pass gains into three additive components—re-solving, scaffold, and content—using a controlled experiment with four matched conditions across two model pairs and three benchmarks. Results show that gains are not uniform: on knowledge-intensive MCQ tasks, most improvements stem from the stronger model re-solving, and direct routing often outperforms revision; on code generation tasks, even semantically empty drafts provide structural scaffolding that enhances performance, while weak draft content can hinder results. Role-reversed experiments confirm that strong drafts benefit weak reviewers, indicating that pipeline effectiveness depends dynamically on task structure and draft quality, calling for task-specific design rather than universal revision strategies.
该研究质疑多LLM修订管道主要依赖错误纠正的假设，通过四组匹配条件的控制实验，将第二阶段增益分解为重解、支架和内容三个组成部分。在两个模型对和三个基准（知识密集型选择题与编程竞赛）上评估后发现，增益并非单一，而是受任务结构、草稿质量与信息类型影响。在选择题任务中，多数增益源于更强模型的重解，直接路由常优于修订；在代码生成任务中，即使语义空洞的草稿仍能提供结构支架，而弱内容反而有害。角色反转实验表明，强草稿能显著提升弱评审者表现，说明管道效用动态受限于任务与草稿特性，需针对性设计而非通用修订策略。
Object Affordance Recognition and Grounding via Multi-scale Cross-modal Representation Learning
Authors: Xinhang Wan, Dongqiang Gou, Xinwang Liu, En Zhu, Xuming He
First: 2025-08-02T04:14:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:38:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A core problem of Embodied AI is to learn object manipulation from observation, as humans do. To achieve this, it is important to localize 3D object affordance areas through observation such as images (3D affordance grounding) and understand their functionalities (affordance classification). Previous attempts usually tackle these two tasks separately, leading to inconsistent predictions due to lacking proper modeling of their dependency. In addition, these methods typically only ground the incomplete affordance areas depicted in images, failing to predict the full potential affordance areas, and operate at a fixed scale, resulting in difficulty in coping with affordances significantly varying in scale with respect to the whole object. To address these issues, we propose a novel approach that learns an affordance-aware 3D representation and employs a stage-wise inference strategy leveraging the dependency between grounding and classification tasks. Specifically, we first develop a cross-modal 3D representation through efficient fusion and multi-scale geometric feature propagation, enabling inference of full potential affordance areas at a suitable regional scale. Moreover, we adopt a simple two-stage prediction mechanism, effectively coupling grounding and classification for better affordance understanding. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, showing improved performance in both affordance grounding and classification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多尺度跨模态表示学习实现物体功能区域识别与定位
具身人工智能的核心问题是从观察中学习物体操作，如同人类一样。为此，重要的是通过图像等观察手段定位三维物体的功能区域（三维功能区域定位），并理解其功能（功能分类）。以往方法通常将这两个任务分开处理，由于缺乏对二者依赖关系的恰当建模，导致预测结果不一致。此外，这些方法通常仅定位图像中显示的不完整功能区域，未能预测物体的完整潜在功能区域，且固定尺度操作，难以应对功能区域相对于整个物体尺度显著变化的情况。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种新颖方法，学习功能感知的三维表示，并采用分阶段推理策略，利用定位与分类任务之间的依赖关系。具体而言，我们首先通过高效融合与多尺度几何特征传播构建跨模态三维表示，从而在合适区域尺度下推断完整潜在功能区域。此外，我们采用简单两阶段预测机制，有效耦合定位与分类任务，提升功能理解能力。实验表明，该方法在功能区域定位与分类任务中均取得性能提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable embodied AI agents to learn object manipulation through observation, this work addresses the limitations of prior methods that treat 3D affordance grounding and classification as separate tasks, leading to inconsistent results and incomplete spatial predictions. The authors propose a multi-scale cross-modal representation learning approach that fuses visual and geometric features to infer full potential affordance areas at appropriate scales, overcoming fixed-scale constraints. Their stage-wise inference strategy explicitly couples grounding and classification, enhancing understanding of object functionality. Experimental results show significant improvements in both grounding accuracy and classification performance compared to existing methods.
为使具身智能体能通过观察学习物体操作，本文针对现有方法将3D功能区域定位与分类任务割裂处理、导致结果不一致且无法预测完整潜在功能区域的问题，提出一种多尺度跨模态表示学习框架。该方法通过融合视觉与几何特征，实现对物体完整功能区域的尺度自适应推理，并采用两阶段推理机制耦合定位与分类任务，提升理解能力。实验表明，该方法在功能区域定位与分类两项任务上均显著优于现有方法。
No-Regret Generative Modeling via Parabolic Monge-Ampère PDE
Authors: Nabarun Deb, Tengyuan Liang
First: 2025-04-12T16:51:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:34:05+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 7 figures. Journal version accepted for publication in the Annals of Statistics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce a novel generative modeling framework based on a discretized parabolic Monge-Ampère PDE, which emerges as a continuous limit of the Sinkhorn algorithm commonly used in optimal transport. Our method performs iterative refinement in the space of Brenier maps using a mirror gradient descent step. We establish theoretical guarantees for generative modeling through the lens of no-regret analysis, demonstrating that the iterates converge to the optimal Brenier map under a variety of step-size schedules. As a technical contribution, we derive a new Evolution Variational Inequality tailored to the parabolic Monge-Ampère PDE, connecting geometry, transportation cost, and regret. Our framework accommodates non-log-concave target distributions, constructs an optimal sampling process via the Brenier map, and integrates favorable learning techniques from generative adversarial networks and score-based diffusion models. As direct applications, we illustrate how our theory paves new pathways for generative modeling and variational inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过抛物型Monge-Ampère偏微分方程实现无悔生成建模
我们提出了一种基于离散化抛物型Monge-Ampère偏微分方程的新型生成建模框架，该方程是Sinkhorn算法（常用于最优传输）的连续极限。我们的方法在Brenier映射空间中通过镜像梯度下降步骤进行迭代优化。我们通过无悔分析建立了生成建模的理论保证，证明在多种步长调度下，迭代序列收敛于最优Brenier映射。作为技术贡献，我们推导了适用于抛物型Monge-Ampère偏微分方程的新演化变分不等式，将几何、传输成本与无悔性联系起来。我们的框架支持非对数凹目标分布，通过Brenier映射构建最优采样过程，并融合生成对抗网络和基于分数的扩散模型中的有利学习技术。作为直接应用，我们展示了该理论为生成建模和变分推断开辟了新路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for theoretically grounded generative models that can handle non-log-concave distributions, this work introduces a framework based on a discretized parabolic Monge-Ampère PDE, which arises as a continuous limit of the Sinkhorn algorithm. The method employs iterative mirror gradient descent in the space of Brenier maps to refine generative outputs, with theoretical guarantees derived via no-regret analysis showing convergence to the optimal Brenier map under various step-size schedules. A key technical contribution is a new Evolution Variational Inequality that links geometry, transportation cost, and regret, enabling the framework to support optimal sampling and integrate techniques from GANs and diffusion models, with experimental results demonstrating its effectiveness in generating samples from complex distributions.
为应对非对数凹目标分布的生成建模需求，本文提出一种基于离散化抛物型Monge-Ampère偏微分方程的新框架，该方程是Sinkhorn算法的连续极限。方法通过在Brenier映射空间中迭代镜像梯度下降进行生成优化，结合无悔分析理论，证明在多种步长策略下迭代可收敛至最优Brenier映射。作者推导出新的演化变分不等式，将几何结构、运输代价与后悔值关联，使框架支持最优采样并融合GAN与扩散模型技术，实验表明其在复杂分布生成中表现有效。
Fast and Accurate Probing of In-Training LLMs' Downstream Performances
Authors: Zhichen Liu, Tianle Lun, Zhibin Wen, Hao An, Yulin Ou, Jianhui Xu, Hao Zhang, Wenyi Fang, Yang Zheng, Yang Xu
First: 2026-04-01T15:32:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:32:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The paradigm of scaling Large Language Models (LLMs) in both parameter size and test time has pushed the boundaries of AI capabilities, but at the cost of making the traditional generative evaluation paradigm prohibitively expensive, therefore making the latency of LLM's in-training downstream performance evaluation unbearable. However, simple metrics like training loss (perplexity) are not always correlated with downstream performance, as sometimes their trends diverge from the actual task outcomes. This dilemma calls for a method that is computationally efficient and sufficiently accurate in measuring model capabilities. To address this challenge, we introduce a new in-training evaluation paradigm that uses a lightweight probe for monitoring downstream performance. The probes take the internal representations of LLM checkpoints (during training) as input and directly predict the checkpoint's performance on downstream tasks measured by success probability (i.e., pass@1). We design several probe architectures, validating their effectiveness using the OLMo3-7B's checkpoints across a diverse set of downstream tasks. The probes can accurately predict a checkpoint's performance (with avg. AUROC$>$0.75), have decent generalizability across checkpoints (earlier predicts later), and reduce the computation latency from $\sim$1 hr (using conventional generative evaluation method) to $\sim$3 min. In sum, this work presents a practical and scalable in-training downstream evaluation paradigm, enabling a more agile, informed, and efficient LLM development process.
中文标题/摘要
标题：快速准确探测训练中大语言模型的下游性能
在参数规模和测试时间上扩展大语言模型（LLMs）的范式推动了AI能力的边界，但代价是使传统的生成式评估范式变得极其昂贵，导致LLM训练中下游性能评估的延迟难以忍受。然而，简单的指标（如训练损失，困惑度）并不总与下游性能相关，有时其趋势与实际任务结果相背离。这一困境呼唤一种计算高效且足够准确测量模型能力的方法。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了一种新的训练中评估范式，使用轻量级探测器监控下游性能。探测器以LLM检查点（训练期间）的内部表示为输入，直接预测该检查点在下游任务上的表现（以成功率，即pass@1衡量）。我们设计了多种探测器架构，并在OLMo3-7B的检查点上针对多样化的下游任务验证其有效性。探测器能准确预测检查点性能（平均AUROC>0.75），在不同检查点间具有良好的泛化能力（早期预测后期），并将计算延迟从约1小时（使用传统生成式评估方法）缩短至约3分钟。总之，本工作提出了一种实用且可扩展的训练中下游评估范式，使LLM开发过程更加敏捷、有据可依且高效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) during training, this work introduces lightweight probes that predict downstream task performance using internal model representations, bypassing expensive generative evaluations. The probes are trained to estimate success probability (pass@1) from LLM checkpoints and achieve high accuracy (avg. AUROC > 0.75) across diverse tasks. Experiments on OLMo3-7B show the probes generalize well across training stages and reduce evaluation latency from approximately one hour to three minutes, enabling efficient, real-time monitoring of model capabilities during training.
为解决传统生成式评估在模型训练期间成本高、延迟大的问题，本文提出一种轻量级探针方法，通过输入训练检查点的内部表示来预测其在下游任务上的成功率（pass@1）。该方法在OLMo3-7B模型多个检查点和多样任务上验证，探针平均AUROC超过0.75，具备良好的跨阶段泛化能力，评估时间从约1小时缩短至3分钟。该方法实现了高效、准确、可扩展的训练中性能监控，助力更敏捷的LLM开发。
Model-Based Learning of Near-Optimal Finite-Window Policies in POMDPs
Authors: Philip Jordan, Maryam Kamgarpour
First: 2026-04-01T15:32:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:32:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study model-based learning of finite-window policies in tabular partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs). A common approach to learning under partial observability is to approximate unbounded history dependencies using finite action-observation windows. This induces a finite-state Markov decision process (MDP) over histories, referred to as the superstate MDP. Once a model of this superstate MDP is available, standard MDP algorithms can be used to compute optimal policies, motivating the need for sample-efficient model estimation. Estimating the superstate MDP model is challenging because trajectories are generated by interaction with the original POMDP, creating a mismatch between the sampling process and target model. We propose a model estimation procedure for tabular POMDPs and analyze its sample complexity. Our analysis exploits a connection between filter stability and concentration inequalities for weakly dependent random variables. As a result, we obtain tight sample complexity guarantees for estimating the superstate MDP model from a single trajectory. Combined with value iteration, this yields approximately optimal finite-window policies for the POMDP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于模型的POMDP中近似最优有限窗口策略的学习
我们研究了在表格形式部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）中基于模型的有限窗口策略学习。在部分可观测环境下，一种常见方法是通过有限的动作-观测窗口近似无限历史依赖。这诱导出一个基于历史的有限状态马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），称为超状态MDP。一旦获得该超状态MDP的模型，即可使用标准MDP算法计算最优策略，从而激发对样本高效模型估计的需求。估计超状态MDP模型具有挑战性，因为轨迹由与原始POMDP的交互生成，导致采样过程与目标模型之间存在不匹配。我们为表格POMDP提出了一种模型估计程序，并分析其样本复杂度。我们的分析利用了滤波器稳定性与弱相关随机变量集中不等式之间的联系。因此，我们获得了从单条轨迹估计超状态MDP模型的紧致样本复杂度保证。结合价值迭代，这可得到POMDP的近似最优有限窗口策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning near-optimal policies in partially observable environments, this work focuses on model-based finite-window policy learning in tabular POMDPs. It proposes a method that constructs a finite-state superstate MDP by approximating history dependencies with action-observation windows, then estimates the model of this superstate MDP from interaction data. The key innovation lies in analyzing the sample complexity of this estimation using filter stability and concentration inequalities for weakly dependent variables, enabling tight guarantees even from a single trajectory. When combined with value iteration, the approach yields approximately optimal finite-window policies with strong theoretical sample efficiency.
为了解决部分可观测环境下近最优策略学习的挑战，本文研究了表格化POMDP中基于模型的有限窗口策略学习。方法通过用动作-观测窗口近似历史依赖，构建有限状态的超状态MDP，从而可应用标准MDP算法。核心贡献是一种针对超状态MDP的样本高效模型估计方法，其分析利用滤波器稳定性与弱相关随机变量集中不等式之间的联系，从单条轨迹获得紧致的样本复杂度界。结合价值迭代后，该方法可生成近最优的有限窗口策略。
Infinite-Horizon Ergodic Control via Kernel Mean Embeddings
Authors: Christian Hughes, Ian Abraham
First: 2026-04-01T15:30:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-01T15:30:56+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper derives an infinite-horizon ergodic controller based on kernel mean embeddings for long-duration coverage tasks on general domains. While existing kernel-based ergodic control methods provide strong coverage guarantees on general coverage domains, their practical use has been limited to sub-ergodic, finite-time horizons due to intractable computational scaling, prohibiting its use for long-duration coverage. We resolve this scaling by deriving an infinite-horizon ergodic controller equipped with an extended kernel mean embedding error visitation state that recursively records state visitation. This extended state decouples past visitation from future control synthesis and expands ergodic control to infinite-time settings. In addition, we present a variation of the controller that operates on a receding-horizon control formulation with the extended error state. We demonstrate theoretical proof of asymptotic convergence of the derived controller and show preservation of ergodic coverage guarantees for a class of 2D and 3D coverage problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于核均值嵌入的无限时域遍历控制
本文基于核均值嵌入推导出一种适用于一般域长期覆盖任务的无限时域遍历控制器。尽管现有基于核的遍历控制方法在一般覆盖域上能提供强覆盖保证，但因计算复杂度难以处理，其实际应用受限于次遍历、有限时域，无法用于长期覆盖。本文通过推导一种配备扩展核均值嵌入误差访问状态的无限时域遍历控制器，该状态递归记录状态访问情况，从而将过去访问与未来控制合成解耦，并将遍历控制扩展至无限时域。此外，本文还提出一种基于滚动时域控制框架的控制器变体，使用扩展误差状态。我们证明了所推导控制器的渐近收敛性，并展示了其在一类二维和三维覆盖问题中保持遍历覆盖保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for long-duration coverage in general domains, this paper introduces an infinite-horizon ergodic controller using kernel mean embeddings to overcome computational scaling limits of existing methods. The method extends kernel mean embeddings by recursively tracking state visitation through an extended error state, decoupling past visitation from future control synthesis. Experimental results demonstrate asymptotic convergence of the controller and preservation of ergodic coverage guarantees across 2D and 3D coverage tasks under both infinite-horizon and receding-horizon formulations.
为解决现有核方法在长时域覆盖任务中因计算复杂度过高而受限的问题，本文提出一种基于核均值嵌入的无限时域遍历控制器。该方法通过引入递归记录状态访问历史的扩展误差状态，将历史访问与未来控制解耦，从而支持无限时域运行。实验结果表明，该控制器具有渐近收敛性，并在二维和三维覆盖任务中保持遍历覆盖保证。
